### [1] [CriticGen: Generation-Aware Evaluation as Actionable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05439)
*Huifang Du,Zecheng Zuo,Sen Wang,Chenghao Fan,Haofen Wang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将细粒度评估转为可执行改进反馈，可迁移到agent自我反思与迭代优化。

TL;DR: CriticGen 是一个细粒度、生成感知的评估框架，通过动态评分细则联合生成评分、理由、可执行建议与精炼答案，在提升评估质量的同时实现可靠的答案改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估方法粗粒度且与生成过程解耦，产生的解释过于泛化，无法为模型改进提供可操作反馈。

Method: 提出 CriticGen，在主观、客观、自推导约束等高层类别下生成样本特定的评估维度与评分标准，并将其作为动态评分细则，联合生成分数、理由、可执行精炼建议和精炼答案；该评分细则条件化精炼过程支持模型诊断缺陷并进行针对性改进。

Result: CriticGen 诱导出更高质量评分细则，相关性/覆盖率由 3.33/4.03 提升至 3.97/4.24；取得最佳评分相关性，皮尔逊 0.9556、斯皮尔曼 0.9560；基于标准的理由与可执行建议 F1 由 0.6369/0.5994 提升至 0.7554/0.7900；反馈改进 73.17% 的答案，非退化率 93.28%。

Conclusion: 细粒度评估应同时具备实例特定性与可操作性；CriticGen 将评估转化为可操作控制，并能带来可靠的答案改进。

中文摘要：当前面向大语言模型的评估方法较为粗粒度，且与生成过程解耦，会产生泛化的解释，无法为模型改进提供可操作的反馈。我们提出 CriticGen，一个细粒度、生成感知的评估框架，将评估转化为面向答案改进的可操作控制。CriticGen 首先在主观、客观和自推导约束等高层类别下生成样本特定的评估维度与评分标准。这些标准随后作为动态评分细则，用于联合生成分数、理由、可执行的精炼建议以及精炼后的答案。这种以评分细则为条件的精炼过程使模型能够诊断缺陷并执行有针对性的答案改进。实验结果表明，细粒度评估应兼具实例特定性与可操作性。CriticGen 能诱导出更高质量的评分细则，将相关性/覆盖率从 3.33/4.03 提升至 3.97/4.24。CriticGen 还取得了最佳评分相关性，皮尔逊相关系数为 0.9556，斯皮尔曼相关系数为 0.9560，并将基于标准的理由与可执行建议的 F1 从 0.6369/0.5994 提升至 0.7554/0.7900。至关重要的是，其反馈能转化为可靠的答案改进，改进 73.17% 的答案，且非退化率达到 93.28%。

Original abstract: Current evaluation methods for large language models are coarse-grained and decoupled from generation, producing generic explanations that fail to provide actionable feedback for model improvement. We propose CriticGen, a fine-grained, generation-aware evaluation framework that turns evaluation into actionable control for answer improvement. CriticGen first generates sample-specific evaluation dimensions and scoring criteria under high-level categories such as subjective, objective, and self-derived constraints. These criteria then serve as a dynamic rubric for jointly producing a score, a reason, an executable refinement suggestion, and a refined answer. This rubric-conditioned refinement process enables models to diagnose flaws and perform targeted answer improvement. Experimental results show that fine-grained evaluation should be both instance-specific and actionable. CriticGen induces higher-quality rubrics, improving relevance/coverage from 3.33/4.03 to 3.97/4.24. CriticGen also achieves the best score correlations, with 0.9556 Pearson and 0.9560 Spearman, and raises the F1 of criterion-grounded reasons and executable suggestions from 0.6369/0.5994 to 0.7554/0.7900. Crucially, its feedback translates into reliable answer improvement, improving 73.17% of answers with a 93.28% non-degradation rate.

</details>


### [2] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向工具使用LLM agent的长时记忆成本感知评测基准，直接相关agent记忆与可靠性。

TL;DR: MERIT 基准在显式成本核算下衡量工具使用智能体的记忆边际效用，发现记忆可将依赖任务成功率从 0.00 提升至 0.55-1.00，但更新事实检索不稳定，且全重放不经济。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话回忆基准只评估对话历史问答，不评估记忆是否改变工具使用智能体的行为，也缺乏对记忆操作成本的核算。

Method: 提出 MERIT 基准与测试框架，包含三个领域的情景式工具使用任务、自动泄漏检查、以更新事实回忆结尾的难度阶梯、受控记忆损坏、完整 token 与美元计量；在 23,440 个已评分情节、多模型多种子网格中评估，并进行全重放对照和最新模型抽查。

Result: 记忆将依赖任务成功率从 0.00 提升到 0.55-1.00；更新事实上嵌入检索表现 0.30-0.95 且最大种子差距 0.45，正确检索后仅 55% 行动，写时更新存储保持 0.70-1.00；更换记忆实现最多改变 60 个百分点；全重放从不经济，最佳条件每美元边际效用为全重放的 2.7-3.9 倍。

Conclusion: 记忆实现选择显著影响任务成功；更新事实需要写时更新存储而非不可靠的嵌入检索；应发布基准、测试框架和轨迹以支持可复现评估。

中文摘要：面向 LLM 智能体的长期记忆如今由对话式回忆基准（LoCoMo、LongMemEval）评估，这些基准衡量的是在对话历史上的问答，而不是记住的事实是否改变使用工具的智能体的行为。我们提出 MERIT（Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks），一个基准与测试框架，在显式成本核算下衡量记忆对执行任务智能体的边际效用。MERIT 提供三个领域的情景式工具使用任务，这些任务对先前情景事实的依赖由自动泄漏检查验证；一个以更新事实回忆收尾的难度阶梯；受控记忆损坏；以及对每个记忆操作进行完整的 token 与美元计量。在 23,440 个已评分情节（42.57 美元）中，包括在 gpt-4.1-mini 上的两代试点，以及一个预注册的 3 模型 x 3 种子网格（GPT-4.1、Claude Haiku 4.5；记忆侧保持固定），记忆将依赖任务的成功率从经泄漏验证的 0.00 下限提升到 0.55-1.00。在更新事实上，嵌入检索不可预测地崩溃（跨模型 0.30-0.95；最大种子差距 0.45），智能体仅在 55% 的情况下对正确检索到的值采取行动，而写时更新存储（结构化事实存储，以及值得注意的是 LLM 摘要）保持在 0.70-1.00；混合方法比单独的事实存储更差。最新一代的抽查（Claude Sonnet 5，以干净的全重放对照为门控条件）复现了该模式。更换记忆的实现可使任务成功率改变最多 60 个百分点，而全重放从不经济：每个领域的最佳条件每美元提供的边际效用是全重放的 2.7-3.9 倍。我们发布该基准、测试框架和所有轨迹。

Original abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [3] [AutoFyn Technical Report: Non-Parametric Expert Iteration for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05446)
*Adib Hasan,Daniel Schaffield,Akashnil Dutta,Tarik Adnan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 持久状态+验证器奖励的非参数专家迭代agent harness，直接可迁移到长程代码优化agent。

TL;DR: AutoFyn 通过将验证奖励蒸馏到持久状态来适配冻结模型，在数学、数据科学和网络安全任务上取得领先结果并发现漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不更新模型权重的前提下，利用客观验证信号跨轮次持续提升冻结模型在复杂长程任务中的表现。

Method: 受专家迭代启发，每轮从全新模型会话开始，仅通过持久内存文件、报告、代码仓库状态等显式接口保留信息；编排器用专门化智能体探索、规划并构建候选方案，基于任务的验证器提供客观奖励，奖励再蒸馏进持久状态以更新下一轮有效策略。

Result: 在2026年国际数学奥林匹克竞赛六道新题上，仍有提升空间的模型均优于提供商自有编程智能体；在 Spider 2.0 dbt 基准上构建了排名第一的智能体；在 Next.js、MetaMask、pnpm、Warp、LiteLLM、Langflow 和 Open WebUI 中产出16份维护者确认的漏洞公告。

Conclusion: 该循环将验证反馈转化为持久策略更新，为冻结模型在多领域复杂任务中提供了可扩展的智能体改进路径。

中文摘要：我们提出了 AutoFyn，一种受专家迭代算法启发的智能体运行框架；它通过在多个轮次中依据经验证的奖励信号更新持久状态，而非更新模型权重，来适配一个冻结模型。每一轮都从全新的模型会话开始，持久信息仅通过显式接口重新引入，例如持久内存文件、报告和代码仓库状态。在一轮内，一个编排器利用专门化智能体探索、规划并构建多种候选方案，同时一个基于任务的验证器验证工作并提供用于衡量进展的客观奖励。该奖励被蒸馏回持久状态，从而更新下一轮的有效策略。在本技术报告中，我们形式化这一循环，并描述其持久状态和验证接口。随后，我们展示其在三个领域中的应用，即奥林匹克数学、数据科学和网络安全。在2026年国际数学奥林匹克竞赛的六道新题上，每个仍有提升空间的模型在AutoFyn下的得分都高于其提供商自有的编程智能体。AutoFyn还构建了 Spider 2.0 dbt 基准上排名第一的智能体，并在 Next.js、MetaMask、pnpm、Warp、LiteLLM、Langflow 和 Open WebUI 中产出了16份经维护者确认的漏洞公告。

Original abstract: We introduce AutoFyn, an agent harness inspired by the Expert Iteration algorithm, adapting a frozen model across many rounds by updating persistent state from verified reward signals rather than model weights. Each round begins from a fresh model session, and durable information is reintroduced only through explicit interfaces such as persistent memory files, reports, and repository state. Within a round, an orchestrator explores, plans and builds many alternative approaches with specialized agents, while a task-grounded verifier verifies the work and supplies an objective reward for measuring progress. This reward is distilled back into the persistent state, which updates the effective policy for the next round. In this technical report, we formalize this loop and describe its persistent state and verification interfaces. We then demonstrate its use in three domains, namely olympiad mathematics, data science, and cybersecurity. On the six fresh problems of the 2026 International Mathematical Olympiad, every model with room to improve scores higher under AutoFyn than in its provider's own coding agent. AutoFyn also built the top-ranked agent on the Spider 2.0 dbt benchmark, and has produced $16$ maintainer-confirmed vulnerability advisories in Next.js, MetaMask, pnpm, Warp, LiteLLM, Langflow, and Open WebUI.

</details>


### [4] [SCAFFOLD: Self-Improving Web Agents via Recursive Parametric Skill Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05511)
*Bowei He,Xiaokun Zhang,Meng Ding,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted by EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: 从成功轨迹归纳可复用可组合技能并自改进的web agent框架，对agent经验复用与自我改进有直接启发。

TL;DR: SCAFFOLD 是面向视觉 Web 智能体的自我改进框架，通过归纳可执行技能、构建递归技能层次、MDL 压缩和周期性蒸馏，在多个基准上显著提升成功率并稳定迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Web 智能体通常孤立学习任务并丢弃过程性知识；技能增强方法虽迈出第一步，但技能库仍是扁平或两层提示缓存，缺乏压缩冗余和递归组合的原则性机制。

Method: SCAFFOLD 在多实例抽象约束下从成功轨迹归纳参数化可执行技能；构建高层技能调用低层技能的递归层次；用最小描述长度（MDL）和行为等价性检查压缩技能库；周期性将技能增强轨迹蒸馏回模型权重以内化抽象。

Result: 在 WebArena、VisualWebArena 和 Online-Mind2Web 留出划分上，SCAFFOLD 相比最强技能增强基线成功率绝对提升 11.1–17.2 个百分点；五次自我改进迭代中单调增益且无技能库崩溃；代码与文档已发布。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD 表明可对技能库进行原则性压缩与递归组合，并将技能知识内化到模型权重，从而实现视觉 Web 智能体的持续自我改进。

中文摘要：Web 智能体需要导航视觉丰富、长时程且跨站点发生变化的界面，然而大多数先前智能体仍然孤立地学习每个任务，并丢弃它们积累的过程性知识。近期的技能增强框架迈出了重要的第一步，但它们将技能库视为扁平或两层的提示侧缓存，并且没有提供用于压缩冗余或递归组合技能的原则性机制。我们提出 SCAFFOLD，一种用于视觉 Web 智能体的自我改进框架，它（i）在多实例抽象约束下从成功轨迹中归纳出参数化、可执行的技能，（ii）维护一个递归组合的层次结构，其中高层技能调用低层技能，（iii）通过最小描述长度（MDL）准则和行为等价性检查来压缩技能库，并且（iv）周期性地将技能增强轨迹蒸馏回模型权重，以内化这些抽象。在 WebArena、VisualWebArena 以及 Online-Mind2Web 的一个留出划分上，SCAFFOLD 相对于最强的技能增强基线将成功率绝对提升 11.1–17.2 个百分点，并在五次自我改进迭代中显示出单调增益，且未出现技能库崩溃。我们在 GitHub 仓库 https://github.com/BokwaiHo/SCAFFOLD 中发布了代码和文档。

Original abstract: Web agents need to navigate visually rich, long-horizon interfaces that change across sites, yet most previous agents still learn each task in isolation and discard the procedural knowledge they accumulate. Recent skill-augmented frameworks take an important first step, but they treat the skill library as a flat or two-tier prompt-side cache and offer no principled mechanism for compressing redundancy or composing skills recursively. We introduce \textsc{Scaffold}, a self-improving framework for visual web agents that (i) induces parametric, executable skills from successful trajectories under a multi-instance abstraction constraint, (ii) maintains a recursively composed hierarchy in which higher-level skills invoke lower-level ones, (iii) compacts the library via a minimum-description-length (MDL) criterion and behavioral equivalence checking, and (iv) periodically distills skill-augmented trajectories back into model weights to internalize the abstractions. Across WebArena, VisualWebArena, and a held-out split of Online-Mind2Web, \textsc{Scaffold} improves success rate by $11.1$--$17.2$ absolute points over the strongest skill-augmented baseline and shows monotonic gains across five self-improvement iterations without library collapse. We release the code and documents in the Github \href{https://github.com/BokwaiHo/SCAFFOLD}{repository}.

</details>


### [5] [When and What to Teach: Budget-Aware Online Adaptation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05513)
*Jianwei Zhang,Sihan Cao,Pengcheng Zheng,Ya Wen,Pei Ke,Kuien Liu,Shen Gao,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages

Selection reason: 面向web agent的预算感知在线教学与轨迹裁剪，可迁移到agent训练反馈与成本控制。

TL;DR: 提出预算感知在线教学框架，通过可解性教师门控和分数引导轮次裁剪，在保持性能的同时减少教师调用与学生训练计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境部署需持续在线适应，专有模型成本过高，本地模型需依赖更强教师在线教学，但标准交互反馈成本过高；传统轨迹级偏好优化还会在不可解片段和冗余轮次上浪费预算。

Method: 提出 Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming，包含可解性感知教师门控以决定何时查询教师，以及分数引导轮次选择以决定保留哪些信息量大的轮次。

Result: 在 MiniWoB 和 TimeWarp 上，首次通过成功率与基线可比，平均教师调用减少 22.6%，学生训练计算量减少 52.1%。

Conclusion: 该预算感知在线教学框架能系统编排教学时机与内容，降低在线适应成本，并已公开代码。

中文摘要：Web智能体在自动化复杂互联网任务方面已取得显著成功，但在真实环境中部署它们需要持续的在线适应。鉴于部署强大的专有模型在商业上仍然成本过高而难以承受，实践者必须依赖轻量级本地模型，这些模型在部署后通过来自更强教师的在线教学进行演化。然而，标准交互式反馈会带来过高的成本。我们表明，传统的轨迹级偏好优化会在无法解决的片段和冗余执行轮次上浪费预算。为解决这些低效问题，我们提出 Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming，这是一个预算感知框架，系统性地编排何时教以及教什么。具体而言，我们的框架集成了一个可解性感知的教师门控，以决定何时查询教师模型，以及一个分数引导的轮次选择机制，以决定保留哪些信息量大的轮次。在 MiniWoB 和 TimeWarp 上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在首次通过成功率上达到可比水平，同时平均将教师调用减少 22.6%，将学生训练计算量减少 52.1%。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/zjw131f1fc/budgeted-online-teaching 获取。

Original abstract: Web agents have achieved significant success in automating complex internet tasks but deploying them in real-world environments requires continuous online adaptation. Given that deploying powerful proprietary models remains commercially cost-prohibitive, practitioners must rely on lightweight local models that evolve post-deployment via online teaching from a stronger teacher. However, standard interactive feedback imposes prohibitive costs. We show that conventional trajectory-level preference optimization wastes budget on both unresolvable episodes and redundant execution turns. To resolve these inefficiencies, we propose \textbf{Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming}, a budget-aware framework that systematically orchestrates \textbf{when} and \textbf{what} to teach. Specifically, our framework integrates a solvability-aware teacher gate to dictate \textbf{when} to query the teacher model and a score-guided turn selection mechanism to decide \textbf{what} informative turns to retain. Extensive experiments on MiniWoB and TimeWarp demonstrate that our method achieves comparable first-pass success while reducing teacher calls by 22.6\% and student training compute by 52.1\% on average. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjw131f1fc/budgeted-online-teaching.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond "AI Helps Humans": Decision-Targeted Evaluation Design for Human-Agent Teams in the Agentic Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05527)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 针对human-agent协作的决策导向评测设计，涉及编码agent监督，可迁移到agent可靠性与基线比较。

TL;DR: TEAM-Design为每个任务分配两种重放概率，以在固定预算下决定优先测量纯人工还是纯智能体基线，并证明其最优性与错误声明控制；实验表明其在两个比较难度不对称时最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署人-AI工作流需要验证其同时优于纯人工和纯智能体，但缺失基线只能通过昂贵重放获得，因此必须在预算约束下决定重放资源如何分配给两种基线。现有基准、方差采样和贝叶斯信息方法均未直接针对这一部署决策。

Method: 提出TEAM-Design规则，为每个任务分别给出纯人工重放和纯智能体重放的概率；当缺失基线结果难预测或该比较更难确立时提高概率，当重放成本高时降低概率。作者证明该规则解决预算化设计问题，并证明按记录概率随机重放可控制错误宣称工作流同时优于两种基线的概率。评估包括6个临床场景、一个编码基准、合成设计以及基于真实胸部X光读片研究的半合成设计。

Result: 在6个临床场景中，没有人-AI工作流同时优于两种替代方案；在一个编码基准中，有一个工作流做到这一点。TEAM-Design在两种比较中一个明显比另一个更难判定时表现最好，而当两者难度相似时可能劣于基于方差的分配。

Conclusion: TEAM-Design直接面向人-AI工作流是否值得保留的部署决策来分配重放预算，并提供理论保证；但其优势依赖于两种基线比较之间的难度差异。

中文摘要：只要编码智能体在工程师监督下工作，或临床模型辅助放射科医生，部署问题就是：保留人-AI工作流，还是用纯人工或纯智能体替代它。人-AI工作流只有在同时优于这两种替代方案时才值得保留。然而一旦部署，这两种替代结果都无法被观测到：恢复其中一种意味着在该替代方案下重放任务，而每次重放都会消耗专家时间或算力。在固定重放预算下，设计问题因此是：哪些任务应更可能接受纯人工重放，哪些应更可能接受纯智能体重放。现有方法并未直接针对这一决策。智能体基准不选择测量哪个缺失基线，基于方差的采样忽略两个比较中哪一个更接近失败，贝叶斯信息方法关注学习模型参数而非做出部署决策。我们提出TEAM-Design，该规则为每个任务给出两个重放概率，每个基线一个。当缺失基线结果难以根据已知任务信息预测时，以及当该比较更难确立时，它提高相应概率；当重放昂贵时，它降低概率。我们证明该规则解决了这一预算化设计问题，并且从记录的概率中随机抽取重放仍可控制错误宣称工作流同时优于两者的概率。我们重新分析6个临床场景，其中没有人-AI工作流同时优于两种替代方案，以及一个编码基准，其中有一个做到这一点，然后在合成设计以及由真实胸部X光读片研究构建的半合成设计上评估TEAM-Design。TEAM-Design在两种比较中一个明显比另一个更难判定时效果最好，而当两者难度相似时可能不如基于方差的分配。

Original abstract: Wherever a coding agent works under engineer supervision, or a clinical model assists a radiologist, the deployment question is whether to keep the human-AI workflow or replace it with the human alone or the agent alone. The human-AI workflow is worth keeping only if it beats both of those alternatives. Yet once it is deployed, neither alternative outcome is observed: recovering one means replaying the task under that alternative, and every replay costs expert time or compute. Under a fixed replay budget, the design question is therefore which tasks should be more likely to receive a human-only replay, and which an agent-only replay. Existing methods do not directly target this decision. Agent benchmarks do not choose which missing baseline to measure, variance-based sampling ignores which of the two comparisons is closer to failing, and Bayesian information methods focus on learning model parameters instead of making the deployment decision. We propose TEAM-Design, a rule that gives every task two replay probabilities, one per baseline. It raises a probability where the missing baseline outcome is hard to predict from what is already known about the task and where that comparison is harder to establish, and lowers it where replay is expensive. We prove that the rule solves this budgeted design problem, and that drawing the replays at random from recorded probabilities still controls the chance of wrongly declaring that the workflow beats both. We reanalyze 6 clinical settings, where no human-AI workflow beats both alternatives, and a coding benchmark, where one does, then evaluate TEAM-Design on synthetic designs and on a semi-synthetic design built from a real chest X-ray reader study. TEAM-Design works best when one of the two comparisons is clearly harder to settle than the other, and can do worse than variance-based allocation when the two are similarly difficult.

</details>


### [7] [EdgeMem: LLM-Free Agent Memory Construction and Retrieval via Evidence-Preserving Multi-Anchor Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05553)
*Zeyang Cui,Jiannong Cao,Zhiyuan Wen,Bo Yuan,Junlan Feng,Shengyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures, and 5 tables. An earlier implementation is available at https://github.com/Soullesskid/edgemem ; it differs substantially from the version described in this paper. The repository will be updated with the corresponding implementation after peer review

Selection reason: 无LLM生成的证据保留多锚超图agent记忆，直接相关agent记忆构建与检索。

TL;DR: EdgeMem 通过保留原始交互轮次并用多锚点超图组织内容、时间和情节线索，实现无需生成式 LLM 的智能体记忆检索与问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法常将交互历史压缩为摘要或 LLM 生成表示，重复生成成本高，并可能在未知未来查询需求前丢失承载答案的细节。

Method: EdgeMem 保留原始交互轮次，利用内容、时间和情节线索，通过轻量级本地处理构建多锚点超图；检索直接返回源证据，LLM 仅用于最终答案生成，兼顾多会话历史结构化访问与原始对话忠实保留。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 上，EdgeMem 展现出强大的检索和基于记忆的问答能力；在 LoCoMo 共享提示下，七个复现系统中严格评判得分最高，为 61.01 对 58.70，且构建与检索均无需生成式 LLM 调用。

Conclusion: 保留并组织源证据可为智能体记忆提供有效且高效的基础，无需生成式记忆管理。

中文摘要：智能体记忆使 LLM 智能体能够在回答新查询时利用先前的交互。现有方法通常将交互历史压缩为摘要或其他由 LLM 生成的表示。重复生成会增加成本，并可能在系统知道未来查询需要什么之前丢弃承载答案的细节。我们提出 EdgeMem，一种智能体记忆方法，其构建围绕一个简单原则：保留原始交互轮次，并通过互补的内容、时间和情节线索对其进行组织。EdgeMem 通过轻量级本地处理构建的多锚点超图来实现这一原则。检索直接返回源证据，并将 LLM 的使用保留用于最终答案生成，从而将多会话历史的结构化访问与对原始对话的忠实保留相结合。在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 上的实验显示出强大的检索和基于记忆的问答；在 LoCoMo 上，EdgeMem 在共享提示下于七个复现系统中取得了最高的严格评判分数（61.01 对 58.70），而构建和检索不需要生成式 LLM 调用。总体而言，EdgeMem 表明，保留和组织源证据为智能体记忆提供了有效且高效的基础，而无需生成式记忆管理。

Original abstract: Agent memory allows LLM agents to use earlier interactions when answering new queries. Existing methods often compress interaction histories into summaries or other LLM-generated representations. Repeated generation adds cost and can discard answer-bearing details before the system knows what a future query will require. We propose EdgeMem, an agent-memory method built around a simple principle: preserve original interaction turns and organize them through complementary content, temporal, and episodic cues. EdgeMem realizes this principle with a multi-anchor hypergraph constructed by lightweight local processing. Retrieval directly returns source evidence and reserves LLM use for final answer generation, combining structured access to multi-session histories with faithful retention of the original conversation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show strong retrieval and memory-grounded question answering; on LoCoMo, EdgeMem achieves the highest strict-judge score among seven reproduced systems under a shared prompt (61.01 versus 58.70), while construction and retrieval require no generative-LLM calls. Overall, EdgeMem shows that preserving and organizing source evidence provides an effective and efficient foundation for agent memory without generative memory management.

</details>


### [8] [EnvCraft: Synthesizing Executable Environments in Agentic RL for Claw-like Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05576)
*Yirong Zeng,Shen You,Jinhang Feng,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Yutai Hou,Hao Cong,Yuxian Wang,Wu Ning,Wang Xu,Bibo Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 29 pages, 12 figures

Selection reason: 合成可执行环境与训练数据用于agentic RL和类Claw长程agent，直接相关agent训练与harness。

TL;DR: EnvCraft自动化合成可执行环境与可扩展训练数据，缓解Agentic RL交互环境稀缺问题，并在Qwen3/3.5上提升Claw风格与通用工具使用表现，同时降低推理token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从被动语言接口转向可在有状态工作空间中执行长时程任务的自主Claw-like智能体；Agentic RL虽可用于优化这些智能体，但其扩展受交互式训练环境严重稀缺的制约，且现有合成环境仅限工具调用端点，难以满足端到端真实需求。

Method: 提出EnvCraft自动化框架：通过环境合成引擎构建沙箱隔离工作空间，并通过拓扑感知数据生成引擎生成连贯任务轨迹；共合成139个交互式环境和约20K复杂任务用于Agentic RL训练。

Result: 在Qwen3/3.5模型（8B-32B）上，方法在Claw风格基准上最高提升+11.9%，在通用工具使用基准上提升+8.0%，并同时降低推理token成本。

Conclusion: 合成的可执行环境能够为训练提供稳健且可泛化的学习信号。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）的范式已迅速从被动的语言接口转向自主的Claw-like智能体，这些智能体跨有状态工作空间执行长时程任务。尽管智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）为优化这些智能体提供了一条有前景的路径，但其规模化严重受限于交互式训练环境的极度稀缺。现有的合成环境严格局限于工具调用端点，使其不足以满足Claw-like智能体的端到端真实世界需求。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了EnvCraft，一种用于合成可执行环境和可扩展训练数据的自动化框架。具体而言，EnvCraft采用环境合成引擎来构建沙箱隔离的工作空间，并配合一个拓扑感知的数据生成引擎来生成连贯的任务轨迹。总体而言，我们合成了139个交互式环境，包含约2万（20K）个复杂任务，用于智能体强化学习训练。在Qwen3/3.5模型（8B-32B）上的实验表明，我们的方法在Claw风格基准上带来最高+11.9%的提升，在通用工具使用基准上带来+8.0%的提升，同时降低了推理token成本。结果证实，合成的可执行环境为训练提供了稳健且可泛化的学习信号。

Original abstract: The paradigm of LLMs has rapidly shifted from passive language interfaces to autonomous Claw-like agents that execute long-horizon tasks across stateful workspaces. While Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) provides a promising path to optimize these agents, its scaling is heavily bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of interactive training environments. Existing synthetic environments are strictly limited to tool-calling endpoints, rendering them insufficient for accommodating the end-to-end real-world demands of claw-like agents. To bridge this gap, we introduce EnvCraft, an automated framework for synthesizing executable environments and scalable training data. Specifically, EnvCraft employs an environment synthesis engine to build sandbox-isolated workspaces, alongside a topology-aware data generation engine to produce coherent task trajectories. Overall, we synthesize 139 interactive environments comprising approximately 20K complex tasks for Agentic RL training. Experiments on Qwen3/3.5 models (8B-32B) show that our method yields gains of up to +11.9% on Claw-style benchmarks and +8.0% on general tool-use benchmarks, with concurrent reductions in inference token cost. The results confirm that synthesized executable environments provide robust and generalizable learning signals for training.

</details>


### [9] [Agents Trust Tools Too Much: Measuring Reliance on Unreliable Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05587)
*Hoyeol Yang,Woojung Song,Taewon Kim,Jonghyun Song,Seoyeon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 39 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 系统评测工具使用agent对不可靠工具返回的过度信任与干预，直接相关agent工具反馈可靠性。

TL;DR: 对14个LLM在三种工具返回被破坏时的评估显示普遍存在过度信任，且无干预能跨工具稳定缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用智能体评估假设工具返回可靠，忽略现实工具返回可能合理但错误，因而需要研究智能体对不可靠工具返回的反应。

Method: 评估14个LLM，使用网络搜索、LLM子智能体委派、代码执行三种工具；对工具返回进行破坏，测量最终答案是否采用被破坏内容；分析推理轨迹；并从用户提示、工具提供方元数据、构建方后训练三层面进行干预。

Result: 三种工具均出现过度信任，平均采用率超过三分之一，网络搜索达68.0%；智能体常识别冲突甚至内部恢复正确答案，却只输出被破坏答案且不警告；部分干预对特定模型或工具有效，但无一致跨工具缓解效果。

Conclusion: 不可靠工具中的过度信任是严重且持续存在的失败模式，需推动评估和干预，使智能体能验证工具输出并透明沟通未解决冲突。

中文摘要：现有对工具使用智能体的评估主要衡量智能体能否借助工具成功完成多样化任务。这些评估通常假设工具返回可靠信息。然而，现实系统中的工具返回可能看似合理但实际错误。我们通过评估十四种LLM在三种工具——网络搜索、LLM子智能体委派和代码执行——中的表现，研究智能体如何应对不可靠的工具返回。对于每种工具，我们破坏其返回，并衡量智能体是否在其最终答案中采用被破坏的内容。智能体在三种设置中均表现出高度过度信任：每种工具的平均采用率均超过三分之一，网络搜索达到68.0%。对推理轨迹的分析揭示了一个尤其令人担忧的失败模式：智能体常常识别出冲突，甚至在内部恢复出正确答案，却只呈现被破坏的答案而不警告用户。为缓解智能体对工具返回的过度信任，我们在三个层面进行干预：用户提示、工具提供方元数据以及智能体构建方的后训练。尽管某些干预对特定模型或工具有帮助，但没有任何干预能够在不同工具间一致地缓解过度信任。这些发现将不可靠工具中的过度信任识别为一种严重且持续存在的失败模式，推动开展能够使智能体验证工具输出并透明地沟通未解决冲突的评估与干预。

Original abstract: Existing evaluations of tool-using agents primarily measure whether an agent can successfully complete diverse tasks with tools. These evaluations generally assume that tools return reliable information. However, tool returns in real-world systems can be plausible yet incorrect. We investigate how agents respond to unreliable tool returns by evaluating fourteen LLMs using three tools-web search, LLM sub-agent delegation, and code execution. For each tool, we corrupt its returns and measure whether agents adopt the corrupted content in their final answers. Agents exhibit high levels of overtrust across all three settings: the mean adoption rate exceeds one third for every tool and reaches 68.0% for web search. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals a particularly concerning failure mode: agents often recognize conflicts and even recover the correct answer internally, yet present only the corrupted answer without warning the user. To mitigate agents' overtrust in tool returns, we intervene at three levels: prompting by the user, metadata from the tool provider, and post-training by the agent builder. Although some interventions help for particular models or tools, none consistently mitigates overtrust across tools. These findings identify overtrust in unreliable tools as a serious and persistent failure mode, motivating evaluations and interventions that enable agents to validate tool outputs and transparently communicate unresolved conflicts.

</details>


### [10] [What LLM Trading Agents Actually Do in Production: A Six-Month, Population-Scale Record from Two Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05663)
*T. J. Barton,Chris Constantakis,Patti Hauseman,Annie Mous,Alaska Hoffman,Brian Bergeron,Hunter Goodreau*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables. Artifacts, figures, and aggregate data: https://github.com/ProjectDXAI/continuous-record-llm-trading-agents

Selection reason: 生产级 LLM 交易 agent 的大规模实证，揭示运行层/harness 比策略文本更决定行为，并给出 agent 评测方法论，可迁移到 agent 可靠性研究。

TL;DR: 本文基于DX Terminal Pro与DXAP两个生产系统约六个月、750万次单模型调用及大量链上/成交记录，发现运行层比策略文本更决定行为、仓位对波动率盲视、代理未能捕获上行收益且两个舰队均无方向性优势；结论经多种稳健性检验，并总结出17条方法论准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是测量自主语言模型交易代理在真实生产中的行为与绩效，检验策略文本、操作层设计、波动率与模型选择对交易决策和结果的影响。

Method: 方法：连续、群体规模的生产测量记录，覆盖两个同源系统（DX Terminal Pro 3,505个用户资金库，21天；DXAP 500至599个用户创建代理，91至117并发，Hyperliquid永续），约六个月、750万次单模型调用、约30万链上操作、231,638次多工具轮次和14,596笔成交；使用固定效应、回归不连续、日聚类推断、置换零假设、共同费用重述，并在416个生产场景上对前沿模型进行配对回放。

Result: 结果：风险滑块每级+0.425杠杆，代理固定效应吸收60%方差，前三名切点处回归不连续1.75倍；各波动率六分位中位杠杆均为5.0倍，某姿态滑块单元占账簿11%却占62%强平；43.2%仓位24h内至少+300bps有利偏移但其中49.3%负回报平仓，机械括号每仓位挽回+39.0bps；DXAP不盈利，往返胜率41% vs 散户基准50%；416场景中前沿模型决策质量统计不可区分，选择稳定性差异显著。

Conclusion: 结论：运行层设计主导行为，仓位规模对波动率不敏感，代理错失已触及的上行空间，且未展现方向性优势；模型选择在此时间跨度上不改变决策质量但影响稳定性；所有主要结论通过稳健性检验，并形成17条方法论准则。

中文摘要：我们提出了一份连续、群体规模的测量记录，记录自主语言模型交易代理在生产环境中跨两个具有同一设计谱系的系统运行：DX Terminal Pro（3,505个用户资金库，在Base迷因币市场交易真实ETH，为期21天，2026年2月至3月）和DXAP实时alpha舰队（500至599个用户创建的代理，全历史；91至117个同时活跃，交易Hyperliquid永续合约，2026年6月至8月）。该记录跨越约六个月、750万次单模型调用、约30万次链上操作，以及另外231,638次多工具轮次，产生14,596笔成交。四项发现支撑本文。第一，运行层比策略文本中写的任何内容更能决定行为：一个风险滑块解释杠杆（每级+0.425），代理固定效应吸收60%的方差，排行榜渲染边界因果性地引导选择（前三名切点处回归不连续为1.75倍）。第二，仓位规模对波动率盲视：在每个波动率六分位中，中位杠杆均为5.0倍，而一个姿态滑块单元（占账簿的11%）承载了62%的强平。第三，代理几乎未捕获其触及的上行收益：43.2%的仓位在24小时内曾出现至少+300个基点的有利偏移，但其中49.3%以负交易回报平仓；一个机械式括号订单每仓位可挽回+39.0个基点。第四，两个舰队均未显示出方向性优势。DXAP舰队不盈利，并落后于匹配的Hyperliquid散户基准（往返胜率41% vs. 50%）。在416个捕获的生产场景上，对前沿模型进行的配对回放联赛发现，在该时间跨度上决策质量在统计上无法区分，而选择稳定性在不同模型家族之间差异显著。每个主要结论都经受住了按日聚类的推断、置换零假设和共同费用重述的检验；论文以一套17条规则的方法论准则收尾，这是以我们自己的撤回为代价换来的。

Original abstract: We present a continuous, population-scale measurement record of autonomous language-model trading agents operating in production across two systems with one design lineage: DX Terminal Pro (3,505 user-funded vaults trading real ETH in Base memecoin markets for 21 days, February to March 2026) and the DXAP live alpha fleet (500 to 599 user-created agents all-history, 91 to 117 concurrently active, trading Hyperliquid perpetuals, June to August 2026). The record spans roughly six months, 7.5M single-model invocations with about 300K onchain actions, and a further 231,638 multi-tool turns producing 14,596 fills. Four findings carry the paper. First, the operating layer determines behavior more than anything written in strategy text: a risk slider explains leverage (+0.425 per level), agent fixed effects absorb 60% of variance, and a leaderboard render boundary causally routes selection (regression discontinuity 1.75x at the top-3 cut). Second, sizing is volatility-blind: median leverage is 5.0x in every volatility sextile, and one posture-slider cell (11% of the book) holds 62% of liquidations. Third, agents capture almost none of the upside they reach: 43.2% of positions saw at least +300 bps of favorable excursion within 24h, yet 49.3% of those closed with a negative trade return; a mechanical bracket recovers +39.0 bps per position. Fourth, neither fleet shows a directional edge. The DXAP fleet is not profitable and trails a matched Hyperliquid retail benchmark (41% vs. 50% roundtrip win rate). A paired-replay league of frontier models on 416 captured production scenarios finds decision quality statistically indistinguishable at this horizon, while choice stability differs sharply across model families. Every headline survives day-clustered inference, permutation nulls, and a common-fee restatement; the paper closes with a 17-rule methodology canon bought with our own retractions.

</details>


### [11] [CUSP: Decomposable Collective Uncertainty for Multi-Agent Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05708)
*Chung-En Johnny Yu,David Garcia,Brian Jalaian,Nathaniel D. Bastian*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Main paper with supplementary material

Selection reason: 多 VLM/多 agent 推理的系统级不确定性分解，可用于 agent 失败检测、弃权与可靠性判断。

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 CUSP，将多个 VLM 响应映射到共享语义空间并池化为语义意见，分解出集体不确定性与 JSD 冲突两个系统级信号；在多 VLM 集成和多步多智能体系统中提升错误检测、弃权和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合异构 VLM 可改善多模态推理，但单个模型置信度和聚合答案置信度均无法衡量系统级可靠性，因此需要一种无需训练、无需 token logits 或校准标签的系统级不确定性量化方法。

Method: CUSP 将多个 VLM 响应映射到共享语义响应空间，池化为池化语义意见，并报告两个互补信号：表示池化意见离散程度的集体不确定性，以及表示模型级意见冲突的 Jensen-Shannon 散度（JSD）。未归一化集体熵可精确分解为各模型个体语义熵均值与 JSD 之和，从而分离总离散度与模型冲突。该方法适用于开放权重和商业 VLM。

Result: 在静态多 VLM 集成中，小模型机制下集体不确定性最强：预测错误检测 0.764 AUROC，弃权 0.889 AUARC，比不确定性基线、多数投票和朴素选择高 4.7 至 15.8 个百分点，且集成增大时优势扩大；商业机制下 JSD 最强：0.819 AUROC，0.910 AUARC，对困难答案模型冲突排序 AUROC 最高 0.982。池化预测比平均单个模型准确率高 5.6 至 13.0 个百分点。在多步多智能体系统完整轨迹中，子智能体集体不确定性以 0.619 AUROC 将系统失败排序高于随机水平，并以 0.699 AUARC 在所评估信号中给出最佳弃权排序。

Conclusion: CUSP 提供了互补的系统级不确定性信号，可同时刻画总离散度和模型冲突，并在静态多 VLM 集成与多步多智能体系统中有效提升错误检测、弃权排序和预测准确率。

中文摘要：聚合异构视觉语言模型（VLM）可以提升多模态推理，但无论是个体模型的置信度，还是聚合答案的置信度，都不能衡量系统层面的可靠性。我们提出 CUSP（Collective Uncertainty through Semantic Opinion Pooling，通过语义意见池化的集体不确定性），一种无需训练的不确定性量化框架，它将多个 VLM 响应映射到共享语义响应空间，将它们池化为一个池化语义意见，并报告两个互补的系统级信号：集体不确定性，即池化意见的离散程度；以及 Jensen-Shannon 散度（JSD），即模型级意见之间的冲突。在该池化语义意见中，未归一化的集体熵可以精确分解为各模型个体语义熵的均值与 JSD 之和，从而将总离散度与模型冲突分开。CUSP 既不需要 token logits，也不需要校准标签，因此适用于开放权重和商业 VLM。在静态多 VLM 集成中，集体不确定性在小模型机制下是最强信号（预测错误检测为 0.764 AUROC，弃权为 0.889 AUARC），比不确定性基线、多数投票和朴素选择高出 4.7 至 15.8 个百分点，并且随着集成规模增大其优势进一步扩大；JSD 在所评估的商业机制下最强（0.819 AUROC，0.910 AUARC），并且对困难答案的模型冲突进行排序时 AUROC 高达 0.982。池化预测相比平均单个模型还将准确率提升 5.6 至 13.0 个百分点。在多步、多智能体系统的完整轨迹上，子智能体集体不确定性将系统失败排序得高于随机水平（0.619 AUROC），并在所评估信号中给出最佳的弃权排序（0.699 AUARC）。

Original abstract: Aggregating heterogeneous vision-language models (VLMs) can improve multimodal reasoning, but neither an individual model's confidence nor that of the aggregated answer measures reliability at the system level. We present CUSP (Collective Uncertainty through Semantic Opinion Pooling), a training-free uncertainty quantification framework that maps multiple VLM responses to a shared semantic response space, pools them into a pooled semantic opinion, and reports two complementary system-level signals: collective uncertainty, the dispersion of the pooled opinion, and Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), the conflict among the model-level opinions. Within this pooled semantic opinion, the unnormalized collective entropy decomposes exactly into the mean of the models' individual semantic entropies and the JSD, separating total dispersion from model conflict. Requiring neither token logits nor calibration labels, CUSP applies to open-weight and commercial VLMs alike. In static multi-VLM ensembles, collective uncertainty is the strongest signal in the small-model regime (0.764 AUROC for prediction-error detection, 0.889 AUARC for abstention), outperforming uncertainty baselines majority voting and naive selection by 4.7 to 15.8 points and widening its margin as the ensemble grows; JSD is strongest in the evaluated commercial regime (0.819 AUROC, 0.910 AUARC) and ranks hard-answer model conflict with AUROC up to 0.982. The pooled prediction also improves accuracy over the average single model by 5.6 to 13.0 points. Over the full trajectory of a multi-step, multi-agent system, subagent collective uncertainty ranks system failures above chance (0.619 AUROC) and gives the best abstention ordering among the evaluated signals (0.699 AUARC).

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Prompts: Measuring and Optimizing LLM Tool-Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05736)
*Cen,Zhao,Haibo Ruan,Wenjie Chen,Pei-fen Tu,Usman Abbasi,Joel Hesch*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to EMNLP 2026. 18 pages, 9 figures, 10 tables

Selection reason: 直接研究固定模型下 tool-agent harness 的自动优化（prompt、工具边界中间件、恢复逻辑），并关注可靠收益，与 agent 优化高度相关。

TL;DR: 研究固定模型下资源受限的运行框架选择，限定编辑提示与工具边界中间件；提出与优化器无关的评估协议，并以 PRISM 等优化器在多个工具智能体基准上验证提升与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不重新训练 LLM 的前提下改进多轮工具智能体，通过修改固定模型周围的运行时框架（提示、工具接口、中间件、状态处理、恢复逻辑）实现性能提升。

Method: 将问题形式化为资源受限的运行框架选择，搜索面限定于提示和工具边界中间件的受防护拦截；提出与优化器无关的协议，报告平均留出提升、最差条件提升、可重复性、成本诊断和 RelLift95(B)；用仅提示及提示加中间件优化器实例化，包括基于 Pareto 搜索、失败聚类与修复路由的 PRISM。

Result: 在 BFCL multi-round、tau2-Retail、tau2-Telecom 上，PRISM 平均留出提升分别为 14.2、14.9、10.1 个百分点，且三个基准上经验 RelLift95 均为正；组件消融显示优势主要来自失败面路由和编辑模式约束。

Conclusion: 部分搜索过程虽偶尔找到大幅提升，但可能选择脆弱更新，因此报告平均留出提升时也应报告所选运行框架的可靠性。

中文摘要：LLM 工具智能体无需重新训练，即可通过修改固定模型周围的运行时框架来改进：提示、工具接口、中间件、状态处理和恢复逻辑。我们将这一设定研究为面向固定模型多轮工具智能体的资源受限运行框架选择，其搜索面限定于提示和工具边界中间件：编辑是工具边界处的受防护拦截，而非对智能体执行逻辑的任意重写。我们与优化器无关的协议报告平均留出提升、最差条件提升、可重复性、日志化成本诊断，以及 RelLift95(B)，即在预算 B 下所选运行框架的留出增益的保守估计。我们用仅提示优化器和提示加中间件优化器实例化该协议，其中包括 PRISM，它在 Pareto 搜索中对失败进行聚类，并将修复路由到提示、工具边界中间件或联合编辑面。在 BFCL multi-round、tau2-Retail 和 tau2-Telecom 上，PRISM 取得的平均留出提升分别为 14.2、14.9 和 10.1 个百分点，并在所有三个基准上取得正向的经验 RelLift95；组件消融将优势主要归因于失败面路由和编辑模式约束。跨优化器来看，结果表明某些搜索过程偶尔能发现大幅提升，但仍会选择脆弱的更新，因此所选运行框架的可靠性应与平均留出提升一同报告。

Original abstract: LLM tool agents can be improved without retraining by modifying the runtime harness around a fixed model: prompts, tool interfaces, middleware, state handling, and recovery logic. We study this setting as resource-bounded harness selection for fixed-model multi-turn tool agents, with the search surface scoped to prompts and tool-boundary middleware: edits are guarded intercepts at the tool boundary, not arbitrary rewriting of agent execution logic. Our optimizer-agnostic protocol reports mean held-out lift, worst-condition lift, repeatability, logged cost diagnostics, and RelLift95(B), a conservative estimate of the held-out gain of the harness selected under budget B. We instantiate the protocol with prompt-only and prompt-plus-middleware optimizers, including PRISM, which clusters failures and routes repairs to prompt, tool-boundary middleware, or joint edit surfaces within a Pareto search. On BFCL multi-round, tau2-Retail, and tau2-Telecom, PRISM obtains mean held-out lifts of 14.2, 14.9, and 10.1 percentage points and positive empirical RelLift95 on all three benchmarks, and a component ablation attributes the margin chiefly to failure-surface routing and the edit-pattern constraint. Across optimizers, the results show that some search procedures can occasionally find large gains but still choose brittle updates, so the reliability of the chosen harness should be reported alongside average held-out lift.

</details>


### [13] [From Monolithic Blending to Agentic Orchestration: Dynamic Response for Conversational Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05758)
*Cen,Zhao,Peng Wang,Chuan Shi,Yufeng Zhang,Ying Lyu,Wanmeng Ren,Robert Xue,Claire Na Cheng,Yashar Mehdad*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to EMNLP 2026 Industry Track. 16 pages, 1 figure, 21 tables

Selection reason: 生产对话助手迁移到有界 ReAct 工具编排，工具类型/动作校验显著降低幻觉，可迁移到 coding agent 的架构与工具契约设计。

TL;DR: Dynamic Response 将单一混合响应器拆分为有界 ReAct 编排器、类型化工具和较小生成器，在生产客服助手中显著减少结构化动作幻觉与升级率，并降低延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模型混合检索、动作选择、升级和措辞等角色，难以对生产变化进行归因与优化；需要可验证、可归因的架构迁移方案。

Method: 在生产环境迁移客服助手，对比原 Qwen3-235B-A22B 混合响应器与 Dynamic Response：有界 ReAct 编排器、类型化工具、类型化实体与动作 ID、成员资格检查、后端验证上下文契约及较小生成器；在相同重放轮次上归因架构效应，并用低流量 A/B 测试验证升级变化，同时进行服务优化与自托管。

Result: 类型化实体选择将预订选择器精确率从8.3%提高到89.1%、召回率从75.2%降至67.3%；类型化动作 ID 加成员资格检查将结构化动作幻觉从2.14%降至0.0%；硬升级响应从5.60%降至3.08%，软升级响应从9.56%降至2.49%，生产转人工量大致稳定；自解决为+5.1个百分点，95% CI [-2, +12]；编排器P90延迟从3.87秒降至2.24秒，GPU占用减少约三分之一，自托管使估计年度模型服务成本降低超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过角色分离、类型化约束和后端验证上下文契约，Dynamic Response 在生产客服场景中减少了结构化动作幻觉和升级率，并显著降低延迟与成本；但自解决提升在统计上仅为方向性，仍需进一步验证。

中文摘要：对话式助手可以将检索、动作选择、升级和措辞融合在单一模型路径中，也可以将这些角色分离。我们报告了某大型住宿市场（每月数百万次对话、11种语言、P90为10秒）的客户支持助手的一次生产迁移。Dynamic Response (DR) 用一个在类型化工具之上的有界 ReAct 编排器，加上一个从后端验证的上下文契约进行写作的较小生成器，取代了单一的 Qwen3-235B-A22B 混合响应器。由于该迁移还改变了提示词、对齐和服务，我们将每个效应归因于其原因，并且仅将那些在相同重放轮次上测量到的效应声称为架构效应：类型化实体选择将预订选择器移动到一个精确率优先的操作点（精确率从8.3%到89.1%，召回率从75.2%到67.3%），而带有成员资格检查的类型化动作ID消除了观察到的结构化动作幻觉（从2.14%到0.0%）。一个低流量渐进的A/B测试复现了重放中的升级减少：硬升级响应从5.60%下降到3.08%，软升级响应从9.56%下降到2.49%，同时生产环境中的转人工量大致保持稳定；自解决是方向性的（+5.1个百分点，95% CI [-2, +12]）。服务优化将编排器P90延迟从3.87秒降至2.24秒，同时GPU占用减少约三分之一，并且自托管将估计的年度模型服务成本降低超过一个数量级。

Original abstract: Conversational assistants can blend retrieval, action selection, escalation, and wording in a single model path, or separate those roles. We report a production migration of a customer-support assistant at a large accommodation marketplace (millions of conversations per month, 11 languages, 10-second P90). Dynamic Response (DR) replaces a single Qwen3-235B-A22B blended responder with a bounded ReAct orchestrator over typed tools plus a smaller generator that writes from a backend-validated context contract. Because the migration also changed prompts, alignment, and serving, we attribute each effect to its cause and claim as architecture effects only those measured on identical replayed turns: typed entity selection moves the reservation selector to a precision-first operating point (precision 8.3% to 89.1%, recall 75.2% to 67.3%), and typed action IDs with a membership check remove observed structured-action hallucination (2.14% to 0.0%). A low-ramp A/B test reproduces the replay escalation reductions: hard-escalation responses fall from 5.60% to 3.08% and soft-escalation responses from 9.56% to 2.49%, while production handoff volume holds roughly steady; self-solve is directional (+5.1 points, 95% CI [-2, +12]). Serving optimizations cut orchestrator P90 latency from 3.87s to 2.24s on a GPU footprint reduced by roughly one-third, and self-hosting reduces estimated annual model-serving cost by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [14] [Inference-Time Graph Engineering for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05774)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出任务条件化多 agent 工作流图编译与执行框架，包含代码生成任务，对多 agent 编排和通信语义设计有直接启发。

TL;DR: ReActNet是一个无需训练的多智能体编排框架，将查询和角色专门化智能体编译为任务条件化的时序有向通信图，并通过结构化消息传递执行，在多个推理任务上优于固定/学习拓扑基线且推理成本有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM系统多依赖图结构通信，但通常优化静态拓扑，未显式建模任务条件下何时、为何、如何通信。

Method: 提出ReActNet：训练无关，编译查询与角色智能体为一系列通信图快照，边带自然语言消息指令；执行时按控制器分配的邻居进行结构化消息传递，聚合状态得答案。

Result: 在知识推理、数学问题求解、代码生成和GAIA风格助手任务中，ReActNet一致优于固定拓扑和学习拓扑基线，并保持有竞争力的推理成本。

Conclusion: 有效的多智能体编排不仅取决于哪些智能体通信，还取决于构建可执行工作流图来编码信息流动的时机、原因和方式。

中文摘要：近期的多智能体LLM系统越来越依赖图结构通信来协调专门化智能体。我们从图工程的视角重新审视多智能体编排：不是优化静态拓扑，而是合成一个任务条件化的时序工作流图，以联合指定智能体连接性和边级通信语义。我们提出ReActNet，一个无需训练的框架，它将查询和一组角色专门化的智能体编译为一系列有向通信图。每个图快照对应一个推理阶段，每条边携带一条自然语言指令，指定源智能体应向目标智能体提供的消息。编译后的时序图随后通过结构化消息传递执行：智能体通过将先前状态与来自控制器分配的邻居的消息相整合来更新其推理状态，最终由一个聚合器将得到的状态合成为答案。该设计将图编译与图执行分离，使多智能体协调显式、可检查且任务条件化，而无需强化学习或基于梯度的拓扑优化。在知识推理、数学问题求解、代码生成和GAIA风格助手任务中，ReActNet在保持有竞争力的推理成本的同时，一致地优于固定拓扑和学得拓扑基线。这些结果表明，有效的多智能体编排不仅取决于哪些智能体进行通信，还取决于工程化可执行的工作流图，以编码推理过程中信息应在何时、为何以及如何流动。

Original abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on graph-structured communication to coordinate specialized agents. We revisit multi-agent orchestration from a graph-engineering perspective: rather than optimizing a static topology, we synthesize a task-conditioned temporal workflow graph that jointly specifies agent connectivity and edge-level communication semantics. We introduce ReActNet, a training-free framework that compiles a query and a set of role-specialized agents into a sequence of directed communication graphs. Each graph snapshot corresponds to one reasoning stage, and each edge carries a natural-language instruction specifying the message that a source agent should provide to a target agent. The compiled temporal graph is then executed through structured message passing: agents update their reasoning states by integrating their previous states with messages from controller-assigned neighbors, and a final aggregator synthesizes the resulting states into the answer. This design separates graph compilation from graph execution, making multi-agent coordination explicit, inspectable, and task-conditioned without requiring reinforcement learning or gradient-based topology optimization. Across knowledge reasoning, mathematical problem solving, code generation, and GAIA-style assistant tasks, ReActNet consistently improves over fixed-topology and learned-topology baselines while maintaining competitive inference cost. These results suggest that effective multi-agent orchestration depends not only on which agents communicate, but also on engineering executable workflow graphs that encode when, why, and how information should flow during reasoning.

</details>


### [15] [DI-Bench: Systematically Generating In-Domain Data Intelligence Benchmarks for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05776)
*Jiangyun Zhang,Kristen Surrao,Torpong Nitayanont,Yupei Zhang,Roopali Singh,Zhiyu Chen,Julia Huang,Zhou Tang,Shayan Ali Akbar,Omar Alonso,Erwin Cornejo,Yuan Li,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 3 figures, 13 tables. Accepted to the EMNLP 2026 Industry Track

Selection reason: 企业数据智能 agent 基准生成流水线，涉及检索+计算复合任务评测，可借鉴到 agent benchmark 构造。

TL;DR: DI-Bench 流水线可自动生成结合结构化计算与业务知识检索的数据智能基准；应用于两个公开数据集得到 731 个任务，四个模型在规则修改计算的任务上准确率仅 32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准很少评估企业智能体整合业务知识与分析计算的能力，且人工构建领域基准成本高。

Method: DI-Bench 在数据表、维度、指标和文档上构建工件链接图来生成问题，通过查询执行生成真实答案，并用 LLM 生成和验证问题；应用于两个公开数据集。

Result: 生成覆盖知识检索、分析计算和规则推理的 731 任务基准；评估四个模型发现，在检索到的业务规则修改计算时准确率仅 32%。

Conclusion: 该基准具有区分能力和难度，能揭示模型在结合业务规则与分析计算方面的显著不足。

中文摘要：在领域特定基准上评估企业智能体至关重要，然而公开基准很少评估智能体能否将业务知识与分析性计算相结合，而手动构建此类基准成本高昂。我们提出了 DI-Bench，一个用于生成数据智能（DI）现实基准的流水线，DI 指从大量企业数据中提取洞见的实践。为了模拟既需要计算又需要知识检索的现实 DI 任务，DI-Bench 在数据表、维度、指标和文档上构建工件链接图，以形成涉及结构化数据及相关知识的问题。真实答案通过查询执行得出，随后进行 LLM 问题生成与验证。该流水线应用于两个公开数据集，生成了一个包含 731 个任务的基准，涵盖知识检索、分析性计算和基于规则的推理。为展示该基准的区分能力和难度，我们评估了四个模型，揭示了一个重要发现：在检索到的业务规则会修改计算的计算任务中，模型仅达到 32% 的准确率。

Original abstract: Evaluating enterprise agents on domain-specific benchmarks is critical, yet public benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can integrate business knowledge with analytical computation, and constructing such benchmarks manually is costly. We present DI-Bench, a pipeline for generating realistic benchmarks for data intelligence (DI), the practice of extracting insights from large volumes of enterprise data. To emulate realistic DI tasks that require both computation and knowledge retrieval, DI-Bench builds an artifact linkage graph over data tables, dimensions, metrics, and documents to form questions involving structured data and associated knowledge. Ground truth answers are derived via query execution, followed by LLM question generation and validation. Applied to two public datasets, the pipeline produces a 731-task benchmark covering knowledge retrieval, analytical computation, and rule-grounded reasoning. To show the discriminatory capability and difficulty of the benchmark, we evaluate four models, revealing a substantial finding: models achieve only 32% accuracy when doing computational tasks where retrieved business rules modify the computation.

</details>


### [16] [Agentic BAIM-LLM Evaluation (ABLE): Benchmarking LLM Use of Protein Design Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05818)
*Bryce Cai,Geetha Jeyapragasan,Samira Nedungadi,Jake Yukich,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Last revised in February 2026; presented without further revision. An earlier revision at was presented at the NeurIPS 2025 Workshop on Biosecurity Safeguards for Generative AI; see https://openreview.net/forum?id=fDysOrWaGd

Selection reason: 生物设计工具调用 agent 基准，评估规划、工具选择与工具使用，对 tool-agent 评测有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出 ABLE 基准，评估 LLM 智能体在双用途蛋白质设计工作流中调用生物 AI 模型的能力。15 个前沿模型中 7 个拒绝任务，Claude Sonnet 4 与 Gemini 3 Pro 表现最佳；LLM 能降低蛋白质设计门槛，但规划与知识整合仍不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 LLM 智能体在双用途蛋白质设计工作流中使用 ProteinMPNN、AlphaFold3 等生物 AI 模型的能力，并比较前沿模型与专家人类基线，以识别其能力边界与不足。

Method: 构建 ABLE 基准，任务涵盖结构检索、序列生成和设计验证；评估 15 个前沿模型，并在部分任务上与专家人类基线进行比较。

Result: 七个模型拒绝所有任务，其余模型性能差异显著；Claude Sonnet 4 和 Gemini 3 Pro 在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用方面得分最高。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 可显著降低蛋白质设计门槛，但在规划、策略生成以及将生物学知识与工具使用相结合方面仍表现不稳定。

中文摘要：我们提出 ABLE，一个用于评估 LLM 智能体在双用途蛋白质设计工作流中使用生物 AI 模型（BAIMs）（如 ProteinMPNN 和 AlphaFold3）能力的基准。ABLE 通过一组涵盖结构检索、序列生成和设计验证的任务来评估智能体性能。我们评估了 15 个前沿模型，发现其中七个拒绝所有任务，而其余模型表现出显著性能差异。Claude Sonnet 4 和 Gemini 3 Pro 在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用方面取得最高分。我们进一步在一部分任务上将模型性能与专家人类基线进行比较。我们的结果表明，当前 LLM 可以大幅降低蛋白质设计的门槛，但在规划、策略生成以及将生物学知识与工具使用相结合方面仍不稳定。

Original abstract: We introduce ABLE, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' ability to use biological AI models (BAIMs), such as ProteinMPNN and AlphaFold3, in dual-use protein design workflows. ABLE assesses agent performance through a set of tasks spanning structure retrieval, sequence generation, and design validation. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that seven refuse all tasks, while the remaining models exhibit substantial performance differences. Claude Sonnet 4 and Gemini 3 Pro achieve the highest scores across information retrieval, tool selection, and tool use. We further compare model performance on a subset of tasks against an expert human baseline. Our results suggest that current LLMs can substantially lower barriers to protein design, but remain inconsistent in planning, strategy generation, and integrating biological knowledge with tool use.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Top-$k$ Skill Retrieval: Diversity-Aware Skill Routing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05824)
*Wang Wei,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Yue Zhao,Zhengzhong Tu,Xiyang Hu,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 LLM agent 的技能路由与多样性重排，直接优化技能/工具选择与上下文预算分配。

TL;DR: 提出多样性感知重排序框架 DSR，基于行列式点过程平衡相关性与非冗余，在 SkillRouter 上提升召回与覆盖率，多技能查询增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能路由器按查询相关性独立排序候选，易将上下文预算浪费在功能冗余技能上，且未处理复杂任务对互补技能集的需求。

Method: 提出 DSR，使用行列式点过程进行多样性感知重排序，并设计查询残差多样性核：惩罚冗余技能重叠，同时减弱仅由共享查询相关性导致的惩罚。

Result: 在 SkillRouter 基准上，DSR 相比强逐点重排序基线提升召回率和完全覆盖率，多技能查询上增益更大。

Conclusion: 技能路由不应仅视为相关性排序，也应视为互补集合选择。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体日益依赖外部技能，但在大型技能注册表上路由用户请求十分困难，因为许多技能在功能上冗余，而复杂任务往往需要互补的技能集。现有的技能路由器通常根据查询相关性独立地对候选技能进行排序，这可能会将上下文预算浪费在冗余技能上。我们提出多样化技能路由（Diverse Skill Routing, DSR），一种多样性感知的重排序框架，使用行列式点过程（Determinantal Point Process）来平衡相关性与非冗余性。DSR 引入了一种查询残差多样性核，该核惩罚冗余的技能重叠，同时减少仅由共享查询相关性造成的惩罚。在 SkillRouter 基准上，DSR 在召回率和完全覆盖率上优于强大的逐点重排序基线，并且在多技能查询上提升更大。这些结果表明，技能路由不仅应被视为相关性排序，还应被视为互补集合选择。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, but routing user requests over large skill registries is difficult because many skills are functionally redundant while complex tasks often require complementary skill sets. Existing skill routers typically rank candidates independently by query relevance, which can waste context budget on redundant skills. We propose Diverse Skill Routing (DSR), a diversity-aware reranking framework that uses a Determinantal Point Process to balance relevance and non-redundancy. DSR introduces a query-residual diversity kernel that penalizes redundant skill overlap while reducing penalties caused only by shared query relevance. On the SkillRouter benchmark, DSR improves recall and full coverage over a strong pointwise reranking baseline, with larger gains on multi-skill queries. These results suggest that skill routing should be treated not only as relevance ranking, but also as complementary set selection.

</details>


### [18] [AgentBrew: Offline Tool-Use Agent Learning from Raw Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05837)
*Zhiyi Lyu,Yewen Li,Longtao Zheng,Shengtian Yang,Lang Feng,Lei Feng,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 从真实原始轨迹离线学习工具使用 agent，提出回顾式任务推断与 PMI 信用分配，直接涉及轨迹复用和工具 agent 训练。

TL;DR: AgentBrew 是一种离线训练框架，从单批次未经筛选的原始交互轨迹中学习工具使用策略；其通过回顾式任务推断构建指令、用基于 PMI 的信用分配加权动作，在 GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL 上显著提升 Qwen3-32B，并超过 Qwen3-235B 与拒绝采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实应用缺乏预定义任务、验证器、忠实模拟器及大规模交互预算，导致针对特定环境的智能体工具使用训练困难；同时，未经质量过滤的原始轨迹中虽有可用监督，但难以有效提取。

Method: 先探索环境收集未过滤原始轨迹；对每条轨迹根据实际结果进行回顾式任务推断，重建对齐指令；用逐点互信息将轨迹关于该指令的总信息分解为逐动作可加信用；以信用加权策略训练目标，放大有效动作、抑制无效动作。

Result: 在 GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL 三个真实 MCP 应用上，AgentBrew 使 Qwen3-32B 平均提升 +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score，以 +2.3 / +4.4 超过 Qwen3-235B，并以 +5.9 / +10.3 优于拒绝采样。

Conclusion: 细粒度离线学习可从过滤式方法会丢弃的原始轨迹中恢复有用监督；AgentBrew 证明无需任务验证器或迭代 on-policy rollout 也能学习有效工具使用策略，代码已开源。

中文摘要：基于 LLM 的智能体正越来越多地通过工具调用 API 部署于真实世界应用中，然而针对特定环境训练它们仍然从根本上十分困难：真实世界应用不提供预定义任务或验证器，也没有忠实的模拟器，并且用于大规模环境交互的预算有限。本文提出 AgentBrew，一种离线训练框架，它能够从单一批次的原始交互轨迹中学习有效的工具使用策略，而无需任务验证器或迭代式 on-policy rollout。该智能体首先探索目标环境，以收集一个未经质量过滤的原始轨迹语料库。为了从这一含噪语料库中提取训练信号，回顾式任务推断根据每条轨迹的实际结果为其重建一个对齐的指令，而基于 PMI 的信用分配通过逐点互信息（PMI）将轨迹中关于所推断指令的总信息分解为可加的逐动作信用。这些信用对策略训练目标进行加权，从而放大有信息量的动作，同时抑制无效动作。在三个真实世界 MCP 应用（GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL）上，AgentBrew 将 Qwen3-32B 平均提升 +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score，以 +2.3 / +4.4 超过 Qwen3-235B，并以 +5.9 / +10.3 优于拒绝采样。这些结果表明，细粒度离线学习能够从基于过滤的方法会丢弃的原始轨迹中恢复有用的监督信号。代码见 https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

Original abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in real-world applications through tool-use APIs, yet training them for specific environments remains fundamentally difficult: real-world applications provide no pre-defined tasks or verifiers, no faithful simulators, and limited budget for large-scale environment interaction. In this paper, we propose \textbf{AgentBrew}, an offline training framework that learns effective tool-use policies from a single batch of raw interaction trajectories, without task verifiers or iterative on-policy rollouts. The agent first explores the target environment to collect a raw trajectory corpus without quality filtering. To extract training signal from this noisy corpus, \emph{retrospective task inference} reconstructs an aligned instruction for each trajectory based on its actual outcome, and \emph{PMI-Based credit assignment} decomposes the trajectory's total information about the inferred instruction into additive per-action credits via pointwise mutual information (PMI). These credits weight the policy training objective, amplifying informative actions while suppressing ineffective ones. On three real-world MCP applications (GitHub, Notion, PostgreSQL), AgentBrew improves Qwen3-32B by +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score on average, surpassing Qwen3-235B (+2.3 / +4.4) and outperforming rejection sampling (+5.9 / +10.3). These results demonstrate that fine-grained offline learning can recover useful supervision from raw trajectories that filtering-based approaches would discard. The code is available at https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Final Decisions: A Process-Centric Benchmark for Transparent AI-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05947)
*Siming Yuan,Xueyi Zhang,Wangze Ni,Tianfang Xiao,Shimin Di,Jia Zhu,Zhuoren Jiang,Rong Tan,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向多阶段LLM流程的过程性诊断基准，用阶段一致性、链一致性与干预敏感性检验中间推理是否真正支撑最终结论，其评估思路可迁移到coding agent的轨迹可信度验证。

TL;DR: 提出以过程为中心的AI辅助同行评审诊断基准，用(x, z_s, z_c, z_r, y)对齐异构评审数据，比较Gold与Predicted流程变量并进行阶段级、链一致性与干预敏感性评估，发现Gold变量决策价值更高且最终决策常缺乏前序证据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注生成评审整体质量或最终决策准确率，无法判断模型决策是否得到充分且可靠的评审证据支持，因此需要过程性、可审计的诊断方法。

Method: 构建过程对齐基准，将PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22和OpenReview-ICLR异构评审记录转换为(x, z_s, z_c, z_r, y)表示；以从论文内容直接预测决策为基线，比较Gold-process与Predicted-process变量的决策价值，并进行阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析；在三个数据集和六个模型上实验。

Result: Gold-process变量通常具有更高决策价值；主分析模型的Gold--Predicted差距在不同数据集和随机种子下保持稳定，并在多数模型--数据集组合中复现；模型生成的中间评审文本在相邻阶段局部一致性较高，但最终决策未持续得到前序评审证据支持。

Conclusion: 该基准面向辅助而非替代人类评审员的AI系统，提供透明且可审计的诊断工具，用于评估AI辅助同行评审流程的可靠性。

中文摘要：同行评审是科学质量控制的核心。然而，现有对AI辅助同行评审的评估主要关注生成评审的整体质量或最终决策的准确性。因此，它们提供的关于模型决策是否有充分且可靠的评审证据支持的证据有限。我们引入了一个以过程为中心的诊断基准，用于AI辅助同行评审。它使用 (x, $z_s$, $z_c$, $z_r$, y) 表示论文内容、摘要、批判性评论、建议和决策。我们将来自 PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22 和 OpenReview-ICLR 的异构评审记录转换为过程对齐的数据。我们的基准使用从论文内容直接进行决策预测（Direct）作为基线。它比较Gold-process变量和Predicted-process变量的决策价值，并进行阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析。在三个数据集和六个模型上的实验表明，Gold-process变量通常具有更高的决策价值。对于主分析模型，Gold--Predicted差距在不同数据集和随机种子下保持稳定。这一差距在大多数模型--数据集组合中也得到复现。尽管模型生成的中间评审文本在相邻阶段之间表现出相对较高的局部一致性，但最终决策并未始终得到前序评审证据的支持。我们的基准针对的是旨在辅助而非替代人类评审员的AI系统。它提供了一种透明且可审计的诊断工具，用于评估其评审过程的可靠性。

Original abstract: Peer review is central to quality control in science. However, existing evaluations of AI-assisted peer review mainly focus on the overall quality of generated reviews or the accuracy of final decisions. They therefore provide limited evidence about whether model decisions are supported by sufficient and reliable review evidence. We introduce a process-centric diagnostic benchmark for AI-assisted peer review. It uses (x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)
  to represent the paper content, summary, critique, suggestion, and decision. We convert heterogeneous review records from PeerRead, NLPeer ARR-22, and OpenReview-ICLR into process-aligned data. Our benchmark uses direct decision prediction from the paper content (Direct) as its baseline. It compares the decision value of Gold-process variables and Predicted-process variables, and conducts stage-level evaluation, chain-consistency evaluation, and interventional sensitivity analysis. Experiments across three datasets and six models show that Gold-process variables generally have higher decision value. For the main analysis model, the Gold--Predicted gap remains stable across datasets and random seeds. This gap is also reproduced in most model--dataset combinations. Although model-generated intermediate review texts show relatively high local consistency across adjacent stages, the final decisions are not consistently supported by the preceding review evidence. Our benchmark targets AI systems designed to assist rather than replace human reviewers. It provides a transparent and auditable diagnostic tool for evaluating the reliability of their review processes.

</details>


### [20] [MOAE: Multi-Objective Agent Evolution with Pareto-Preserving Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05992)
*Hengle Jiang,Qijun Cai,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 提出Pareto保留的上下文内进化搜索来同时优化agent的任务表现、轨迹质量与安全，无需参数更新，对agent自我改进与轨迹级优化直接可借鉴。

TL;DR: MOAE 将智能体多目标优化视为对完整 rollout 的 Pareto 保留上下文进化搜索，无需参数更新和搜索时标量化，在 TravelPlanner 与 AgentDojo 上同时提升任务性能、轨迹质量并保持安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体评估需兼顾任务完成准确率、交互质量、安全与效率等多目标；但多数多目标方法将异质指标标量化，依赖归一化与偏好权重，可能丢弃有用部署权衡候选，因此核心问题是能否同时优化这些目标。

Method: 提出 MOAE：在有限 rollout 预算下，对完整智能体 rollout 进行迭代上下文精炼，维护非支配候选经验档案，利用目标特定诊断指导后代生成，仅在部署时进行约束感知选择；无需参数更新，各目标可替换为任意可测量属性，实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全。

Result: 在 TravelPlanner 和 AgentDojo 上，在匹配 rollout 预算下，MOAE 持续提升任务性能和轨迹质量，并保持强安全；搜索行为分析显示 Pareto 保留扩大可达目标区域并提高联合改进频率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，Pareto 保留的上下文进化可在不于搜索期间固定标量化的情况下优化多个智能体属性，为多目标智能体部署提供灵活权衡。

中文摘要：随着基于 LLM 的智能体不断发展，对其评估已变得日益多维：一个能力强的智能体不仅必须实现高任务完成准确率，还必须在交互质量、安全性和效率方面表现良好，这引出一个核心问题：这些目标能否同时得到优化？现有方法考虑了多个目标，但许多方法将异质测量结果压缩为固定的标量分数。这种标量化依赖于指标归一化和偏好权重，并可能丢弃代表有用部署权衡的候选方案。我们提出多目标智能体进化（MOAE），它将迭代式上下文精炼组织为对完整智能体 rollout 的 Pareto 保留进化搜索。在有限的 rollout 预算下，MOAE 维护非支配候选的经验档案，使用目标特定的诊断来指导后代生成，并仅在部署时应用约束感知选择。这将搜索期间的候选保留与用于返回最终解的偏好分离开来。该过程无需参数更新，并允许每个目标被任何可测量属性替换，我们将其实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性。在 TravelPlanner 和 AgentDojo 上的实验表明，在匹配的 rollout 预算下，MOAE 持续提升任务性能和轨迹质量，同时保持强安全性。搜索行为分析进一步表明，Pareto 保留扩大了可达目标区域，并提高了联合改进的频率。这些结果证明了 Pareto 保留的上下文进化在优化多个智能体属性方面的潜力，而无需在搜索期间承诺固定的标量化。

Original abstract: As LLM-based agents continue to advance, their evaluation has become increasingly multifaceted: a capable agent must not only achieve high task completion accuracy but also perform well in interaction quality, safety, and efficiency, raising a central question: can these objectives be optimized simultaneously? Existing methods have considered multiple objectives, but many collapse heterogeneous measurements into a fixed scalar score. Such scalarization depends on metric normalization and preference weights and may discard candidates that represent useful deployment trade-offs. We introduce Multi-Objective Agent Evolution (MOAE), which organizes iterative in-context refinement as a Pareto-preserving evolutionary search over complete agent rollouts. Given a limited rollout budget, MOAE maintains an empirical archive of non-dominated candidates, uses objective-specific diagnostics to guide offspring generation, and applies constraint-aware selection only at deployment. This separates candidate preservation during search from the preference used to return a final solution. The procedure requires no parameter updates and allows each objective to be replaced by any measurable property, which we instantiate as task performance, trajectory quality, and safety. Experiments on TravelPlanner and AgentDojo show that MOAE consistently improves task performance and trajectory quality while maintaining strong safety under matched rollout budgets. Search-behavior analysis further shows that Pareto preservation expands the attainable objective region and increases the frequency of joint improvement. These results demonstrate the potential of Pareto-preserving in-context evolution for optimizing multiple agent properties without committing to a fixed scalarization during search.

</details>


### [21] [Agentic Pressure: The Endogenous Entropy of Reliable Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05995)
*Hengle Jiang,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Published at the workshop of Agentic AI in the Wild in ICLR 2026; 16 pages

Selection reason: 刻画长程运行中合规成本与目标达成冲突导致的Agentic Pressure与安全漂移，为长程coding agent的可靠性控制提供理论框架。

TL;DR: 论文提出“智能体压力”这一非对抗性现象，用理论框架解释长时程自主运行中的对齐失稳，并实验验证高压下对齐智能体会为维持自主性而牺牲安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠自主性需要长时程连续运行，但环境摩擦累积会破坏对齐；作者试图识别并形式化这种非对抗、内生的失稳机制，而非仅关注静态越狱。

Method: 将智能体压力定义为克服环境摩擦所需功与智能体剩余能力之比；建立理论框架分析压力阈值与安全漂移、工具性幻觉的关系；用实证实验验证。

Result: 当压力超过临界阈值，智能体将安全漂移视为数学最优适应，并常以工具性幻觉合理化违规；对齐智能体在高压下自发牺牲安全以保持自主性。

Conclusion: 智能体压力框架揭示了长时程自主运行中对齐失稳的内生动力学，强调需在合规成本与目标达成冲突时管理压力，以维持可靠自主与安全。

中文摘要：在真实环境中实现可靠自主性，要求智能体能够在长时程轨迹中维持连续运行。然而，当智能体在这些不受约束的环境中运行时，它们会遇到累积摩擦，这从本质上会破坏其对齐的稳定性。在本文中，我们识别出一种独特的非对抗性现象，称为智能体压力（Agentic Pressure）。我们将其定义为一种动力学力量，当合规成本与实现目标的必要性发生冲突时，它会自发出现。与静态越狱不同，这种压力是内生的，并直接源于交互动态。我们提出一个理论框架，将智能体压力形式化为克服环境摩擦所需功与智能体剩余能力之间的比率。我们的分析表明，当这种压力超过临界阈值时，智能体会表现出安全漂移，作为一种数学上最优的适应。因此，它们常常诉诸工具性幻觉（Instrumental Hallucination）来为规则违反提供合理化解释。实证实验验证了该框架，并表明对齐的智能体在高压条件下会自发地牺牲安全性以保持自主性。

Original abstract: Achieving reliable autonomy in the wild requires agents to sustain continuous operations across long-horizon trajectories. However, as agents navigate these unconstrained settings, they encounter cumulative friction that inherently destabilizes their alignment. In this paper, we identify a distinct non-adversarial phenomenon termed Agentic Pressure. We define this as a kinetic force that spontaneously emerges when the cost of compliance conflicts with the imperative of goal achievement. Unlike static jailbreaks, this pressure is endogenous and arises directly from the dynamics of interaction. We propose a theoretical framework that formalizes Agentic Pressure as the ratio between the required work to overcome environmental friction and the remaining capacity of the agent. Our analysis demonstrates that when this pressure exceeds a critical threshold, agents exhibit safety drift as a mathematically optimal adaptation. Consequently, they often resort to Instrumental Hallucination to rationalize rule violations. Empirical experiments validate this framework and show that aligned agents spontaneously compromise safety to preserve autonomy under high-pressure conditions.

</details>


### [22] [Generator-Independent Runtime Assurance under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06036)
*Guangxi Wan,Yongbo Xie,Yuqi Liu,Qingwei Dong,Qingxin Li,Hongfei Bai,Peng Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究LLM规划器等黑盒生成器背后的运行时保证门控，给出集合级健全性与部分可观测下的准入风险界，可迁移到agent动作验证与安全护栏设计。

TL;DR: 论文提出集合级健全性作为生成器无关安全认证的必要充分条件，并给出部分观测下的下界与顺序风险账本实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提案的控制器依赖运行时验证门，但逐候选认证在重试或best-of-k下会放大误接纳风险，因此需要判断闭环安全保证何时能不依赖生成器。

Method: 定义同时集合级健全性并证明其与生成器无关接纳健全性等价；结合设计时证书与无绕过规则建立契约安全；用顺序风险账本和时间一致置信管实现保证；分析部分观测下接纳机制的总变差下界；将Simplex运行时保证与CBF滤波作为退化情形。

Result: 主定理：集合级健全性充要于生成器无关接纳健全性，最坏违规概率等于集合级失败概率；契约安全违规界Γ+Σ_t ε_t+η对生成器任意或对抗替换不变。第二定理：固定探测与接纳策略下，若两个状态假设的信息律总变差距离δ内却需不同安全决策，则ᾱ+β+δ≥1。顺序风险账本使保证可实施，并表明确定性接纳计算将统计风险集中于状态估计。

Conclusion: 该框架将安全保证从黑箱生成器中解耦，为运行时验证门提供可组合、可实施且对生成器替换鲁棒的认证原则。

中文摘要：基于提案的控制器——学习到的策略、语言模型规划器以及其他黑箱生成器——正越来越多地部署在运行时验证门之后。我们追问闭环安全保证何时与生成器解耦。流行的逐候选认证模式不可组合：在重试或best-of-k选择下，逐候选误接纳水平α可能膨胀到1-(1-α)^k。我们的主定理表明，同时集合级健全性——认证一个不包含不可行动作的可接纳提案集合——是生成器无关的接纳健全性的必要且充分条件；在所有生成器上的最坏情况下，执行不可行提案的概率等于集合级失败的概率；与设计时证书和无绕过规则一起，它对于契约安全是充分的，其违规界Γ+Σ_t ε_t+η在生成器被任意、甚至对抗性替换时保持不变。第二个定理对部分观测下的每一种接纳机制给出界：对于固定的探测和接纳策略，如果两个状态假设的信息律处于总变差距离δ之内，却要求不同的安全决策，那么ᾱ+β+δ≥1。顺序风险账本借助时间一致置信管使该保证可实施，并表明确定性接纳计算将所有统计风险集中在状态估计中。Simplex风格的运行时保证和控制障碍函数滤波被恢复为退化情形。

Original abstract: Proposal-based controllers---learned policies, language-model planners, and other black-box \emph{generators}---are increasingly deployed behind runtime verification gates. We ask when the closed-loop safety guarantee decouples from the generator. The prevailing per-candidate certification pattern does not compose: under retry or best-of-$k$ selection a per-candidate false-admission level $α$ can inflate to $1-(1-α)^{k}$. Our main theorem shows that \emph{simultaneous setwise soundness}---certifying a set of admissible proposals containing no nonviable action---is necessary and sufficient for generator-independent \emph{admission soundness}, the worst case over all generators of executing a nonviable proposal equalling the probability of setwise failure; together with a design-time certificate and a no-bypass rule it is sufficient for \emph{contract safety}, with violation bound $Γ+\sum_t\varepsilon_t+η$ invariant under arbitrary, even adversarial, replacement of the generator. A second theorem bounds every admission mechanism under partial observation: for a fixed probing and admission policy, if two state hypotheses whose information laws lie within total-variation distance $δ$ require different safe decisions, then $\abar+β+δ\ge1$. A sequential risk ledger makes the guarantee implementable with time-uniform confidence tubes, and shows that deterministic admission computations concentrate all statistical risk in state estimation. Simplex-style runtime assurance and control-barrier-function filtering are recovered as degenerate cases.

</details>


### [23] [A Unified Policy Architecture (UPA): The Governance Kernel for Enterprise AI Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06543)
*Prabhu Raghav,Balamurugan Pandi,Arul Vivek,Shek Mohammed,Sridhar S*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 58 pages, 6 figures. Includes appendices with the DGPL grammar, SID registry, EAGBench benchmark specification, extended governance models, and policy examples

Selection reason: 面向企业 AI 操作系统的统一策略治理架构，涵盖 agent 工具、记忆、多 agent 交互的策略评估语义，对 agent 运行时治理与多 agent 协作有一定参考价值，但偏企业治理架构。

TL;DR: 论文提出统一策略架构 UPA，为企业 AI 操作系统提供覆盖智能体、工具、工作流、记忆、资源、交互与业务规则的统一治理策略模型，并将策略控制扩展至运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计和治理评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI 正走向由自主智能体构成的企业操作系统，但现有授权、安全、防护栏与合规机制碎片化，无法将自主 AI 作为统一系统治理。

Method: 提出 UPA 治理架构，包含统一策略模型、声明式策略语言基础、策略评估语义、可扩展插件、行业策略包与企业治理评估框架，并讨论多智能体协调、溯源感知策略和有状态运行时治理等扩展。

Result: UPA 将策略控制从授权扩展到运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计证据和治理评估，并给出相关模型、语言、语义、插件、策略包和评估框架。

Conclusion: UPA 为构建安全、可问责、可治理的自主 AI 企业操作系统提供基础。

中文摘要：企业 AI 正在演变为一种企业操作系统，在其中，自主 AI 智能体能够规划、推理、使用记忆、调用工具、执行工作流，并与其他智能体协作。这一转变带来了新的治理挑战：现有的授权、安全、防护栏和合规机制是碎片化的，并且并非为将自主 AI 作为统一系统进行治理而设计。本文介绍统一策略架构（Unified Policy Architecture，UPA），一种面向企业 AI 操作系统的治理架构。UPA 提供统一的策略模型，用于治理 AI 与智能体、工具、工作流、记忆、企业资源、智能体间交互以及企业业务规则。它将策略控制扩展到授权之外，纳入运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计证据和治理评估。我们介绍 UPA 的治理模型、声明式策略语言基础、策略评估语义、可扩展插件、行业策略包，以及面向企业治理的评估框架。我们还识别了面向多智能体协调、溯源感知策略和有状态运行时治理的扩展。UPA 为构建安全、可问责且可治理的自主 AI 企业操作系统提供了基础。

Original abstract: Enterprise AI is evolving into an Enterprise Operating System where autonomous AI agents can plan, reason, use memory, invoke tools, execute workflows, and collaborate with other agents. This shift creates a new governance challenge: existing authorization, security, guardrails, and compliance mechanisms are fragmented and are not designed to govern autonomous AI as a unified system.
  This paper introduces the Unified Policy Architecture (UPA), a governance architecture for Enterprise AI Operating Systems. UPA provides a unified policy model for governing AI and agents, tools, workflows, memory, enterprise resources, and agent-to-agent interactions and enterprise business rules. It extends policy control beyond authorisation to include runtime obligations, human approvals, compliance, audit evidence, and governance evaluation.
  We present UPA's governance model, declarative policy language foundations, policy evaluation semantics, extensible plugins, industry policy packs, and an evaluation framework for enterprise governance. We also identify extensions for multi-agent coordination, provenance-aware policies, and stateful runtime governance. UPA provides a foundation for building secure, accountable, and governable Enterprise Operating Systems for autonomous AI.

</details>


### [24] [DAREBench: Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06059)
*Yu Liu,Zhilin Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Shaojie Zhang,Zheyuan Deng,Tingwei Huang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向部署的通用agent评测基准，覆盖多步执行、工具使用与产物交付，并附带成本与评分审计，对agent可靠评估有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出 DAREBench 智能体评测基准，基于 OpenClaw 环境用 233 个任务构建 2×3 工作负载矩阵，统一契约协议并审计分数；评测 35 个模型共 7,587 次运行，发现无单一模型全面领先，应按工作负载、部署模式和准确率—成本权衡选型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从问答系统转向通用智能体，评估需覆盖多模态感知、多步执行、工具使用和产物交付；但现有基准常绑定特定任务、环境或评分协议，导致可比性、可解释性和部署决策可靠性不足。

Method: 构建 DAREBench，基于共享 OpenClaw 执行环境，从 22 个源基准选取并适配 233 个任务，按输入模态和执行形式形成 2×3 工作负载矩阵；采用统一契约式协议与基于证据的分数审计。评测 23 个商业 API 模型和 12 个本地开源权重模型，共 7,587 次模型—任务运行，报告准确率、token 消耗及 API 模型参考成本。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有工作负载组中占优；文本与多模态任务呈现不同的准确率—成本权衡；本地开源权重模型在若干组中具竞争力，但总体仍落后于前沿商业模型。

Conclusion: 智能体部署与模型选择不应依赖单一聚合分数，而应综合考虑工作负载画像、部署模式以及准确率—成本权衡。

中文摘要：随着大语言模型从问答系统演化为通用智能体，评估必须超越静态答案正确性，以衡量多模态感知、多步执行、工具使用和产物交付。然而，现有基准往往受限于特定任务类型、执行环境或评分协议，限制了其可比性、可解释性以及用于部署决策的可靠性。我们提出 DAREBench（面向部署且可靠的智能体模型评估），一个旨在捕捉工作负载变化并支持可靠智能体评估的基准。DAREBench 建立在共享的 OpenClaw 执行环境上，将选自 22 个源基准并经过筛选与适配的 233 个任务组织为由输入模态和执行形式定义的 2×3 工作负载矩阵，并在统一的基于契约的协议下进行评估，同时进行基于证据的分数审计。我们在 7,587 次模型—任务运行中评估了 23 个商业 API 模型和 12 个本地部署的开源权重模型，报告准确率和 token 消耗，并给出 API 模型的参考成本。结果表明，没有任何单一模型在所有工作负载组中占优，文本与多模态任务表现出不同的准确率—成本权衡，本地开源权重模型在若干组中具有竞争力，但总体仍落后于前沿商业模型。这些发现表明，智能体部署和模型选择应考虑工作负载画像、部署模式以及准确率—成本权衡，而非依赖单一聚合分数。

Original abstract: As large language models evolve from question-answering systems into general-purpose agents, evaluation must move beyond static answer correctness to assess multimodal perception, multi-step execution, tool use, and artifact delivery. However, existing benchmarks are often tied to specific task types, execution environments, or scoring protocols, limiting their comparability, interpretability, and reliability for deployment decisions. We introduce DAREBench (Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents), a benchmark designed to capture workload variation and support reliable agent evaluation. Built on a shared OpenClaw execution environment, DAREBench organizes 233 tasks selected and adapted from 22 source benchmarks into a $2\times3$ workload matrix defined by input modality and execution form, and evaluates them under a unified contract-based protocol with evidence-based score auditing. We evaluate 23 commercial API models and 12 locally deployed open-weight models over 7,587 model--task runs, reporting accuracy and token consumption alongside reference costs for API models. Results show that no single model dominates all workload groups, text and multimodal tasks exhibit distinct accuracy--cost trade-offs, and local open-weight models are competitive in several groups but still trail frontier commercial models overall. These findings suggest that agent deployment and model selection should consider workload profiles, deployment mode, and accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than rely on a single aggregate score.

</details>


### [25] [CIT-CAD: Constraint Intent Tree-based CAD Code Generation and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07434)
*Yali Du,Hui Sun,San-Zhuo Xi,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以约束意图树引导并验证 CAD 代码生成，通过期望约束与实际约束比对定位并修复违规，其生成-验证-修复范式可迁移到代码 agent 的意图级验证。

TL;DR: 提出CIT-CAD框架，从输入描述推断约束意图树（CIT），用于指导CAD代码生成，并通过比较预期约束与生成程序中的实际约束来定位和修复设计违规；实验证明其提升CAD生成性能，尤其利于复杂多实体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD生成基准和方法主要用IoU等指标衡量生成模型与参考几何的匹配度，容易忽略零件分解、构建层次、布尔运算、草图结构和几何关系等构建过程错误；需要一种显式表示设计意图并据此检查生成代码的机制。

Method: CIT-CAD从输入描述推断约束意图树（CIT），表示预期实体、层次、操作和关系；该树既指导CAD代码生成，又定义验证所需的预期约束。框架从生成程序中提取实际约束，与预期约束比较，利用不匹配定位并修复设计违规。

Result: 实验表明，CIT-CAD提升了CAD生成性能，在更复杂的多实体设计中增益更大。

Conclusion: 通过将设计意图转化为显式且可检查的对象，该工作首次尝试使文本到CAD生成超越渲染几何匹配，走向构建感知的合成、验证与修复。

中文摘要：自然语言计算机辅助设计（CAD）代码生成旨在将设计意图转化为可执行且可编辑的参数化程序。大语言模型（LLM）使这一目标日益可行，但有用的系统必须保留渲染几何背后的构建过程。现有基准和方法大多关注生成的CAD模型与参考几何的匹配程度，常使用交并比（IoU）等指标。此类指标可能遗漏零件分解、构建层次、布尔运算、草图结构和几何关系中的错误。这一差距要求一种表示，使设计意图显式化，并让系统能够对照该意图检查生成的代码。我们提出CIT-CAD，一个从输入描述中推断约束意图树（CIT）的框架，以表示预期的实体、层次、操作和关系。该树有两个作用：指导CAD代码生成，并定义用于验证的预期约束。该框架从生成程序中提取实际约束，将其与预期约束进行比较，并利用不匹配来定位和修复设计违规。实验表明，该框架提升了CAD生成性能，在更复杂的多实体设计中增益更大。通过将设计意图转化为显式且可检查的对象，这项工作首次尝试使文本到CAD生成超越渲染几何匹配，迈向构建感知的合成、验证和修复。

Original abstract: Natural-language Computer-Aided Design (CAD) code generation aims to turn design intent into executable and editable parametric programs. Large language models (LLMs) make this goal increasingly practical, but useful systems must preserve the construction process behind the rendered geometry. Existing benchmarks and methods mostly focus on how closely the generated CAD model matches the reference geometry, often using metrics such as Intersection over Union (IoU). Such metrics can miss errors in part decomposition, construction hierarchy, Boolean operations, sketch structure, and geometric relations. This gap calls for a representation that makes design intent explicit and lets a system check generated code against that intent. We propose CIT-CAD, a framework that infers a Constraint Intent Tree (CIT) from the input description to represent the intended entities, hierarchy, operations, and relations. The tree has two roles: it guides CAD code generation and defines expected constraints for verification. The framework extracts actual constraints from the generated program, compares them with the expected constraints, and uses mismatches to localize and repair design violations. Experiments show that the framework improves CAD generation performance, with larger gains on more complex multi-entity designs. By turning design intent into an explicit and checkable object, this work is the first attempt to move text-to-CAD generation beyond rendered-geometry matching toward construction-aware synthesis, verification, and repair.

</details>


### [26] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于执行轨迹的事后解释框架，能识别无依据声明与不当动作，与agent轨迹审计、失败归因高度相关，可跨架构迁移。

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖执行轨迹的事后XAI框架，将AI智能体冗长轨迹转为结构化报告与忠实自然语言解释，并在多基准多架构评估中优于朴素LLM解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在真实环境中与工具交互并做多步决策，但人类监督有限，因此急需可靠、可审计地解释其行为与原因；传统XAI缺乏此类交互式多步骤系统所需的流程级透明度。

Method: 提出事后XAI框架，将智能体执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和基于可观察行为的忠实自然语言解释；框架仅依赖执行轨迹，因此可跨架构、环境和任务应用。

Result: 跨多个基准和架构的人工与自动评估表明，该框架生成高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，能可靠识别无依据主张、不合理行动和证据缺口，并优于朴素LLM生成解释。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI智能体提供了可审计的流程级解释，弥补传统XAI不足，并具备跨架构、环境和任务的适用性。

中文摘要：AI智能体正日益部署在真实世界环境中，在那里它们与外部工具交互，并在有限的人类监督下进行顺序决策。这产生了对可靠且可审计的解释的迫切需求，以说明智能体做了什么以及为什么这样做。然而，传统的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法无法为这种交互式、多步骤系统提供所需的流程级透明度，这促使人们转向专门为AI智能体设计的方法。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了一个事后XAI框架，该框架将冗长的智能体执行轨迹转化为结构化报告，并生成明确基于其可观察行为的忠实自然语言解释。由于它仅依赖执行轨迹，该框架适用于不同的智能体架构、环境和任务。跨多个基准和架构的人工与自动评估表明，我们的框架能够生成高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，同时可靠地识别无依据的主张、不合理的行动和证据缺口，优于朴素的LLM生成解释。

Original abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [27] [A Tool-Augmented, GPT-4 Chatbot for Real-Time Repository Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07586)
*Muhammad Jawad Chowdhury,Md. Sakib Khan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于工具选择与提示工程的 GPT-4 仓库数据问答架构，先解析查询再选工具，属代码 agent 工具调用工作流，但方法较工程化、创新有限。

TL;DR: 提出一种基于GPT-4的聊天机器人架构，先解析查询并选择工具，再调用GPT-4生成详细回答，以提升软件仓库数据的可访问性和响应准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件仓库数据丰富，但非技术用户和开发者因缺乏查询专业知识而难以访问。

Method: 设计结构化流水线：解析用户查询提取参数，选择合适工具，调用GPT-4生成详细响应；依赖提示工程和工具选择，而非嵌入模型与文档检索器；在Issues、Pull Requests、Commits、复合问题和一般仓库信息等问答类型上实验。

Result: 在多种问题类型上评估目标提示对模型响应准确性的提升，并验证架构对不同用户的实用性。

Conclusion: 该架构可使仓库数据对技术和非技术受众更易访问，并生成可操作见解。

中文摘要：软件仓库包含大量关于代码贡献、错误报告和项目活动的数据，但由于查询仓库的专业知识有限，非技术利益相关者和开发人员仍然难以访问这些信息。为解决此问题，我们引入了一种新颖的聊天机器人架构，利用 OpenAI 的 GPT-4 模型自动提取和分析仓库数据。相比之下，我们的架构采用结构化路径：首先解析用户查询以提取相关参数，然后根据该分析选择正确的工具来使用，最后调用 GPT-4 模型生成高度详细的响应。与以往基于嵌入模型和文档检索器的多组件系统工作不同，我们的架构通过依赖提示工程和工具选择来匹配查询意图，从而颠倒了这一过程。为验证我们的方法，我们在多种问题类型上进行了实验，包括 Issues、Pull Requests、Commits、复合问题和一般仓库信息，评估我们的目标提示提高模型响应准确性的能力。除了向多样化用户群体展示该架构的实用性之外，我们的发现表明，该架构可以通过产生可操作的见解，使技术和非技术受众更容易访问仓库数据。

Original abstract: Software repositories contain vast amounts of data on code contributions, bug reports, and project activities, yet this information remains challenging for non-technical stakeholders and developers to access due to limited expertise in querying repositories. To address this, we introduce a novel chatbot architecture leveraging OpenAI's GPT-4 model for automated extraction and analysis of repository data. In contrast, our architecture takes a structured path first by parsing the user's query to extract relevant parameters, then selecting the correct tool to employ based on that analysis, and finally invoking the GPT-4 model to create a highly detailed response. In contrast to previous work based on multi-component systems with embedding models and document retrievers, our architecture inverts the process by relying on prompt engineering and tool selection to fit with the query intent. To validate our approach, we conducted experiments on various question types, including Issues, Pull Requests, Commits, Compound Questions, and General Repository Information, evaluating our target prompts' ability to improve the accuracy of responses from the model. Beyond demonstrating the utility of this architecture to a diverse set of users, our findings suggest that this architecture can make repository data more accessible to technical and non-technical audiences through the production of actionable insights.

</details>


### [28] [Generating Instance Generators in PDDL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06071)
*Nicola J. Müller,Naya Rudolph,Katharina Stein,Jörg Hoffmann,Ayal Taitler,Timo P. Gros*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 用LLM生成PDDL实例生成器程序并强制内置健全性检查，属于带验证约束的LLM程序合成，对代码生成+校验闭环有方法迁移价值。

TL;DR: 针对PDDL无法指定实际实例集、实例生成依赖手工且现有推理/学习方法可扩展性差的问题，提出利用LLM自动生成带健全性检查的实例生成程序，可高效产出大量健全且多样的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL只定义规划领域，无法规定实际实例集合、初始状态与目标条件的合法性约束及感兴趣的实例子集约束，导致实例生成长期依赖针对特定领域和子集手工编写的生成器；已有推理与学习方法又受限于可扩展性。

Method: 利用LLM生成实例生成程序，并通过规定的检查为生成的程序提供内置的健全性保证。

Result: 自动生成的实例生成器能够高效地返回大量健全且多样的实例。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过LLM自动生成实例生成程序并提供健全性检查，可以为PDDL实例生成提供一种替代路径，并高效产出大量健全且多样的实例。

中文摘要：PDDL，作为AI规划社区中事实上的标准语言，旨在指定规划领域：共享相同谓词和动作模式的实例集合。然而，它没有提供任何手段来指定实际的实例集合，即对初始状态和目标条件的合法性约束，以及可能指定我们感兴趣的实例子集的领域子集约束。这带来的一个后果是，实例生成一直是临时的，需要手动编写特定于领域和子集的实例生成器。近期工作已开始通过推理和学习方法来解决这一问题，但这些方法受到可扩展性限制。在此，我们引入一种替代方法，利用LLM生成实例生成程序，并通过规定的检查提供内置的健全性保证。我们表明，这些自动生成的实例生成器能够高效地返回大量健全且多样的实例。

Original abstract: PDDL, the de-facto standard language in the AI Planning community, is designed to specify planning domains: sets of instances that share the same predicates and action schemas. Yet it does not provide any means to specify the actual instance set, i.e., legality constraints on initial states and goal conditions, as well as possibly domain subset constraints specifying an instance subset we are interested in. One consequence of this is that instance generation has always been ad-hoc, with manually written domain- and subset-specific instance generators. Recent work has started to address this, through reasoning and learning methods that however suffer from scalability limitations. Here we introduce an alternative approach, leveraging LLMs to generate instance-generation programs, with built-in soundness guarantees through prescribed checks. We show that these automatically generated instance generators return large numbers of sound and diverse instances efficiently.

</details>


### [29] [SWE-Bench Pro Verified: A Reliable Benchmark for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08149)
*Pujun Zheng,Zixin Shang,Shufan Jiang,Wenhui Tian,Dongsheng Zhu,Zerun Ma,Dingbo Yuan,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 针对 SWE-Bench Pro 的奖励黑客与任务质量问题做防泄漏加固与实例修正，给出更可信的 SE agent 评测基准，对 agent 评测机制高度相关。

TL;DR: SWE-Bench Pro 存在奖励黑客与任务质量不可靠问题；作者提出 SWE-Bench Pro Verified，通过反黑客防护与任务精炼修正，发现部分模型性能被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SWE-Bench Pro 评估不可靠，奖励黑客与任务质量问题会夸大基准性能并掩盖智能体真实编码能力。
