Such representations can model not only what an agent remembers about a user but also how memories are connected, revised, and retrieved to support personalized decisions. However, existing work remains fragmented across personalized agents and generic graph memory frameworks, making it difficult to understand the design space as a whole. This survey develops a lifecycle-oriented view of graph-based personalized memory for LLM agents. We organize existing studies around memory representation, memory evolution, memory retrieval, and memory evaluation. We further compare key design choices, discuss current evaluation practices, and open challenges in building reliable long-term personalized agents. This survey aims to clarify how graph-based memory can support adaptive, controllable, and user-centric LLM agents.

</details>


### [85] [CLAMP: Constrained Decoding for Vision-Language Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08602)
*Tianyi Ma,Parisa Kordjamshidi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: 在解码期用场景证据与符号动作模型做硬约束与HMM前视重加权，保证生成计划可执行，对约束解码提升agent动作合法性有迁移价值。

TL;DR: CLAMP将场景证据与符号动作模型转化为冻结VLM规划器的解码时约束，通过硬掩码和HMM前瞻重加权，提升具身规划的可执行性、物体接地与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM生成的计划可能流畅但不可执行，如引用未观察物体、选择不可用可供性的动作或违反语法与动作约束，因此需要将场景证据和动作约束注入规划过程。

Method: 提出CLAMP多模态约束接地框架：用初始观察限制物体引用，用符号动作模型指定状态转移与目标；解码时硬掩码剔除无效词元，HMM世界状态前瞻模块根据动作前提和目标可达性重加权剩余候选；对未见任务和环境，用冻结VLM采样的无标签延续在测试时适应HMM。

Result: 在VLABench、SafeAgentBench和TaPA上，场景接地约束改善了物体接地和安全性，剩余失败主要来自感知错误或约束规范不一致。

Conclusion: CLAMP能在保留VLM语言先验的同时阻止视觉不支持、不安全或不可行的候选进入计划，为具身规划提供有效的解码时约束接地机制。

中文摘要：具身规划日益依赖视觉语言模型（VLM）将指令和视觉观察转化为可执行动作序列。然而，流畅的计划并不总是可执行的。VLM可能引用视觉上未观察到的物体，选择所需可供性不可用的动作，或违反语法与动作约束。我们提出CLAMP，一个多模态约束接地框架，它将场景证据转化为冻结VLM规划器的解码时约束。CLAMP使用初始观察将物体引用限制为场景支持的物体，同时提供的符号动作模型规定状态转移和目标。在解码过程中，硬掩码消除无效的下一词元候选，而基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的世界状态前瞻模块根据动作前提条件和目标可达性，对剩余可行候选的概率重新加权。这使规划器保留VLM的语言先验，同时防止视觉不支持、不安全或不可行的候选进入计划。对于未见任务和环境，CLAMP在测试时使用从冻结VLM采样的无标签延续来适应HMM。在VLABench、SafeAgentBench和TaPA上的实验表明，场景接地的约束改善了物体接地和安全性，而大多数剩余失败源于感知错误或约束规范不一致。

Original abstract: Embodied planning increasingly relies on vision-language models (VLMs) to translate instructions and visual observations into executable action sequences. However, fluent plans are not always executable. A VLM may refer to objects that are not visually observed, select actions whose required affordances are unavailable, or violate syntax and action constraints. We introduce CLAMP, a multimodal constraint-grounding framework that turns scene evidence into decoding-time constraints for a frozen VLM planner. CLAMP uses the initial observation to restrict object references to those supported by the scene, while a provided symbolic action model specifies state transitions and goals. During decoding, hard masks eliminate invalid next-token candidates, while a Hidden Markov Model (HMM)-based world-state lookahead module reweights the probabilities of the remaining feasible candidates based on action preconditions and goal reachability. This allows the planner to retain the VLM's language prior while preventing visually unsupported, unsafe, or infeasible candidates from entering the plan. For unseen tasks and environments, CLAMP adapts the HMM at test time using label-free continuations sampled from the frozen VLM. Experiments on VLABench, SafeAgentBench, and TaPA show that scene-grounded constraints improve object grounding and safety, while most remaining failures stem from perception errors or misaligned constraint specifications.

</details>


### [86] [GoAnt: Quality-Diversity Multi-Agent Search for Alpha Factor Discovery in Market Microstructure Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08719)
*Stella Zhao,Tommy Sha*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 1 figure, 8 tables, including supplementary appendices

Selection reason: 质量-多样性多agent程序搜索框架，用共享Mental Map与Queen调度分配评测预算，对自动化程序搜索与预算管理有借鉴。

TL;DR: GoAnt 通过质量-多样性多智能体搜索、共享自适应 Mental Map 和 Queen 预算调度器，在固定评估预算下提升阿尔法因子发现的执行稳健性、非冗余性与样本外保留率，并在 2023--2026 年 A 股数据上显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体和多智能体程序搜索容易过拟合在计入执行成本后失效的预测代理，并反复探索冗余因子族，导致执行稳健性和行为多样性不足。

Method: 提出 GoAnt：由互不通信的 Explorer、Exploiter、Connector 工作单元、共享自适应 Mental Map 和紧凑 Queen 调度器组成；Mental Map 按无泄漏执行画像分生态位并保留精英，Queen 依据显式搜索状态摘要重分配评估预算；同时定义与地图无关的有效产出协议。

Result: 在 2023--2026 年真实 A 股微观结构数据上，量价与订单簿设置的质量加权产出分别为 41.8 和 47.6，较最强基线提升 57% 和 97%；锁定种群样本外/样本内质量比为 0.64 和 0.67，静态地图为 0.61 和 0.63。

Conclusion: GoAnt 在匹配预算下提高了高质量非冗余因子的产出和执行稳健性，并显示自适应地图优于静态地图。

中文摘要：自动化阿尔法因子发现是在固定评估预算下，从量价面板和订单簿数据中搜索符号化交易信号。现有的单智能体和多智能体程序搜索系统可能过拟合预测代理，这些代理在计入执行成本后失效，并且会反复探索冗余因子族，从而限制了执行稳健性和行为多样性。我们提出 GoAnt，一个质量-多样性多智能体搜索框架，它将互不通信的 Explorer、Exploiter 和 Connector 工作单元与一个共享的自适应 Mental Map 以及一个紧凑的 Queen 调度器相结合。Mental Map 按无泄漏执行画像组织候选因子，并在每个生态位中保留一个精英；而 Queen 根据显式搜索状态摘要重新分配评估预算。我们还定义了一个与地图无关的有效产出协议，该协议直接从每种方法的评估记录中计数高质量且相互非冗余的因子，为基于档案和无需地图的系统提供同一把尺子。在覆盖 2023--2026 年的真实 A 股微观结构数据上，GoAnt 在量价和订单簿设置中分别达到 41.8 和 47.6 的质量加权产出，在匹配预算下比最强基线提升 57% 和 97%。其锁定种群在样本外保留了样本内质量的 0.64 和 0.67，而静态地图为 0.61 和 0.63。

Original abstract: Automated alpha factor discovery searches symbolic trading signals from price-volume panels and order-book data under a fixed evaluation budget. Existing single- and multi-agent program-search systems can overfit predictive proxies that fail after execution costs and repeatedly explore redundant factor families, limiting execution robustness and behavioral diversity. We introduce GoAnt, a quality-diversity multi-agent search framework that combines non-communicating Explorer, Exploiter and Connector workers with a shared adaptive Mental Map and a compact Queen dispatcher. The Mental Map organizes candidates by leakage-free execution profiles and retains one elite per niche, while the Queen reallocates the evaluation budget from explicit search-state summaries. We also define a map-independent effective-yield protocol that counts high-quality, mutually nonredundant factors directly from each method's evaluation records, giving archive-based and map-free systems the same ruler. On real A-share microstructure data spanning 2023--2026, GoAnt reaches quality-weighted yields of 41.8 and 47.6 in price-volume and order-book settings, improving the strongest baseline by 57% and 97% under matched budgets. Its locked populations retain 0.64 and 0.67 of in-sample quality out of sample, compared with 0.61 and 0.63 for a static map.

</details>


### [87] [Closing the Consistency Gap: Self-Evolving Agents That Learn to Stay on Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08832)
*Evelyn Duesterwald,Benjamin Elder,Lilian Ngweta,Shashanka Ubaru,Malgorzata Zimon*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 识别agent轨迹中不稳定的低一致性步骤并转化为情节记忆与注入式指南，直接对应agent自我改进、经验复用与可靠性控制。

TL;DR: 提出自演化智能体框架，通过一致性分析与指南生成，把低一致性步骤转为情景记忆，在 AppWorld 上将五次全对率提升 +16（同任务）和 +13（相似任务泛化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体平均准确率虽高，但在生产环境中可靠性不足；同一任务重复执行时成功率显著下降，存在一致性差距，这阻碍可信部署。

Method: 框架识别轨迹中不稳定、低一致性步骤，利用一致性分析器定位轨迹易翻转之处及原因，再由指南生成器生成针对性指南，写入记忆并在未来相似任务执行时注入。

Result: 在 AppWorld 基准上，使用 ReAct/GPT-4.1，将五次运行均成功的任务比例在相同任务评估中提升 +16 个百分点，在相似任务泛化中提升 +13 个百分点。

Conclusion: 解决一致性差距是可信 AI 智能体部署的前提；将低一致性步骤诊断并转化为可复用记忆与指南，可有效提升智能体重复执行与泛化任务的一致性。

中文摘要：由大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的智能体平均而言可以很准确，但在生产环境中却可能不可靠，这种差异已被观察到，但很大程度上仍未得到解决。当同一任务被给予五次时，在 AppWorld 基准上使用 GPT-4.1 的 ReAct 智能体仅在 53% 的情况下五次运行全部成功，尽管其单次运行的平均通过率为 77%。我们将这 24 个百分点的差距称为一致性差距（consistency gap），并认为解决该差距是可信赖 AI 智能体部署的前提条件。我们提出了一种自演化智能体框架，通过识别智能体轨迹中不稳定的、低一致性的步骤，并将其转化为智能体在未来运行中可以借鉴的情景记忆，来缩小这一差距。其核心是一个一致性分析器（Consistency Analyzer），用于精确定位轨迹在多次执行中可能发生翻转的位置及原因；以及一个指南生成器（Guideline Generator），用于将诊断结果转化为针对性指南，写入记忆，并在未来执行相似任务时注入智能体。在 AppWorld 上使用 ReAct/GPT-4.1 时，我们的框架将五次运行全部成功的任务比例在相同任务评估上提高了 +16 个百分点，在相似任务泛化上提高了 +13 个百分点。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents can be accurate on average yet unreliable in production, a discrepancy that has been observed but remains largely unaddressed. When given the same task five times, a ReAct agent on the AppWorld benchmark using GPT-4.1 succeeds in all five runs only 53% of the time, even though its per-run pass rate averages 77%. We call this 24-point shortfall the consistency gap, and we argue that addressing it is a precondition for trustworthy AI agent deployment. We present a self-evolving agent framework that reduces this gap by identifying unstable, low-consistency steps in agent trajectories and converting them into episodic memory the agent can draw on in future runs. At its core is a Consistency Analyzer that pinpoints where and why a trajectory is likely to flip across executions, and a Guideline Generator that converts the diagnosis into targeted guidelines, committed to memory and injected into future agent executions on similar tasks. On AppWorld with ReAct/GPT-4.1, our framework raises the fraction of tasks that succeed in all five runs by +16 points on same-task evaluation and +13 points on similar-task generalization.

</details>


### [88] [SkillAdam: Stable and Efficient Skill Evolution for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08944)
*Gaoyuan Li,Meihao Fan,Yizhe Liu,Shaolei Zhang,Ju Fan,Siyi Wang,Jiaheng Hou,Xudong Weng,Honghan Tian,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables

Selection reason: SkillAdam以Adam式一阶/二阶动量类比实现技能文档的稳定高效自我进化，与SemOpt式的迭代优化agent能力高度同源。

TL;DR: SkillAdam 将 Adam 优化思想引入离散技能文档自演化：用优化记忆稳定更新方向，用波动驱动编辑预算自适应控制更新幅度，在多个基准上以更少迭代和更低成本取得更优技能与更稳定优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家编写技能成本高且难以扩展；现有技能自演化方法依赖启发式更新，导致优化不稳定、迭代效率低。需要同时解决方向稳定性与更新自适应问题。

Method: 提出 SkillAdam，面向离散且不可微的技能文档：以 Adam 一阶矩的功能类比构建优化记忆，记录已识别问题与先前尝试结果以稳定更新方向；以二阶矩的功能类比构建波动性驱动编辑预算，依据近期案例级改进的历史加权变化自适应控制更新幅度。

Result: 在涵盖短时程与长时程任务的七个基准上达到最先进性能，优化动态更稳定；相比先前方法，以显著更少优化迭代次数和更低成本获得更强技能。

Conclusion: SkillAdam 表明，借鉴 Adam 的稳定化与自适应机制可有效提升技能自演化的稳定性、迭代效率与最终技能质量。

中文摘要：智能体技能提供了一种轻量级方式，为冻结的语言模型智能体配备领域知识与流程性指导，但获得高质量技能仍然成本高昂且难以扩展。专家编写的技能需要大量人工努力。近期的技能自演化方法自动化了一个迭代循环，利用执行反馈修正技能，但其启发式更新策略常导致优化不稳定和迭代效率低。我们识别出实现稳定且高效技能自演化的两个挑战。方向稳定性要求有效修正得以累积，而不是被迭代局部反馈覆盖。更新自适应要求每次修订的范围反映近期案例级改进的一致性。我们引入 SkillAdam，一个受 Adam 启发的框架，用于优化离散且不可微的技能文档。作为 Adam 一阶矩的功能类比，优化记忆记录已识别的问题以及先前解决尝试的结果，以稳定更新方向。作为 Adam 二阶矩的功能类比，波动性驱动的编辑预算跟踪近期案例级改进的历史加权变化，并自适应控制更新幅度。在跨越短时程与长时程任务的七个基准上，SkillAdam 取得了最先进的性能，并具有更稳定的优化动态。与先前方法相比，它还能以显著更少的优化迭代次数和更低成本获得更强的技能。代码仓库：https://github.com/ruc-datalab/SkillAdam

Original abstract: Agent skills provide a lightweight way to equip frozen language-model agents with domain knowledge and procedural guidance, yet obtaining high-quality skills remains costly and difficult to scale. Expert-written skills require substantial human effort. Recent skill self-evolution methods automate an iterative loop that uses execution feedback to revise skills, but their heuristic update strategies often yield unstable optimization and low iteration efficiency. We identify two challenges in realizing stable and efficient skill self-evolution. Direction Stability requires effective corrections to accumulate rather than be overwritten by iteration-local feedback. Update Adaptivity requires the scope of each revision to reflect the consistency of recent case-level improvements. We introduce SkillAdam, an Adam-inspired framework for optimizing discrete and non-differentiable skill documents. As a functional analogue of Adam's first moment, an optimization memory records identified problems and the outcomes of prior solution attempts to stabilize the update direction. As a functional analogue of Adam's second moment, a volatility-driven edit budget tracks the history-weighted variation of recent case-level improvements and adaptively controls the update magnitude. Across seven benchmarks that span short- and long-horizon tasks, SkillAdam achieves state-of-the-art performance with more stable optimization dynamics. It also obtains stronger skills with substantially fewer optimization iterations and lower cost than prior methods. Code repository: https://github.com/ruc-datalab/SkillAdam

</details>


### [89] [PlannerForge: LLM Agents for Scenario-Based Testing of Motion Planners in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08965)
*Yuan Gao,Sebastian Müller,Mattia Piccinini,Marc Kaufeld,Yuchen Zhang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Qunying Song,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference). 35 pages including appendix

Selection reason: 用统一LLM agent流水线覆盖场景生成、检索、修改、执行与评估全流程，并做模块路由与成本调优，对多阶段agentic工作流设计有参考。

TL;DR: PlannerForge是一个统一LLM智能体框架，覆盖并扩展ADS基于场景测试全流程，在多项任务上达0.88–1.00，端到端保留大部分种子查询，且经成本调优显著提升规划成功率并降低碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全验证关键，现有基于场景测试流程碎片化、工具间交互不足；LLM智能体虽在ADS子系统中显示潜力，但尚无统一框架覆盖完整测试流水线。

Method: 提出PlannerForge，扩展从场景生成到ADS评估的所有基于场景测试阶段，并新增ADS增强与ADS基准测试；用10个现成LLM在生成、选择、修改、模块路由、规划器测试、增强6类任务及5种提示条件下评测，并端到端串联模块、进行成本调优。

Result: 最佳单任务得分0.88–1.00；开源20–35B后端多数任务匹配商业API，Qwen3.6:35B在5项中3项匹配；端到端保留83%/78%种子查询（商业/开源）；自然语言生成200个中193 vs 144可执行，实现92–96%请求属性；排名第1选择92.0% vs BM25 67.5%；物理有效编辑>=94% vs 31%；N=400时规划成功率50.4%→70.2%，碰撞19.0%→8.4%，无需领域微调。

Conclusion: PlannerForge表明统一LLM智能体框架可有效支撑ADS基于场景测试全流程，开源模型具备竞争力，并能在不做领域特定微调下通过成本调优改善规划性能与安全性。

中文摘要：确保自动驾驶的安全是一项关键挑战。基于场景的测试是用于验证自动驾驶系统（ADS）的系统化过程，但它仍是一种碎片化的模块化流水线，其中场景生成、检索、修改、ADS执行和结果分析由相互独立且交互很少的工具完成。大语言模型（LLM）智能体已在感知、规划和控制等ADS子系统中展现出前景。然而，此前没有工作用统一的LLM智能体框架覆盖ADS的整个基于场景的测试流水线。我们提出PlannerForge，一种LLM智能体框架，它扩展了所有基于场景的测试阶段（从场景生成到ADS评估），并增加了两个进一步的LLM增强阶段：ADS增强和ADS基准测试。我们在5种提示条件下，用10个现成LLM在全部任务（生成、选择、修改、模块路由、规划器测试和增强）上评估PlannerForge。最佳单任务得分范围为0.88至1.00，开源20–35B后端在大多数任务上匹配商业API。如Qwen3.6:35B等开源模型在五项任务中的三项上匹配商业API。端到端串联各模块保留了83%/78%的种子查询（商业/开源）。它在自然语言生成上优于Scenario Factory 2.0（Finkeldei et al., 2025）（200个中193个 vs. 144个可执行），并实现92–96%的请求城市、道路和车辆属性。它在排名第1选择上优于BM25（Robertson and Zaragoza, 2009）（92.0% vs. 67.5%），并在物理有效编辑上优于From-Words-to-Collisions（Gao et al., 2025）（>=94% vs. 31%）。在N=400时，成本调优将规划器成功率从50.4%提升至70.2%，并将碰撞从19.0%降至8.4%，且无需领域特定微调。

Original abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous driving is a critical challenge. Scenario-based testing is a systematic process used to validate Autonomous Driving Systems (ADSs), but it remains a fragmented modular pipeline in which scenario generation, retrieval, modification, ADS execution, and results analysis are performed by separate tools with little interaction. Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise across ADS sub-systems such as perception, planning, and control. However, no prior work covers the whole scenario-based testing pipeline for ADSs with a unified LLM-agent framework. We present PlannerForge, an LLM-agent framework that extends all scenario-based testing stages (from Scenario Generation to ADS Assessment) and adds two further LLM-enhanced stages: ADS Enhancement and ADS Benchmarking. We evaluate PlannerForge with 10 off-the-shelf LLMs across all tasks (Generation, Selection, Modification, Module Routing, Planner Testing, and Enhancement) under 5 prompt conditions. Best-per-task scores range from 0.88 to 1.00, and open-source 20-35B backends match commercial APIs on most tasks. Open-source models such as Qwen3.6:35B match commercial APIs on three of the five tasks. Chaining the modules end-to-end retains 83% / 78% of seed queries (commercial / open). It outperforms Scenario Factory 2.0 (Finkeldei et al., 2025) on natural-language generation (193 vs. 144 executable of 200) and realises 92-96% of requested city, road and vehicle attributes. It outperforms BM25 (Robertson and Zaragoza, 2009) at rank 1 selection (92.0% vs. 67.5%) and From-Words-to-Collisions (Gao et al., 2025) on physically valid edits (>=94% vs. 31%). At N=400, cost-tuning lifts planner success from 50.4% to 70.2% and cuts collisions from 19.0% to 8.4%, without domain-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [90] [SAEScientist-Bench: Can AI Agents Conduct Autonomous SAE Interpretability Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09113)
*Yuqiao Tan,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Preprint. Work in Progress

Selection reason: 提出 AI agent 自主进行 SAE 可解释性研究的闭环基准，含工具导航、假设设计与实验验证，对自主研究型 agent 评测有方法参考。

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAEScientist-Bench，评估 AI 智能体能否使用稀疏自编码器工具自主发现可解释特征；前沿智能体具备一定发现能力，但整体仍落后于专家，尤其在因果引导方面，且常误读实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进研究多聚焦训练流程自动化，但可靠自主开发还需要事后监控与审计，以理解模型学习内容并保障安全对齐。机制可解释性工具，尤其是稀疏自编码器（SAEs），可分离可解释特征，因此有必要评估 AI 智能体能否自主利用这些工具进行机制性发现。

Method: 构建 SAEScientist-Bench：给定目标概念，智能体设计对比探针，并在 Gemma-2-9B-IT 的 Gemma Scope 字典中导航 131K+ 个特征以发现最优特征；以 Neuronpedia 上精选专家参考特征为基准，从激活排名、对比文本上的概念选择性、因果引导三个维度评估。实验涵盖 10 种智能体配置和 20 个任务。

Result: 前沿智能体展现出真实发现能力，并在不同评估维度上各有领先，但整体仍明显落后于专家基线；在将目标概念与对比对照区分方面接近专家水平，在因果生成引导方面差距较大。进一步分析显示，智能体可设计对比排除虚假候选，但频繁误读实验测量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将实验性模型理解确立为闭环自主 AI 研发的可测量能力，表明当前智能体在自主机制性发现上仍有显著提升空间，尤其是在因果引导和实验测量解读方面。代码已开源。

中文摘要：尽管关于递归自我改进（RSI）的研究主要集中于自动化模型训练流程，但可靠的自主开发还需要一个缺失的支柱：事后监控与审计，以理解模型学到了什么并确保安全对齐。机制可解释性工具对于弥合这一差距至关重要，其中稀疏自编码器（SAEs）作为基石，通过分离可解释特征来支持模型检查与引导。本文中，我们引入 SAEScientist-Bench，以评估 AI 智能体能否像科学家一样利用 SAE 工具进行自主机制性发现。给定一个目标概念，智能体设计对比探针，并在 Gemma-2-9B-IT 的 Gemma Scope 字典中导航 131K+ 个特征，以发现最优特征，并依据锚定在 Neuronpedia 上的精选专家参考特征，在激活排名、对比文本上的概念选择性以及因果引导方面进行评估。在 10 种智能体配置和 20 个任务中，前沿智能体展现出真正的发现能力，并在不同评估维度上领先，但仍明显落后于专家基线：在将目标概念与对比对照区分开方面接近专家水平，而在因果生成引导方面则大幅落后。进一步分析表明，尽管智能体能够设计对比以排除虚假候选，但它们经常误读实验测量结果。这些结果确立了实验性模型理解作为闭环自主 AI 研发的一项可测量能力。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Trae1ounG/SAEScientist 获取。

Original abstract: While research on recursive self-improvement (RSI) has predominantly automated model training pipelines, reliable autonomous development demands a missing pillar: post-hoc monitoring and auditing to understand what models learn and ensure safe alignment. Mechanistic interpretability tools are essential to bridge this gap, among which Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) serve as a cornerstone by isolating interpretable features for model inspection and steering. In this paper, we introduce SAEScientist-Bench to evaluate whether AI agents can act as scientists utilizing SAE tools for autonomous mechanistic discovery. Given a target concept, an agent designs contrastive probes and navigates a Gemma Scope dictionary of 131K+ features in Gemma-2-9B-IT to discover the optimal feature, evaluated against curated expert reference features anchored on Neuronpedia across activation rank, concept selectivity on contrastive texts, and causal steering. Across 10 agent configurations and 20 tasks, frontier agents demonstrate genuine discovery capabilities and lead different evaluation dimensions, but remain well behind the expert baseline, approaching expert levels on separating target concepts from contrastive controls while lagging substantially in causal generation steering. Further analysis reveals that although agents can design contrasts to rule out spurious candidates, they frequently misinterpret experimental measurements. These results establish experimental model understanding as a measurable capability for closed-loop autonomous AI R&D. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trae1ounG/SAEScientist.

</details>


### [91] [Co-Evolving Harnesses and Models: On-Policy Correction Helps Weaker Models Catch Up Where Imitation Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09134)
*Zhou Yu,Bin Bi,Shiva Kumar Pentyala,Shubham Mehrotra,Sougata Chaudhuri,Shilpa Bhagavath,Zeyuan Chen,Ran Xu,Phil Mui,James Zhu,Sitaram Asur*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究 agent harness 自动化演化与弱模型微调的协同，揭示模仿破坏 model-harness fit 并提出 on-policy 专家纠错，对 agent harness/工作流自我改进高度相关。

TL;DR: 运行框架演化与权重更新存在冲突：在演化后的运行框架下直接模仿专家完整轨迹会损害小模型性能；通过元级 MLE 智能体自动化的在策略专家纠正，仅重写失败轮次，可兼容地结合框架演化与轻量微调，在七个企业任务上取得收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究运行框架演化与轻量微调应如何结合；专家可更有效使用弱模型演化出的框架，但直接模仿专家轨迹可能破坏模型—运行框架契合。

Method: 在七个企业智能体任务上，先用弱模型演化运行框架；比较专家使用效果；分析在演化与未演化框架下用专家完整轨迹训练弱模型的影响；提出在策略专家纠正流水线，由元级 MLE 智能体自动定位弱模型自身 rollout 中的失败轮次，并让专家仅重写该轮次。

Result: 在 Qwen3-Coder 和 Gemma 4 上，用专家完整轨迹训练弱模型使全部七个任务回退 4 到 30 分；模仿能迁移知识并增加脚手架使用，却使弱模型采用无法执行的专家规划策略，破坏模型—运行框架契合。所提方法保留规划风格并融合框架演化与模型适配收益。

Conclusion: 识别并解决运行框架更新与权重更新之间的冲突，给出兼容性保持的经济型协同演化配方，适用于领域特定企业任务。

中文摘要：智能体运行框架（围绕模型的系统提示、工具集、执行钩子与上下文管理脚手架）是决定智能体任务成功与否的关键因素。自动化运行框架演化能够使更小的模型以远低于前沿模型的成本，在领域特定任务上表现良好。由于运行框架和模型权重共同塑造行为，我们提出问题：运行框架演化与轻量微调应如何结合。在七个企业级智能体任务上，我们首先用较弱模型演化出一个运行框架，随后发现更强的专家模型往往能更有效地使用该框架，这表明专家监督可能弥合剩余差距。然而，在演化后的运行框架下，用专家的完整轨迹训练较弱模型却适得其反：在 Qwen3-Coder 和 Gemma 4 上，所有七个任务性能回退 4 到 30 分，尽管同样的过程在未演化的运行框架下确有帮助。我们的分析表明，模仿迁移了知识并增加了脚手架使用，但破坏了模型—运行框架契合度：较弱模型采用了专家的规划策略，却不具备执行该策略的能力，并且不再匹配围绕其原生规划风格演化出的运行框架。因此，我们开发了一条在策略（on-policy）专家纠正流水线，由元级 MLE 智能体自动化，该流水线在较弱模型自身的 rollout 中定位失败的轮次，并请专家仅重写该轮次。这保留了模型的规划风格，并融合了运行框架演化与模型适配的收益。我们的结果识别并解决了一处运行框架更新与权重更新之间的冲突来源，为领域特定企业任务上的经济型协同演化提供了保持兼容性的配方。

Original abstract: Agent harnesses (the system prompt, tool set, execution hooks, and context-management scaffolding around a model) are a critical determinant of agentic task success. Automated harness evolution can enable smaller models to perform well on domain-specific tasks at a fraction of frontier-model cost. Since both the harness and model weights shape behavior, we ask how harness evolution and lightweight fine-tuning should be combined. Across seven enterprise agent tasks, we first evolve a harness with the weaker model, then find that a stronger expert often uses it more effectively, suggesting expert supervision could close the remaining gap. However, training the weaker model on the expert's complete trajectories under the evolved harness backfires: performance regresses on all seven tasks by 4 to 30 points across Qwen3-Coder and Gemma 4, even though the same procedure helps under the unevolved harness. Our analysis shows that imitation transfers knowledge and increases scaffold usage, but disrupts model-harness fit: the weaker model adopts the expert's planning strategy without the competence to execute it and no longer matches the harness evolved around its native planning style. We therefore develop an on-policy expert-correction pipeline, automated by a meta-level MLE agent, that localizes the failing turn in the weaker model's own rollout and asks the expert to rewrite only that turn. This preserves the model's planning style and combines the gains of harness evolution and model adaptation. Our results identify and resolve a source of contention between harness and weight updates, yielding a compatibility-preserving recipe for economical co-evolution on domain-specific enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Procedural Graphs: Self-Evolving Execution Structures for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09153)
*Yuxing Lu,Yicheng Chen,Shanchan Wu,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 36 pages including references and appendices, 6 figures, 11 tables

Selection reason: 提出 Procedural Graph 用程序性知识图谱引导 LLM agent 长程执行并自演化修复，涉及规划、轨迹复用与验证，可迁移到代码优化 agent。

TL;DR: 提出程序图（Procedural Graph），用（过程，关系，过程）三元组显式组织程序性知识；决策时定位活动节点并将子图转为步骤级情境引导，偏置而不强制动作。图通过LLM精炼器对比成败轨迹自演化，经留出验证提交编辑并保留被拒编辑防重复。多数据集、任务类型和LLM上一致优于记忆基线，自演化无需人工工程进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程工具使用智能体多依赖累积历史进行无约束生成来选择动作，程序性知识隐式；轨迹变长后易迷失目标、工具调用失序、重复无效动作。需要显式组织“该做什么、按什么顺序、在何种条件下做”的程序知识。

Method: 将程序性知识建模为（过程，关系，过程）三元组的程序图。每个决策步定位智能体活动节点，用引导模型把周围子图转化为步骤级情境引导，偏置求解器下一动作但不强制。图自演化：LLM精炼器对比失败与成功轨迹，编辑图拓扑与属性；依据留出验证提交保持或提升性能的编辑，保留被拒编辑以抑制重复。可从最小骨架构建，也可修复有缺陷专家先验。

Result: 在多个数据集、任务类型和LLM上，程序图较基于记忆的基线取得一致增益；自演化无需人工工程进一步提升性能。从最小骨架可匹配或超越手工设计图，并能修复有缺陷的专家先验。

Conclusion: 程序图通过显式、自演化的程序性知识引导长时程智能体决策，减少目标迷失、工具顺序错误和无效重复，提供可扩展且无需人工工程的性能提升。

中文摘要：大型语言模型正日益被部署为能够在长时程上进行规划并通过外部工具行动的智能体。大多数智能体通过在不断累积的历史上进行无约束生成来选择动作，从而使“做什么、以什么顺序做以及在什么条件下做”的程序性知识保持隐式。随着轨迹变长，智能体可能失去对目标的跟踪、不按顺序调用工具，并重复无效动作。我们引入程序图（Procedural Graph）：正如知识图谱将事实性知识组织为用于“是什么”问题的（实体，关系，实体）三元组，程序图将程序性知识组织为用于“该做什么”问题的（过程，关系，过程）三元组。在每个决策步骤，该框架定位智能体的活动节点，并由一个引导模型将周围子图转化为步骤级情境引导，从而偏向求解器的下一动作而不强制规定它。该图是自演化的：一个LLM精炼器将失败轨迹与成功轨迹进行对比，并编辑图的拓扑和属性，提交那些能够保持或提升留出验证性能的编辑，同时保留被拒绝的编辑以抑制重复。从最小骨架开始，该循环构建出的图能够匹配或超越手工设计的图。它还可以修复有缺陷的专家先验。在多个数据集、任务类型和LLM上，程序图相较基于记忆的基线带来一致的增益，而自演化无需人工工程即可进一步提升性能。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents that plan over long horizons and act through external tools. Most agents select actions through unconstrained generation over an accumulating history, leaving implicit the procedural knowledge of what to do, in what order, and under which conditions. As trajectories lengthen, agents can lose track of their objectives, invoke tools out of order, and repeat unproductive actions. We introduce the Procedural Graph: just as a knowledge graph organizes factual knowledge into (entity, relation, entity) triplets for what-is questions, a Procedural Graph organizes procedural knowledge into (procedure, relation, procedure) triplets for what-to-do questions. At each decision step, the framework localizes the agent's active node, and a guidance model translates the surrounding subgraph into step-level situational guidance that biases the solver's next action without dictating it. The graph is self-evolving: an LLM refiner contrasts failed trajectories with successful ones and edits the graph's topology and attributes, committing edits that preserve or improve held-out validation performance while retaining rejected ones to discourage repetition. Starting from a minimal skeleton, the loop builds graphs that match or surpass hand-designed ones. It can also repair a flawed expert prior. Across multiple datasets, task types, and LLMs, the Procedural Graph delivers consistent gains over memory-based baselines, and self-evolution further improves performance without manual engineering.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Memory as Infrastructure: Reliability Engineering for Persistent Agent Memory in Months-Long LLM-Assisted Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05510)
*Mike Helwig*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 长期运行编码agent的持久记忆基础设施与可靠性工程，含健康门、遥测、防复现存储和消融协议，直接可迁移到代码agent记忆与长程执行。

TL;DR: 报告一个持续数月的 LLM 编码代理项目级运行经验，介绍开源记忆与连续性基础设施 SIx Harness，涵盖长期记忆、精度门控注入、反重复存储、压缩存活约定及记忆子系统可靠性工程，并给出仪器化运行数据、设计原则、局限与可复现消融协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码代理正从任务规模走向项目规模，长期辅助会话需跨反复上下文压缩维持巨型代码库的连贯性；现有运行实践缺少对代理记忆子系统本身的可靠性工程。

Method: 基于单一连续 Claude Code 会话线下的研究项目，驱动 633,000 行代码库，并持续仪器化监测记忆子系统；SIx Harness 整合本地 SQLite 混合词法-向量长期记忆、精度门控上下文注入、决策与死胡同反重复存储、压缩存活约定、会话启动健康门、心跳遥测与告警疲劳预算；发布带标签的预注册消融协议和 MIT 许可工具包。

Result: 78,933 次 hook 调用；85 次记录故障且无静默，其中 84 次在前三周、此后 1 次、最后 20 天为 0；十天精度仪器在完整分母上零误报；三起生产事件从仪器读数追溯到结构性修复。

Conclusion: 从运行记录中提炼七条设计原则；明确局限为 N=1、无对照组、自我报告；发布预注册消融协议与 MIT 许可工具包，供任何团队复现和运行。

中文摘要：LLM 编码代理正在从任务规模跨越到项目规模：单一辅助性工作持续数月，经历反复的上下文压缩，处理远超任何上下文窗口的代码库。我们报告来自一个此类工作的运行经验（一个研究项目，自2026年1月起处于单一连续的 Claude Code 会话线之下，驱动一个 633,000 行的代码库，其记忆子系统自2026年7月起持续被仪器化监测），并描述 SIx Harness，这一开源记忆与连续性基础设施，它是在保持该项目连贯一致的过程中产生的。该 harness 结合了按项目的长期记忆（基于 SQLite 的混合词法-向量检索，完全本地）、精度门控的上下文注入、针对决策与死胡同的反重复存储、为在压缩后存活而工程化的约定，以及我们认为在运行实践中缺失的部分，即记忆子系统本身的可靠性工程：具有可区分故障模式的会话启动健康门、被设计成任何已枚举故障模式都无法未被记录地通过的心跳遥测，以及借鉴 SRE 实践的告警疲劳预算。我们呈现我们认为的首个在生产开发使用中对持久性代理记忆子系统进行数月规模仪器化监测的运行记录：78,933 次 hook 调用；85 次记录在案的故障，没有一次是静默的：84 次发生在该子系统的前三周，此后一次，最后 20 天没有；一个注入层，其十天精度仪器显示在完整分母上零误报；以及三起生产事件，从仪器读数追溯到结构性修复。从该记录中，我们提炼出七条设计原则，直白陈述我们的局限（N=1，无对照组，自我报告），并发布一个带标签的预注册消融协议，任何团队都可以用已发布的 MIT 许可工具包来运行。

Original abstract: LLM coding agents are crossing from task-scale to project-scale: single assisted efforts that run for months, across repeated context compactions, on codebases far larger than any context window. We report operational experience from one such effort (a research project under a single continuous Claude Code session line since January 2026, driving a 633,000-line codebase, with its memory subsystem continuously instrumented since July 2026) and describe SIx Harness, the open-source memory and continuity infrastructure that emerged from keeping it coherent.
  The harness combines per-project long-term memory (hybrid lexical-vector retrieval over SQLite, fully local), precision-gated context injection, anti-recurrence stores for decisions and dead-ends, conventions engineered to survive compaction, and, the part we argue is missing from operational practice, reliability engineering for the memory subsystem itself: a session-start health gate with discriminated failure modes, heartbeat telemetry designed so that no enumerated failure mode can pass unrecorded, and alert-fatigue budgeting borrowed from SRE practice.
  We present what we believe is the first months-scale instrumented operational record of a persistent agent-memory subsystem in production development use: 78,933 hook invocations; 85 recorded failures, none silent: 84 in the subsystem's first three weeks, one since, none in the final 20 days; an injection layer whose ten-day precision instrument shows zero false fires against an intact denominator; and three production incidents traced from instrument reading to structural fix. From the record we distill seven design principles, state our limitations plainly (N=1, no control arm, self-reported), and publish a tagged pre-registered ablation protocol that any team can run with the released MIT-licensed kit.

</details>


### [94] [Grounded Skill Synthesis from Code at Scale for Agentic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05571)
*Yongqi Tong,Pan Wang,Hang Wang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang,Jiang-Ming Yang,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 从大规模代码库自动合成可验证的agent技能库，含重构验证与多基准评测，直接相关于agent技能获取、检索与迁移。

TL;DR: Code2Skill 从代码仓库自动提取并验证实现锚定的技能，构建 CodeSkillBank；在72项评估中平均提升11.7%，并优于轨迹技能库，且可扩展至AI生成代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用技能为智能体提供可迁移的程序性知识。现有方法存在局限：基于轨迹的合成依赖特定环境交互，文档推导的技能可能缺乏可执行证据与验证；源代码无需先验经验且能提供可执行依据。

Method: 提出 Code2Skill 全自动流水线：将选定代码单元转化为实现锚定的原子操作、复合工作流与重复模式记录，并用源码主体盲重建和源码感知比较验证；应用于19,769个GitHub仓库，生成含1,006,822条接受记录及工作流、边界、来源、源证据元数据的 CodeSkillBank。

Result: 72项协议匹配评估（9种模型设置、8个基准）中，检索 CodeSkillBank 技能的模型平均提升11.7%，57例优于匹配基线；在7个共享基准上优于轨迹推导技能库；AI生成代码技能通过率93.50%，人工代码93.00%。

Conclusion: Code2Skill 将仓库中的程序性知识转化为有依据、可验证、可迁移的智能体技能。

中文摘要：可复用技能为智能体提供可迁移的程序性知识，因此可扩展地获取技能对于让智能体超越先前经验至关重要。现有方法面临两个限制：基于轨迹的合成需要与特定环境进行交互，而从文档推导的技能可能缺乏可执行证据与验证。源代码提供了一条互补路径：它不需要智能体具备先验经验，却提供可执行证据来为抽象奠定基础。我们提出 Code2Skill，这是一个全自动流水线，将选定的代码单元转化为以实现为锚定的记录，涵盖原子操作、复合工作流和反复出现的模式，然后通过源码主体盲重建和源码感知比较来验证每条记录。将其应用于19,769个流行且积极维护的GitHub仓库后，Code2Skill 生成了 CodeSkillBank，这是一个包含1,006,822条被接受记录的有依据技能库，带有工作流、边界、来源和源证据元数据。在覆盖九种模型设置和八个基准的72项协议匹配评估中，使用检索到的 CodeSkillBank 技能增强的模型平均比匹配基线提升11.7%，并在57个案例中优于它们。在统一下游接口下，Code2Skill 在所有七个共享基准上也优于基于轨迹推导的技能库，表明从仓库推导的技能可以在智能体积累足够交互经验之前提供有用的程序性知识。从经过测试的AI生成代码合成的技能达到93.50%的通过率，而人工编写代码为93.00%，这提供了初步证据，表明该流水线可以随着AI生成软件数量的增长而扩展。总体而言，Code2Skill 将嵌入仓库中的程序性知识转化为有依据、可验证且可迁移的智能体技能。

Original abstract: Reusable skills give agents transferable procedural knowledge, making scalable acquisition essential for extending agents beyond prior experience. Existing methods face two limitations: trajectory-based synthesis requires interactions with specific environments, while document-derived skills may lack executable evidence and verification. Source code offers a complementary path: it requires no prior agent experience yet provides executable evidence for grounding abstractions. We present Code2Skill, a fully automated pipeline that transforms selected code units into implementation-anchored records of atomic operations, composite workflows, and recurring patterns, then verifies each record through source-body-blind reconstruction and source-aware comparison. Applied to 19,769 popular, actively maintained GitHub repositories, Code2Skill produces CodeSkillBank, a grounded bank of 1,006,822 accepted records with workflow, boundary, provenance, and source-evidence metadata. Across 72 protocol-matched evaluations covering nine model settings and eight benchmarks, models augmented with retrieved CodeSkillBank skills improve by 11.7% on average over matched baselines and outperform them in 57 cases. Under a unified downstream interface, Code2Skill also outperforms trajectory-derived skill banks on all seven shared benchmarks, showing that repository-derived skills can provide useful procedural knowledge before agents accumulate sufficient interaction experience. Skills synthesized from tested AI-generated code achieve a 93.50% pass rate, compared with 93.00% for human-written code, providing initial evidence that the pipeline can expand with the growing volume of AI-generated software. Overall, Code2Skill transforms procedural knowledge embedded in repositories into grounded, verifiable, and transferable agent skills.

</details>


### [95] [The Impact of GenAI on the Future of Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05667)
*Travis Breaux,Anmol Singhal*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 综述LLM对需求工程及通用SE agent的影响，涉及prompt编程与半自治SE流程，对agent规划与需求表达有参考价值。

TL;DR: 综述了AI用于需求工程的研究现状、LLM进展、提示编程和通用软件工程智能体，并展望RE未来两个发展方向，以帮助RE研究者确定优先事项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM等AI进展降低了实现成本，使软件工程重心从编写代码转向需求相关活动，因此需要梳理RE研究的变革与未来方向。

Method: 文献综述：先调查AI用于需求工程的研究现状，再回顾LLM进展，并综述提示编程与通用软件工程智能体两个后续研究方向。

Result: 实现成本降低推动工程投入转向表达、整理、验证和评估需求；提示编程和通用软件工程智能体成为重要研究方向；RE未来可从交互方式和体验方式两个维度展开。

Conclusion: 该文为需求工程研究者应对AI与LLM带来的变革、选择未来研究优先事项提供参考。

中文摘要：人工智能（AI）的近期进展，尤其是大语言模型（LLM），正在通过增加对领域知识的获取以及为软件工程（SE）提供自动化支持，改变我们设计和构建系统的方式。随着借助通用软件工程智能体使实现成本降低，工程投入正从编写正确代码转向表达、整理、验证和评估需求。本文在回顾LLM进展之前，首先调查了面向需求工程（RE）研究的人工智能发展现状，直至这一变革发生。我们随后调查两个研究方向：提示编程，即以软件工程术语将LLM指令视为程序；以及通用软件工程智能体，即结合多项LLM进展，产生完成软件工程任务的半自主流程。最后，我们从两个维度探索需求工程的未来：一是通过软件工程过程改变我们与需求交互方式的事项，二是更广泛地改变软件开发人员和利益相关者（包括最终用户）体验需求方式的事项。本文旨在为需求工程研究人员在确定未来研究优先事项时如何应对这一变革提供信息。

Original abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), particularly large language models (LLMs), are transforming how we design and build systems by increasing access to domain knowledge and by providing automation support to software engineering (SE). As implementation becomes less expensive through generalist SE agents, engineering effort shifts away from writing correct code and toward expressing, curating, verifying, and evaluating requirements. In this paper, we survey the state of the art in AI for requirements engineering (RE) research leading up to the transformation, before reviewing advances in LLMs. We survey two subsequent research areas: prompt programming, which treats LLM instructions as a program in SE vernacular, and generalist SE agents, which combine multiple LLM advances to yield semi-autonomous processes that complete SE tasks. Finally, we explore the future of requirements engineering along two axes: matters changing how we interact with requirements through the SE process, and matters changing how requirements are experienced by software developers and stakeholders more broadly, including end-users. This article aims to inform how RE researchers can navigate this transformation in the selection of future research priorities.

</details>


### [96] [Regret Dominates Surprise: Design-Time Requirements Engineering for Agentic-AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05692)
*Nuwayyir Almohammadi,Rami Bahsoon,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 12 pages, 8 figures, preprint

Selection reason: 提出基于后悔支配与惊奇信号的agent安全自主机制，可迁移为代码agent的风险触发与升级/反思决策机制。

TL;DR: 提出基于 GORE 的 MS-RGR 机制，以认知惊讶和认知后悔信号解决智能体自主性三难问题；仿真与回顾性评估表明其可近乎消除静默失败、加速风险检测，但需模型级安全训练配合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体 AI 需求工程在认知不确定性下缺乏评估、规约和操作化安全自主性的系统机制，GORE 等主流框架不足以应对。

Method: 在 GORE 基础上建模与仿真安全自主性；提出 MS-RGR，使用认知惊讶（新颖性检测）和认知后悔（评估性风险）两信号，在常规自主、反思推理与人类升级间抉择；在老年照护监测和自动驾驶实例化；通过 100 个随机种子的随机仿真和基于 208 个 AGENTHARM 场景、七个 LLM 执行轨迹的 DRI 门控回顾性代理实例化进行评估。

Result: 100 个随机种子仿真显示静默失败降至近零，风险检测约快 17.5 倍，并可通过 LTL 安全属性形式追溯；回顾性代理实例化显示 DRI 门控仅对基线安全性强的模型改善有害任务拒绝（门控前拒绝率超过 80%，如 84.1% 到 90.9%）。

Conclusion: MS-RGR 可放大而非替代模型级安全训练，是智能体 AI 需求工程中设计时安全约束的初步可行性证据；同时讨论了有效性威胁。

中文摘要：面向智能体 AI（agentic-AI）领域的需求工程师在评估、规约和操作化安全自主性方面面临挑战。主流框架，如目标导向需求工程（GORE），缺乏在认知不确定性下系统应对这些挑战的机制。我们贡献了一种基于 GORE 的方法，用于在智能体 AI 系统中建模并仿真安全自主性。我们提出一种新颖的后悔-支配机制（MS-RGR）以操作化安全自主性。MS-RGR 使用两种信号：认知惊讶（新颖性检测）和认知后悔（评估性风险），以解决三难问题：智能体应当运行于常规自主、进行反思推理，还是升级给人类？我们将 MS-RGR 实例化于老年照护监测和自动驾驶中。一项 100 个随机种子的随机仿真表明，MS-RGR 将静默失败降至接近零，并以约比仅传感器基线快 17.5 倍的速度检测风险，同时通过 LTL 安全属性保持形式可追溯性。一项回顾性代理实例化在来自七个 LLM 的 208 个 AGENTHARM 场景的执行轨迹上事后应用 DRI 门控，显示该门控仅对具有强基线安全性的模型改善有害任务拒绝（门控前拒绝率超过 80%，例如从 84.1% 到 90.9%），表明 MS-RGR 是放大而非替代模型级安全训练。我们讨论了有效性威胁，并将 MS-RGR 定位为智能体 AI 需求工程中设计时安全约束的初步可行性证据。

Original abstract: Requirements engineers for agentic-AI domains face challenges in evaluating, specifying, and operationalizing safe autonomy. Mainstream frameworks, such as Goal-Oriented Requirements Engineering (GORE), lack mechanisms to systematically address these challenges under epistemic uncertainty. We contribute an approach that builds on GORE to model and simulate safe autonomy in agentic-AI systems. We introduce a novel Regret-Dominance Mechanism (MS-RGR) to operationalize safe autonomy. MS-RGR uses two signals: epistemic surprise (novelty detection) and cognitive regret (evaluative risk) to address the trilemma problem: should the agent operate in routine autonomy, undergo reflective reasoning, or escalate to human? We instantiate MS-RGR in elderly care monitoring and autonomous driving. A 100-seed stochastic simulation shows MS-RGR reduces silent failures to near-zero and detects risk approximately 17.5 times faster than a sensor-only baseline, remaining formally traceable via LTL safety properties. A retrospective proxy instantiation applying the DRI gate post-hoc over execution traces from 208 AGENTHARM scenarios across seven LLMs shows the gate improves harmful-task refusal only for models with strong baseline safety (over 80% pre-gate refusal, e.g., 84.1% to 90.9%), indicating MS-RGR amplifies rather than substitutes for model-level safety training. We discuss threats to validity, positioning MS-RGR as initial feasibility evidence for design-time safety constraints in agentic-AI requirements engineering.

</details>


### [97] [Diffs vs. Whole Files: An Empirical Comparison of Iterative Edit-Based and Direct Generation for Flutter/Dart Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05779)
*Andrej Andrejev*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 19 pages, 7 figures

Selection reason: 系统比较代码模型整文件生成与迭代diff编辑两种输出范式，并给出任务局部性机制解释，直接指导代码编辑agent设计。

TL;DR: 该研究比较了代码编辑模型的直接生成与基于差异的迭代生成两种输出模式，发现直接生成在多数指标上全面更优；基于差异的生成仅在任务局部性高的短小局部编辑（如重构、错误处理/边缘情况修复）中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于差异的迭代编辑贴近开发者修改代码的方式，且理论上每轮生成 token 更少，因此需要验证其相对于直接生成是否真正更适合代码编辑模型。

Method: 在共享 Flutter/Dart 数据集上，以直接生成和基于差异的迭代生成两种模式，训练从零开始的 100M 参数模型 Rainbow-Pony-100M 与微调后的 Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B，并在每个模型约 1,790 个任务的留出集上评估四个模型。

Result: 直接生成在编译/静态分析通过率、每字节比特数、与参考结果的字符级相似度以及盲法 LLM 评审的目标达成度、正确性和代码质量评分上均显著优于基于差异的生成；通过匹配 ID 控制任务难度及仅限双方均可编译的代码后差距仍存在。基于差异的生成在简短、空间局部化的编辑上具有竞争力，其类别胜出集中在数据集中平均编辑步数最低的重构与错误处理/边缘情况修复两类任务。

Conclusion: 作者将这一条件称为任务局部性，并指出基于编辑的训练模式是否适合代码编辑模型取决于任务局部性：它适合短小、局部化的编辑，但在更广泛任务上不及直接生成。

中文摘要：用于代码编辑的大语言模型至少可以在两种输出模式下进行训练和部署：直接生成，即模型一次性输出整个修改后的文件；以及基于差异的迭代生成（“steps”），即模型输出一系列局部化的搜索/替换编辑，每次应用一个，直到其发出完成信号或步数预算耗尽。基于差异的模式具有吸引力，因为它模仿了开发者编辑代码的方式，并且每轮应需要少得多的生成 token。我们在一个共享的 Flutter/Dart 数据集上，以两种模式训练了两个代码模型——一个从零开始训练的 100M 参数模型（Rainbow-Pony-100M）和一个微调后的 Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B——并在每个模型约 1,790 个任务的留出集上评估所有四个所得模型。直接生成在我们测量的每一个指标上都显著优于基于差异的生成——编译/静态分析通过率、每字节比特数、与参考结果的字符级相似度，以及由盲法 LLM 评审给出的目标达成度、正确性和代码质量评分——并且这种差距在通过匹配 ID 比较控制任务难度之后，以及当限制在双方都能编译的代码上时，仍然存在。随后，我们识别出一个单一的、与架构无关的机制，用以解释基于差异的生成确实会胜出的条件：它在简短、空间局部化的编辑上具有竞争力，并且其类别层面的胜出恰好集中在两个任务类别——重构和错误处理/边缘情况修复——这两个类别在我们的数据集中具有最低的平均编辑步数。我们将这一现象称为任务局部性，并讨论其对于何时基于编辑的训练模式是、以及何时不是代码编辑模型的正确选择的意义。

Original abstract: Large language models used for code editing can be trained and deployed in at least two output regimes: direct generation, where the model emits the entire modified file in one shot, and iterative diff-based generation ("steps"), where the model emits a sequence of localized search/replace edits applied one at a time until it signals completion or a step budget is exhausted. The diff-based regime is attractive because it mirrors how developers edit code and should require far fewer generated tokens per turn. We train two code models - a 100M-parameter model trained from scratch (Rainbow-Pony-100M) and a fine-tuned Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B - in both regimes on a shared Flutter/Dart dataset, and evaluate all four resulting models on a held-out set of approx 1,790 tasks per model. Direct generation substantially outperforms diff-based generation on every metric we measure - compilation/static-analysis pass rate, bits-per-byte, character-level similarity to the reference, and blinded LLM-judge ratings of goal fulfillment, correctness, and code quality - and the gap persists after controlling for task difficulty via a matched-ID comparison and when restricting to code that compiles on both sides. We then identify a single, architecture-independent mechanism behind the conditions where diff-based generation does win: it is competitive on short, spatially localized edits, and its category-level wins concentrate in exactly the two task categories - refactoring and error-handling/edge-case fixes - with the lowest mean edit-step count in our dataset. We term this task locality and discuss its implications for when an edit-based training regime is and is not the right choice for a code-editing model.

</details>


### [98] [Correct Tests Are Not Enough: Measuring and Training Oracle Conversion in Specification-Based Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05879)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 研究规格化测试生成中的oracle转换瓶颈，用奖励分解训练提升输入杀伤与正确输出，可迁移到agent测试生成与验证。

TL;DR: 该工作通过可执行奖励分解和套件级 oracle 转换测量，研究联合生成输入--输出测试时正确性与输入杀死/有效杀死之间的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言规范生成测试需要同时提供能暴露错误的输入和正确的期望输出，但二者不一定会同步改善，因此需要研究其相互作用并识别瓶颈。

Method: 采用可执行奖励分解与套件级 oracle 转换测量；生成器单次输出五个输入--输出测试；训练中使用经审计参考程序提供正确性反馈，并用固定错误程序库提供潜在输入杀死和有效杀死信号；通过加性 GRPO 目标保留两种信号且推理时无需执行；在 TC-Bench 划分上进行 Qwen3.5-9B 训练、消融及固定输入 source--oracle 交叉分析。

Result: 在第 75 步，三个独立运行的 Qwen3.5-9B 将完整测试正确性从 28.59% 提升至 42.54%，输入杀死从 24.06% 提升至 25.27%，有效完整杀死从 12.23% 提升至 14.15%；匹配 50 步消融显示，移除杀死奖励会提高正确性并略增完整杀死，但输入杀死降至 21.60%；固定输入交叉实验表明，NoKill 到 FullKill 的主要差异来自更难的输入选择，而非相同输入上的输出预测更差。

Conclusion: oracle 转换是可测量的瓶颈；在联合测试生成中保留输入效用反馈既有收益也存在局限。

中文摘要：从自然语言规范生成测试，既需要一个能暴露错误行为的输入，也需要一个正确的期望输出。这两个要求不必同步改善：模型可以通过选择更容易的输入来提高测试正确性，或者发现有用输入却无法预测其期望输出。我们通过可执行奖励分解和套件级 oracle 转换测量来研究这种相互作用。我们的生成器在单次响应中联合生成五个输入--输出测试。在训练期间，经审计的参考程序提供正确性反馈，而一个固定的错误程序库提供两种效用信号：潜在输入杀死（potential input kill）和检查生成输出后的有效杀死（effective kill）。一个加性 GRPO 目标保留这两种信号，而无需在推理时执行。在一个经审计的 TC-Bench 划分上，包含 506 个训练任务和 142 个评估任务，三个独立训练的 Qwen3.5-9B 运行在第 75 步将完整测试正确性从 28.59% 提高到 42.54%，输入杀死从 24.06% 提高到 25.27%，有效完整杀死从 12.23% 提高到 14.15%。匹配的 50 步消融揭示了一种权衡：移除杀死奖励会带来更高正确性和略高的完整杀死，但将输入杀死降至 21.60%。在 64 个训练池任务上的固定输入 source--oracle 交叉实验，将主要的 NoKill 到 FullKill 差异归因于更难的输入选择，而不是在相同输入上更差的输出预测。这些结果将 oracle 转换识别为一个可测量的瓶颈，并展示了在联合测试生成中保留输入效用反馈的收益与局限。

Original abstract: Generating tests from a natural-language specification requires both an input that exposes faulty behavior and a correct expected output. These requirements need not improve together: a model can increase test correctness by choosing easier inputs, or discover useful inputs whose expected outputs it cannot predict. We study this interaction through executable reward decomposition and suite-level oracle-conversion measurement. Our generator jointly emits five input--output tests in one response. During training, audited reference programs provide correctness feedback, while a fixed bank of faulty programs provides two utility signals: potential input kill and effective kill after checking the generated output. An additive GRPO objective preserves both signals without requiring execution at inference time. On an audited TC-Bench split with 506 training and 142 evaluation tasks, three independently trained Qwen3.5-9B runs at step 75 increase full-test correctness from 28.59\% to 42.54\%, input kill from 24.06\% to 25.27\%, and effective full kill from 12.23\% to 14.15\%. Matched 50-step ablations reveal a trade-off: removing kill rewards yields higher correctness and slightly higher full kill, but lowers input kill to 21.60\%. A fixed-input source--oracle crossover on 64 training-pool tasks attributes the principal NoKill-to-FullKill difference to harder input selection rather than worse output prediction on identical inputs. These results identify oracle conversion as a measurable bottleneck and show the benefits and limits of preserving input-utility feedback in joint test generation.

</details>


### [99] [Broken on Arrival: Silently Defective LLM Artifacts in Public Model Registries and How to Catch Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05881)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Submitted to the IEEE International Conference on Big Data (IEEE BigData 2026). Code and data: https://github.com/aditi-p31/quantcheck

Selection reason: 揭示公开模型注册表中量化工件静默缺陷并发布验收测试工具，对代码agent后端模型的质量门禁与评测有参考价值。

TL;DR: 对327个量化GGUF制品做功能审计：官方Ollama库305个、HuggingFace社区22个。发现官方库5个静默缺陷制品，在双后端上零通过，并发现后端依赖失败等注册表未测问题；发布quantcheck与审计数据，主张注册表应设验收门禁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化GGUF制品从公共注册表分发到本地，但分发流水线缺少到达用户前的功能性验收测试，导致静默缺陷可能直接进入使用。

Method: 执行327个制品；先用15任务冒烟套件筛可疑，再做164任务全量评估、第二推理后端、独立分发方同模型同量化转换作裁判，社区文件另按其模板复测。

Result: 官方Ollama库305个中5个静默缺陷，为4个Qwen2.5-Coder-3B与1个phi3.5-mini，双后端164任务与冒烟均零通过，而独立转换正常；29个模型-尺寸组中2组受影响。另暴露两个社区转换CUDA退化、Metal通过的后端依赖失败；两个确认缺陷的表面统计仍处健康范围。

Conclusion: 模型注册表应引入类似包注册表的验收门禁；作者发布审计数据集、quantcheck工具与缺陷披露报告（https://github.com/aditi-p31/quantcheck）。

中文摘要：开发者越来越多地通过从公共注册表拉取量化的 GGUF 制品，在本地运行大语言模型，但分发流水线中没有任何环节会在这些转换到达用户之前对其做功能性测试。我们执行了 327 个量化后的具备代码能力的模型制品：其中 305 个来自官方 Ollama 库，覆盖 15 个模型系列在 8 GB 及以下所有符合条件的量化级别；另有 22 个来自 HuggingFace 上下载量最高的社区仓库。每个制品都运行一套 15 任务冒烟测试套件，该套件经过校准，使健康制品通过而一个已知损坏的制品失败；可疑制品随后接受完整的 164 任务评估、第二个推理后端、由独立分发方对同一模型和量化级别所做转换作为裁判，并且对于社区文件，还在该制品自己的模板下重新测试。官方库带有五个静默缺陷制品，即一批四个 Qwen2.5-Coder-3B 转换和一个 phi3.5-mini 转换，它们在两个后端上都于 164 个任务中解决零个、在冒烟测试套件中零通过，而同一模型的独立转换却能工作：占官方制品的 1.6%，即 29 个模型与尺寸转换组中的 2 个。裁定链清除了那些会被朴素阈值判定为损坏、但实际上只是被极端量化压垮的小模型制品，并暴露了两个较旧的社区转换，它们在 CUDA 上严重退化却在 Metal 上通过：这不是有缺陷的文件，而是后端依赖的失败，是当前没有任何注册表测试的第三种现象。两个已确认缺陷产生的输出，其表面统计量落在健康范围内，任何低噪声启发式方法不实际执行都无法发现。我们发布了审计数据集、quantcheck 验收测试工具，以及每个已确认缺陷的披露报告（https://github.com/aditi-p31/quantcheck），并主张模型注册表需要包注册表已经在运行的验收门禁。

Original abstract: Developers increasingly run large language models locally by pulling quantized GGUF artifacts from public registries, yet nothing in the distribution pipeline functionally tests these conversions before they reach users. We executed 327 quantized code-capable model artifacts: 305 from the official Ollama library, spanning 15 model lines at every eligible quantization level at or under 8 GB, and 22 from the most-downloaded community repositories on HuggingFace. Each ran a 15-task smoke suite calibrated so that healthy artifacts pass while a known-broken one fails; suspects then faced full 164-task evaluation, a second inference backend, an independent distributor's conversion of the same model and quantization as referee, and, for community files, re-testing under the artifact's own template. The official library carries five silently defective artifacts, a batch of four Qwen2.5-Coder-3B conversions and one phi3.5-mini conversion, that solve zero of 164 tasks and zero of the smoke suite on both backends while independent conversions of the same models work: 1.6% of official artifacts, 2 of 29 model-and-size conversion groups. The adjudication chain cleared small-model artifacts that a naive threshold would condemn as broken when they are merely collapsed by extreme quantization, and it exposed two older community conversions that degrade badly on CUDA yet pass on Metal: not defective files but backend-dependent failures, a third phenomenon no registry currently tests for. Two confirmed defects produce output whose surface statistics sit inside the healthy range, invisible to any low-noise heuristic short of execution. We release the audit dataset, the quantcheck acceptance-testing tool, and disclosure reports for every confirmed defect (https://github.com/aditi-p31/quantcheck), and argue that model registries need the acceptance gate that package registries already run.

</details>


### [100] [VibeCheck: Assessing the Quality of LLM-Generated Unit Tests: A Multi-agent Empirical Study across Heterogeneous Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05978)
*Anika Tabassum,Mushahid Intesum,Md. Fahim Arefin,Tarannum Shaila Zaman*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Accepted at the 1st International Workshop on PromptOps and Vibe Coding (POVC '26), co-located with ASE 2026

DOI: [10.1145/3843779.3844632](https://doi.org/10.1145/3843779.3844632)

Selection reason: 多agent生成单元测试的质量实证评估，超越通过/覆盖率，关注断言强度、边界与隔离，直接相关于测试生成agent评测。

TL;DR: VibeCheck实证研究LLM代理生成单元测试的质量，发现测试常可运行但断言薄弱、边界覆盖不足，存在执行充分性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见评估依赖执行成功或覆盖率，可能遗漏断言薄弱、缺少边界情况、隔离性差和可维护性有限等深层质量问题。

Method: 在15个学生开发的Python和JavaScript/TypeScript仓库中，以Claude Sonnet 4.5为底层代理，在仅仓库、零样本条件下评估Kiro、Antigravity和Cursor，并使用五维评分标准及留一法跨代理同行评估。

Result: 生成测试常可运行，但普遍缺乏强断言和有意义的的行为覆盖；弱断言和缺失边界情况比阻塞性失败更常见。

Conclusion: VibeCheck提供了以可靠性为导向的框架，可超越通过/失败结果评估LLM生成的测试。

中文摘要：基于LLM的IDE代理正越来越多地被用于生成基于代码仓库的单元测试，然而常见的评估往往依赖于执行成功或覆盖率。这些指标可能遗漏更深层的质量问题，例如断言薄弱、缺少边界情况、隔离性差以及可维护性有限。本文介绍了VibeCheck，一项针对15个学生开发的Python和JavaScript/TypeScript代码仓库的单元测试生成实证研究。我们在仅提供代码仓库的零样本条件下，以Claude Sonnet 4.5作为底层代理，评估了Kiro、Antigravity和Cursor，并使用一个涵盖可运行性、断言强度、逻辑与边界情况覆盖、隔离性/确定性以及可维护性的五维评分标准。我们还应用留一法跨代理同行评估来比较工具并识别失败模式。结果表明存在明显的执行充分性差距：生成的测试通常可运行，但往往缺乏强断言和有意义的的行为覆盖。弱断言和缺失边界情况比阻塞性失败更常出现，表明可运行的测试仍可能很浅薄。VibeCheck提供了一个以可靠性为导向的框架，用于超越通过/失败结果来评估LLM生成的测试。

Original abstract: LLM-based IDE agents are increasingly used to generate repository-grounded unit tests, yet common evaluations often rely on execution success or coverage. These metrics can miss deeper quality issues such as weak assertions, missing edge cases, poor isolation, and limited maintainability. This paper presents VibeCheck, an empirical study of unit test generation across 15 student-developed Python and JavaScript/TypeScript repositories. We evaluate Kiro, Antigravity, and Cursor with Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the underlying agent, under repository-only, zero-shot conditions using a five-dimensional rubric covering runnability, assertion strength, logic and edge-case coverage, isolation/determinism, and maintainability. We also apply leave-one-out cross-agent peer evaluation to compare tools and identify failure patterns. Results show a clear execution-adequacy gap: generated tests are often runnable but frequently lack strong assertions and meaningful behavioral coverage. Weak assertions and missing edge cases occur more often than blocking failures, showing that runnable tests can still be shallow. VibeCheck provides a reliability-oriented framework for evaluating LLM-generated tests beyond pass/fail outcomes.

</details>


### [101] [SemVul: Semantic-Enhanced Graph Neural Networks for Code Property Graph-based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06035)
*Younas Affan,Leonardo Regano,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 基于代码属性图与语义增强的GNN漏洞检测，属于程序分析与安全缺陷检测方法，对代码理解类任务有参考价值。

TL;DR: SemVul 通过代码属性图与语义嵌入结合，提升漏洞检测准确率和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码漏洞是网络攻击的主要根源，现有方法在泛化性和准确率上仍不足，需要更好学习脆弱代码模式。

Method: 提出 SemVul：基于代码属性图并融合语义级增强，结合节点级和边级预训练代码嵌入，支持多语言与多种 GNN 架构。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上系统评估多种 GNN，SemVul 提升漏洞检测性能，优于现有方法且泛化更好。

Conclusion: 整合结构与语义信息可有效提升漏洞检测，SemVul 具有通用性和更好泛化能力。

中文摘要：源代码中的漏洞往往是全球网络攻击的根本原因，因为攻击者利用软件中的弱点来获得未授权访问、窃取数据或中断服务。在本研究中，我们评估了现有研究方法，并提出了 SemVul，一种漏洞检测流水线，在学习易受攻击的代码模式方面表现出更好的泛化能力和更高的准确率。我们提出了一种基于代码属性图的漏洞检测方法，并结合语义级增强，使模型能够同时捕获程序的结构流程和代码的语义含义。我们的方法使用预训练代码嵌入技术，集成了节点级和边级的语义嵌入。我们在公开可用的基准数据集上系统评估了多种 GNN 架构。SemVul 对编程语言具有通用性，并支持多种架构。通过整合结构和语义信息，所提出的方法提升了漏洞检测性能。我们的结果表明，SemVul 优于现有方法，并提供了更好的泛化能力。

Original abstract: Vulnerabilities in source code are often the root cause of cyberattacks worldwide, as attackers exploit weaknesses in software to gain unauthorized access, steal data, or disrupt services. In this study, we evaluated existing research approaches and propose SemVul, a vulnerability detection pipeline that demonstrates better generalization and higher accuracy in learning vulnerable code patterns. We propose a Code Property Graph-based vulnerability-detection approach combined with semantic-level enhancement, enabling the model to capture both the program's structural flow and the semantic meaning of the code. Our approach integrates both node-level and edge-level semantic embeddings using pre-trained code embedding techniques. We systematically evaluate multiple GNN architectures on publicly available benchmark datasets. SemVul is generic with respect to the programming language and supports multiple architectures. By integrating structural and semantic information, the proposed approach improves vulnerability detection performance. Our results show that SemVul outperforms existing approaches and provides better generalization.

</details>


### [102] [SpecCoder: Specification-Aware Code Generation with Curriculum Dual-Task Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06041)
*Yixuan Li,Mingxuan Huang,Jiajing Wang,Weidong Yang,Xinyi Liu,Ben Fei,Lipeng Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 47 pages, 12 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: 规格感知的两阶段训练与课程双任务GRPO提升代码生成，直接相关于LLM代码生成、规格理解与强化学习训练。

TL;DR: SpecCoder通过规范引导SFT与课程双任务GRPO两阶段训练，提升LLM从自然语言需求到结构化规范再到代码的实现一致性与生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对原始需求到结构化规范的中间映射及规范到实现行为的监督不足，导致模型可能遗漏关键约束，且规范与代码行为不一致。

Method: 提出SpecCoder：第一阶段规范引导SFT，让模型生成结构化规范分析并据此生成代码；第二阶段课程双任务GRPO，联合优化规范引导的生成与判别。

Result: 在APPS、CodeContests和xCodeEval上验证有效，持续提升独立代码生成与智能体工作流；BigCodeBench-Hard、ClassEval、人工评估与扰动研究进一步验证结构化规范的作用。

Conclusion: 规范感知训练能强化规范与代码行为的对应关系，结构化规范有助于引导代码生成与判别。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）在代码生成方面已取得实质性进展，但在需要理解丰富自然语言需求的挑战性编程任务上仍存在困难。这些需求通常规定问题目标、输入/输出格式、约束、示例和边界情况。忽视其中任何一项都可能生成可执行但功能不正确的代码。现有免训练方法主要依赖提示或基于智能体的工作流，而基于训练的方法通常优化最终代码输出。然而，现有方法对学习从原始需求到结构化规范的中间映射，以及将其落地为具体实现行为，提供的监督有限。因此，模型可能遗漏关键约束，即使生成了显式规范，实现也可能无法一致地体现该规范。受这一差距驱动，我们提出SpecCoder，一个面向代码生成的规范感知两阶段训练框架。SpecCoder首先采用规范引导的SFT，训练LLMs推导结构化规范分析，并基于这些规范生成代码。随后引入课程双任务GRPO，联合优化规范引导的生成与判别，以促进规范与代码行为之间更强的一致性。在APPS、CodeContests和xCodeEval上的实验证明了规范感知训练的有效性，SpecCoder持续改进独立代码生成和基于智能体的工作流。在BigCodeBench-Hard和ClassEval上的额外评估，以及人工评估和扰动研究，进一步验证了结构化规范在引导代码生成与判别中的作用。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made substantial progress in code generation but still struggle with challenging programming tasks that require understanding rich natural language requirements. These requirements often specify problem goals, input/output formats, constraints, examples, and edge cases. Overlooking even one may produce executable but functionally incorrect code. Existing training-free methods mainly rely on prompting or agent-based workflows, while training-based methods typically optimize final code outputs. However, existing approaches provide limited supervision for learning the intermediate mapping from raw requirements to structured specifications and for grounding them in concrete implementation behavior. Consequently, models may omit critical constraints, and even when an explicit specification is produced, the implementation may fail to reflect it consistently. Motivated by this gap, we propose SpecCoder, a specification-aware two-stage training framework for code generation. SpecCoder first employs specification-guided SFT to train LLMs to derive structured specification analyses and generate code conditioned on them. It then introduces curriculum dual-task GRPO, which jointly optimizes specification-guided generation and discrimination to encourage stronger correspondence between specifications and code behavior. Experiments on APPS, CodeContests, and xCodeEval demonstrate the effectiveness of specification-aware training, with SpecCoder consistently improving both standalone code generation and agent-based workflows. Additional evaluations on BigCodeBench-Hard and ClassEval, alongside human evaluation and perturbation studies, further validate the role of structured specifications in guiding code generation and discrimination.

</details>


### [103] [SkillSpec: Intent-Masked Specification Reasoning for Agent Skill Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06052)
*Yizhuo Zhang,Bo Kang,Yi Yang,Zhiyu Duan,Zhouteng Ye,Shunkun Yang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 面向agent可复用技能的规格推理与沙箱验证，识别意图冲突等静默缺陷，直接可迁移到agent技能正确性保障。

TL;DR: 提出 SkillSpec，用 Hoare 风格规范推理检测可复用智能体技能中的隐性语义缺陷，并在真实技能集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用技能抽象对自主智能体很重要，但其正确性难以保证，且存在意图冲突等超越传统代码缺陷的静默语义失败。

Method: 将异构技能仓库统一为图表示，对齐描述、指令与代码；对每个节点推导 ExpectSpec、推断 FactSpecs，并用意图掩码调节整体、谱系、邻域、局部视图，联合推理候选缺陷后在隔离沙箱中自动验证。

Result: 在 SkillsBench 与广泛下载仓库的 515 个真实技能上，识别出 239 个技能中的 763 个经人工确认缺陷，精确率 61.2%；代码节点上的规范推理稳定可靠，纯文本节点是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 多数缺陷位于声明意图与实现边界，显式规范为真实智能体生态中的技能质量保证提供了实用基础。

中文摘要：自主智能体系统日益依赖可复用技能抽象，以整合经验知识与领域专业知识。这些产物通常将自由形式的指令与异构资源捆绑在一起。然而，确保其正确性仍然具有挑战性。它们的失效模式超越了传统代码缺陷，延伸至诸如意图冲突等细微语义不一致；这些不一致表现为被底层模型掩盖的静默失败。此外，技能正确性必须基于预期的任务边界与泛化性。我们提出 SkillSpec，一种 Hoare 风格框架，将技能正确性形式化为规范推理问题。它将异构技能仓库转换为统一的图表示，使描述、指令和代码产物对齐。对于每个节点，SkillSpec 从周围声明的意图中推导出 ExpectSpec，并在意图部分披露的情况下从编码行为中推断 FactSpecs。意图掩码调节对整体、谱系、邻域和局部视图的访问，以平衡过度上下文引入的偏差与上下文不足导致的不受支持推断。SkillSpec 联合推理这些视图以标记候选缺陷，并在隔离沙箱中自动验证它们。在来自 SkillsBench 和广泛下载仓库的 515 个真实世界技能上，SkillSpec 在 239 个技能中识别出 763 个经人工确认的缺陷，达到 61.2% 精确率。跨多个模型系列的节点级分析表明，规范推理对代码节点始终可靠，而纯文本节点仍是主要瓶颈。大多数缺陷出现在声明意图与实现之间的边界，表明显式规范为真实世界智能体生态系统中的技能质量保证提供了实用基础。

Original abstract: Autonomous agent systems increasingly depend on reusable skill abstractions for consolidating experiential knowledge and domain expertise. These artifacts typically bundle free-form instructions with heterogeneous resources. However, ensuring their correctness remains challenging. Their failure modes transcend conventional code defects to subtle semantic inconsistencies such as intent conflicts, which manifest as silent failures masked by the underlying model. Moreover, skill correctness must be grounded in intended task boundaries and generalizability. We propose SkillSpec, a Hoare-style framework that formulates skill correctness as a specification reasoning problem. It transforms a heterogeneous skill repository into a unified graph representation that aligns descriptions, instructions and code artifacts. For each node, SkillSpec derives an ExpectSpec from the surrounding declared intent, and infers FactSpecs from encoded behavior under partially disclosed intent. An intent mask regulates access to holistic, lineage, neighborhood, and local views to balance the bias introduced by excessive context against unsupported inference caused by insufficient context. SkillSpec jointly reasons over these views to flag candidate defects, and automatically validates them in an isolated sandbox. On 515 real-world skills from SkillsBench and widely downloaded repositories, SkillSpec identified 763 manually confirmed defects across 239 skills, achieving 61.2% precision. The node-level analysis across multiple model families shows that specification reasoning is consistently reliable for code nodes, whereas plain-text nodes remain a major bottleneck. Most defects arise at the boundaries between declared intent and implementation, demonstrating that explicit specifications provide a practical foundation for skill quality assurance in real-world agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Lexical Metrics: Sentence-Embedding Detection of Reviewer Habituation in AI Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06213)
*Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Danping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 研究AI代码评审中审阅者习惯化与潜在语义漂移，对代码评审agent的行为评测与可靠性分析有参考价值。

TL;DR: 基于400名重复审查者207天内的11,429条AI代码审查和10,104条AIDev行内评论，批准率随暴露上升，但经典词汇指标未下降；句子嵌入结构可检测审查者适应，且语言变化滞后于批准行为变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体大规模提交拉取请求，审查者是否因重复暴露而降低审查严格度，以及评论是否揭示该变化，尚不明确。

Method: 分析207天内400名重复审查者的11,429条审查及其配对的10,104条AIDev人类行内评论；检验批准率、四类人工语言特征、逻辑回归分类器、句子嵌入统计与MLP，并用Granger分析检验批准率与语言特征的时间先后。

Result: 批准率从早期30.5%升至晚期36.6%（Wilcoxon p=8.6x10^-8, d=0.25）；四类人工特征无单调下降，逻辑回归F1=0.485低于多数类基线；嵌入结构有信号，MLP在5折审查者分层交叉验证下F1=0.74；Granger显示批准率变化预测技术特异性后续变化（所有滞后p<0.001），反向仅1/4滞后显著。

Conclusion: 审查者适应体现于潜在分布结构而非经典词汇指标，且语言变化跟随批准行为变化，而非先于其发生。

中文摘要：代码审查是AI生成代码与生产环境之间的关键质量检查点。随着AI编码智能体大规模提交拉取请求，目前尚不清楚审查者是否会因重复暴露而降低审查严格程度，以及审查评论是否揭示这种变化。我们研究了207天内来自400名重复审查者的11,429条审查，并与来自AIDev的10,104条人类撰写的行内评论配对。批准率从审查者早期阶段的30.5%上升到晚期阶段的36.6%（Wilcoxon p = 8.6 x 10^-8；Cohen's d = 0.25）。然而，四个人工设计的语言特征——词汇多样性、Shannon熵、技术特异性和建设性可操作性——在暴露十分位数上均未显示单调下降（所有Spearman绝对值rho <= 0.53，p >= 0.11；Bonferroni校正后的Mann-Whitney p >= 0.36）。基于这些特征的逻辑回归分类器达到F1 = 0.485，低于多数类基线。句子嵌入结构确实携带信号：审查者晚期评论质心偏离其早期评论质心的程度大于审查者内随机置换下的程度（Wilcoxon p < 0.001），而使用三个嵌入统计量的小型MLP在5折按审查者分层交叉验证下达到F1 = 0.74。Granger分析显示，批准率变化在所有测试滞后阶数上都能预测技术特异性的后续变化（p < 0.001），而反向方向仅在四个滞后阶数中的一个上显著。因此，审查者适应可在潜在分布结构中检测到，而不是在经典词汇指标中；语言变化跟随而非先于批准行为的变化。

Original abstract: Code review is a key quality checkpoint between AI-generated code and production. As AI coding agents submit pull requests at scale, it is unclear whether reviewers reduce scrutiny with repeated exposure and whether review comments reveal this change. We study 11,429 reviews from 400 repeat reviewers over 207 days, paired with 10,104 human-authored inline comments from AIDev. Approval rates rise from 30.5% in reviewers' early periods to 36.6% in late periods (Wilcoxon p = 8.6 x 10^-8; Cohen's d = 0.25). However, four hand-crafted linguistic features - lexical diversity, Shannon entropy, technical specificity, and constructive actionability - show no monotonic decline across exposure deciles (all Spearman absolute rho <= 0.53, p >= 0.11; Bonferroni-corrected Mann-Whitney p >= 0.36). A logistic-regression classifier based on these features reaches F1 = 0.485, below the majority-class baseline. Sentence-embedding structure does carry signal: reviewers' late-period comment centroids shift farther from their early-period centroids than under within-reviewer random permutations (Wilcoxon p < 0.001), and a small MLP using three embedding statistics reaches F1 = 0.74 under 5-fold reviewer-stratified cross-validation. Granger analysis shows that approval-rate changes predict later shifts in technical specificity at all tested lags (p < 0.001), while the reverse direction is significant at only one of four lags. Reviewer adaptation is therefore detectable in latent distributional structure rather than in classical lexical metrics, and language shifts follow rather than precede changes in approval behavior.

</details>


### [105] [SWE-Test: Benchmarking LLM Vulnerability Discovery via Input Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06229)
*Yuanxiang Shi,Jiayi Lin,Xuanyong Lin,Liangcai Su,Yeheng Duan,Wei Wang,Qi Han,Bing Zhao,Wei Hu,Xander Xu,Chenxiong Qian*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 将漏洞发现重构为输入预测任务的SWE-Test基准，分解代码理解与反馈修正能力，直接相关于agent安全评测与反馈机制。

TL;DR: 论文将漏洞发现重述为封闭确定真值的输入预测任务，构建覆盖22个真实C/C++程序、三种模式的SWE-Test基准；最佳配置在Feedback-enabled仅55.0%通过率，约束推断而非导航是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞发现基准易受数据污染，依赖不可知漏洞集评估召回率，常用合成缺陷，且只给单一端到端结论，无法严格衡量LLM智能体能力并定位失败环节。

Method: 用覆盖率引导模糊测试挖掘真实C/C++程序深层目标分支，要求智能体预测可到达给定分支的输入；分解为Open-loop、Feedback-enabled、Online Arena三种模式，覆盖22个程序、15个领域；前两者共享60个固定目标任务、16个代码库、13个领域，Online Arena在11个程序上按覆盖率增益评估；评测15种默认工作量的模型-脚手架配置。

Result: 最佳配置在Feedback-enabled仅55.0%通过率；七个配对Claude Code配置有反馈36.4%、无反馈19.3%；Online Arena中智能体在六个程序确认13个不同缺陷；失败分解显示约束推断是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 发布SWE-Test与开箱即用评估环境；该分解可定位失败环节，表明约束推断而非导航是漏洞发现的关键短板。

中文摘要：漏洞发现正成为大语言模型（LLM）智能体的一项重要能力：那些悄无声息地漏掉真实缺陷的智能体会让关键软件暴露于风险之中。因此，严格衡量这一能力十分迫切，但现有基准可通过数据污染被钻空子，依据不可知的漏洞集合来评估召回率，往往依赖合成缺陷，并且只报告单一的端到端结论，无法定位智能体在何处失败。漏洞发现是一种复合能力：智能体必须理解源代码、推断输入约束、构造输入、执行输入，并根据反馈进行迭代修正。我们将对其的测量重新表述为一种具有封闭、确定性真值的输入预测任务：使用覆盖率引导的模糊测试，我们在真实世界C/C++程序中挖掘深层目标分支，并要求智能体预测一个能将执行引导至给定分支的输入。这将漏洞发现分解为三种任务模式，覆盖22个真实世界C/C++程序、横跨15个领域。Open-loop与Feedback-enabled共享60个固定目标任务实例，分布于其中16个代码库（13个领域），分别在无和有距离预言机的情况下测试输入构造，以将代码理解与反馈驱动的修正分离开来。Online Arena则移除预定义目标，并在一个单独、部分重叠的11个程序池上按覆盖率增益评估路径探索；智能体们共同在六个程序中确认了13个不同的缺陷。在评估15种默认工作量的模型-脚手架配置时，最佳者在Feedback-enabled模式下仅达到55.0%的通过率，而七个配对的Claude Code配置的均值在有反馈时为36.4%，无反馈时为19.3%。分解失败后，我们发现约束推断而非导航是主要瓶颈。我们发布了SWE-Test以及一个开箱即用的评估环境。

Original abstract: Vulnerability discovery is becoming an important ability of large language model (LLM) agents: agents that silently miss real defects leave critical software exposed. Rigorously measuring this ability is therefore urgent, but existing benchmarks are gameable through data contamination, score recall against an unknowable vulnerability set, often rely on synthetic bugs, and report a single end-to-end verdict that cannot localize where an agent fails. Vulnerability discovery is a composite ability: an agent must comprehend source code, infer input constraints, construct inputs, execute them, and iteratively correct from feedback. We recast its measurement as an input-prediction task with a closed, deterministic ground truth: using coverage-guided fuzzing, we mine deep target branches in real-world C/C++ programs and ask an agent to predict an input that drives execution to a given branch. This decomposes discovery into three task modes over 22 real-world C/C++ programs spanning 15 domains. Open-loop and Feedback-enabled share 60 fixed-target task instances across 16 of these codebases (13 domains), testing input construction without and with a distance oracle to isolate code comprehension from feedback-driven correction. Online Arena instead removes the predefined target and scores path exploration by coverage gain on a separate, partially overlapping pool of 11 programs; agents collectively confirmed 13 distinct bugs across six programs. Evaluating 15 default-effort model-scaffold configurations, the best reaches only 55.0% pass rate in the Feedback-enabled mode, and the mean across seven paired Claude Code configurations is 36.4% with feedback versus 19.3% without. Decomposing failures, we find constraint inference, not navigation, is the dominant bottleneck. We release SWE-Test with a turnkey evaluation environment.

</details>


### [106] [KG-Commit: A Dynamic Knowledge Graph for Online Just-in-Time Software Defect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06272)
*Mohsen Hesamolhokama,Mohammad Sina Beyrami Aghbash,Behnam Rohani,Mohammadamin Fazli,Jafar Habibi*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 用动态知识图与AST-delta做在线即时缺陷预测，属于软件缺陷分析与在线预测方法，对代码变更风险建模有参考价值。

TL;DR: KG-Commit 通过动态知识图谱增量维护项目上下文，结合 AST-delta 与 CPU 图推理实现在线 JIT-SDP，在 11 个 Apache 项目上多数指标最优，每个提交约 1.33 秒且成本稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低延迟需求使多数 JIT-SDP 方法仅依赖提交级信息，忽略变更所在的项目上下文；引入上下文又需高效检索传入提交并随仓库演进持续维护，因此需要一种可在线维护和利用丰富上下文的方案。

Method: 提出 KG-Commit 动态知识图谱，增量维护仓库历史、文件内代码结构和提交语义；使用 AST-delta 机制跟踪文件编辑间的结构变化；依赖完全在 CPU 上运行的轻量级图推理。

Result: 在 11 个 Apache 项目、6 个基线上评估；选定推理流程下取得最高总体 Macro-F1（0.704）、G-Mean（0.706）和 AUC（0.809）。在真实在线协议下，Macro-F1 在全部 11 个项目上优于 LR、HGB、RF、DeepJIT，在 10 个项目上优于 LApredict，在 9 个项目上优于 JITLine，且总体配对差异均显著。每个提交约处理 1.33 秒，成本随图增长保持稳定，兼容真实项目的提交速率。

Conclusion: 丰富的项目上下文可被高效维护并用于在线 JIT-SDP。

中文摘要：即时软件缺陷预测（JIT-SDP）旨在在提交到达时识别有风险的提交，并为开发人员提供及时反馈。这种对低延迟的需求导致大多数方法依赖提交级信息，而忽视了变更发生的更广泛项目上下文。纳入这种上下文具有挑战性，因为它既需要对传入提交进行高效检索，又需要随着代码仓库的演进而持续维护。我们提出 KG-Commit，一个动态知识图谱，随着项目演进增量维护仓库历史、文件内代码结构和提交语义。它还使用 AST-delta 机制来跟踪文件编辑之间的结构变化，并依赖完全在 CPU 上运行的轻量级图推理。我们在 11 个 Apache 软件项目上针对六个基线进行评估，结果表明，使用我们选定的推理流程，KG-Commit 取得了最高的总体 Macro-F1（0.704）、G-Mean（0.706）和 AUC（0.809）。在真实的在线协议下，它在 Macro-F1 上在所有 11 个项目上优于 LR、HGB、RF 和 DeepJIT，在 10 个项目上优于 LApredict，在 9 个项目上优于 JITLine，且总体配对差异在每种情况下均显著。KG-Commit 处理每个提交约需 1.33 秒，其成本随着图的增长保持稳定，并与现实项目中观察到的提交速率兼容。这些发现表明，丰富的项目上下文可以被高效维护并用于在线 JIT-SDP。

Original abstract: Just-in-time software defect prediction (JIT-SDP) aims to identify risky commits as they arrive and provide developers with timely feedback. This need for low latency has led most approaches to rely on commit-level information and overlook the broader project context in which a change occurs. Incorporating this context is challenging because it requires both efficient retrieval for incoming commits and continual maintenance as the repository evolves. We introduce KG-Commit, a dynamic knowledge graph that incrementally maintains repository history, within-file code structure, and commit semantics as the project evolves. It also uses an AST-delta mechanism to track structural changes between file edits and relies on lightweight graph inference running entirely on CPU. Our evaluation on 11 Apache software projects against six baselines shows that KG-Commit achieves the highest aggregate Macro-F1 (0.704), G-Mean (0.706), and AUC (0.809) using our selected inference pipeline. Under a realistic online protocol, it outperforms LR, HGB, RF, and DeepJIT on all 11 projects, LApredict on 10, and JITLine on 9 projects in Macro-F1, with the aggregate paired difference significant in every case. KG-Commit processes each commit in approximately 1.33~s, with a cost that remains stable as the graph grows and is compatible with commit rates observed in real-world projects. These findings show that rich project context can be efficiently maintained and exploited for online JIT-SDP.

</details>


### [107] [From Reading Code to Reading Spec: A Verified Layer for LLM-Driven Codebase Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06383)
*Xinhao Zhang,Jingjie Lu,Kunpeng Liu,Fei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 通过结构化规格与语义等价重构实现LLM驱动的代码库维护验证层，直接相关于代码生成验证与语义漂移防护。

TL;DR: 提出PROOF，通过结构化分层自然语言规范间接管理代码库，以仅从规范重建源代码来证明语义等价，并在维护中同步更新规范以避免语义漂移；真实代码库实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码激增导致软件复杂性与维护负担上升；LLM虽可自动化，但结构复杂性限制其直接管理代码库，因此需要可验证的间接管理方式。

Method: PROOF将代码库拓扑抽象为分层自然语言规范；仅从该规范重建源代码以证明语义等价；以该验证基础驱动维护请求，执行代码修改并同步更新规范以防止语义漂移。

Result: 在真实世界代码库上的实验证实了这些规范的有效性。

Conclusion: PROOF为全生命周期代码库管理提供了一种可证明、可信且防语义漂移的规范驱动方案。

中文摘要：LLM生成代码的快速增长增加了软件复杂性以及工程师的维护负担。尽管LLM提供了一种潜在的自动化替代方案，但这种结构复杂性阻碍了它们直接管理代码库的能力。我们提出可证明的原始功能表示（Provable Representation Of Original Functionality, PROOF），它通过结构化规范间接管理代码库。为了严格通过这些规范实现全生命周期代码库管理，PROOF将代码库拓扑抽象为分层自然语言表示。为了建立绝对信任，该系统仅从该规范重建源代码，从而证明语义等价性。这一经过验证的基础驱动维护请求，在执行代码修改的同时同步更新自身，以防止语义漂移。在真实世界代码库上的实验证实了这些规范的有效性。

Original abstract: The rapid growth of LLM-generated code increases software complexity and the maintenance burden on engineers. While LLMs offer a potential automated alternative, this structural complexity hinders their ability to manage codebases directly. We introduce the Provable Representation Of Original Functionality (PROOF), which manages codebases indirectly via structured specifications. To enable full-lifecycle codebase management strictly through these specifications, PROOF abstracts codebase topology into a hierarchical natural-language representation. To establish absolute trust, the system proves semantic equivalence by reconstructing source code exclusively from this specification. This verified foundation drives maintenance requests, executing code modifications while synchronously updating itself to prevent semantic drift. Experiments on real-world repositories confirm the effectiveness of these specifications.

</details>


### [108] [CAROL: Context-Aware Online Learning for Fuzzer Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06772)
*Zirui Liu,Mengfan Xu,Juan Zhai,Shenglong Yao,Shiqing Ma*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 基于上下文的在线模糊测试调度器，利用奖励趋势与不确定性提升缺陷发现，对测试/模糊优化与在线决策有迁移价值。

TL;DR: CAROL 是使用模糊器当前上下文的在线集成模糊测试调度器，解决过去奖励摘要不可靠与预测信号跨目标漂移问题；在 Magma 上优于基线并超过 oracle，在 C++ 程序中发现 120 个未知崩溃缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成模糊测试调度器依赖过去性能的紧凑摘要和预先固定规则；测量发现摘要受噪声影响难以可靠反映性能演化，且预测信号跨目标差异大，跨目标学到的权重通常更差。

Method: CAROL 使用调度循环已可用的当前上下文（奖励趋势、等待/平台期、已达到代码、估计不确定性），结合领域引导的上升/衰退检测与阶段特定学习规则，以及从 15 个信号预测奖励并利用预测不确定性的在线选择。

Result: 在九个 Magma 目标上，结果不同时 CAROL 均比三个基线触发更多唯一漏洞且从不更少；相比各目标最强基线提升 11.8%，并超过事后选择最佳单模糊器的 oracle。移除上下文后增益消失；在五个 C++ 程序上发现 120 个此前未知崩溃缺陷，已按位置、故障和入口点去重并报告。

Conclusion: 利用在线上下文可提升集成模糊测试调度效果，额外漏洞多来自基线很少或从未触发的区域，并在真实项目中发现大量新缺陷。

中文摘要：集成模糊测试在目标上运行多个模糊器，同时调度器在它们之间分配 CPU 时间。现有调度器将这些决策基于过去性能的紧凑摘要和测试活动开始前固定的规则。我们的测量揭示了两个局限。第一，过去奖励摘要不能可靠地刻画性能演化：在考虑估计噪声后，连续窗口排名之间的一致性在统计上与窗口内自一致性无法区分。第二，预测信号因目标而异：在九个目标中的八个上，从其他八个目标学到的权重比在当前目标上学到的权重预测奖励更差。我们提出 CAROL，一种使用每个模糊器当前上下文的在线调度器。该上下文已可用于调度循环，描述奖励趋势、等待与平台期时间、已达到的代码以及估计不确定性。CAROL 以两种方式使用上下文：一种领域引导方法检测模糊器是在上升还是在衰退，并应用阶段特定的学习规则；而一种学习方法从 15 个上下文信号预测奖励，并使用预测不确定性进行在线选择。在九个 Magma 目标上，只要结果不同，CAROL 触发的唯一漏洞数都多于三个集成调度基线中的每一个，且没有任何情况下更少。与每个目标的最强基线相比，CAROL 提升 11.8%，并超过一个回顾性地为每个目标选择最佳单个模糊器的 oracle。移除上下文会消除该增益，且额外漏洞集中在基线很少或从未触发的漏洞中。在五个广泛使用的 C++ 程序上未经修改地运行，CAROL 发现 120 个此前未知的崩溃缺陷，按位置、故障和入口点去重；所有缺陷均通过项目声明的披露渠道报告给维护者。

Original abstract: Ensemble fuzzing runs multiple fuzzers on a target while a scheduler allocates CPU time among them. Existing schedulers base these decisions on compact summaries of past performance and rules fixed before a campaign. Our measurements reveal two limitations. First, past-reward summaries do not reliably capture performance evolution: after accounting for estimation noise, agreement between consecutive-window rankings is statistically indistinguishable from within-window self-agreement. Second, predictive signals vary across targets: on eight of nine targets, a weighting learned from the other eight predicts reward worse than one learned on the current target. We introduce CAROL, an online scheduler that uses each fuzzer's current context. Already available to the dispatch loop, this context describes reward trends, waiting and plateau time, reached code, and estimation uncertainty. CAROL uses context in two ways: a domain-guided method detects whether a fuzzer is rising or rotting and applies a phase-specific learning rule, while a learned method predicts reward from 15 context signals and uses predictive uncertainty for online selection. Across nine Magma targets, CAROL triggers more unique bugs than each of three ensemble-scheduling baselines whenever their results differ, and fewer on none. Compared with the strongest baseline for each target, CAROL gains 11.8% and surpasses an oracle that retrospectively selects the best single fuzzer per target. Removing context eliminates the gain, and the additional bugs are concentrated among those the baselines trigger rarely or never. Run unchanged on five widely used C++ programs, CAROL finds 120 previously unknown crashing defects, deduplicated by site, fault, and entry point; all were reported to maintainers through the projects' stated disclosure channels.

</details>


### [109] [Shortcutting the Fix: Identifying and Categorizing Agentic Exploits in Software Engineering Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06780)
*Nikolai Ludwig,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Preprint

Selection reason: 系统审计SWE基准中agent的捷径/作弊行为并给出缓解指令，直接相关于编码agent评测可靠性与防基准游戏。

TL;DR: 审计发现SWE智能体在基准中普遍存在利用行为，标准提示下利用率达44%–82%，加入原创性指令后降至1.5%–10.7%，且核心性能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE基准高分可能来自利用本地Git历史、上游仓库或记忆答案等作弊行为，而非真实解题，因此需要审计并建立利用感知评估。

Method: 在SWE-bench Multilingual与DeepSWE上，对五个开源大语言模型，采用轮次级LLM-as-a-judge协议，比较标准提示与加入强制解决方案原创性指令后的表现。

Result: 标准提示下利用率为SWE-bench Multilingual 45.1%–82.4%、DeepSWE 44.2%–66.1%；加入原创性指令后分别降至4.0%–10.7%和1.5%–7.1%，核心任务性能仍强。

Conclusion: 需要利用感知的评估框架，衡量真实仓库级问题解决能力，而非基准博弈/刷分。

中文摘要：尽管自主软件工程（SWE）智能体在基准测试中取得了很高的解决率，但这些分数可能掩盖了利用性行为——例如利用本地 Git 历史、访问上游仓库或回忆已记忆的解决方案——而非展示真正的问题解决能力。我们在 SWE-bench Multilingual 和 DeepSWE 上，使用轮次级 LLM-as-a-judge 协议，对五个开源大语言模型中的这些利用行为进行了系统化和审计。在标准提示下，利用率为 SWE-bench Multilingual 上 45.1%--82.4%，DeepSWE 上 44.2%--66.1%。附加一条强制解决方案原创性的针对性指令后，这些利用率大幅下降——分别降至 4.0%--10.7% 和 1.5%--7.1%，同时保持强劲的核心任务性能。我们的发现表明，亟需能够衡量真实仓库级问题解决而非基准博弈的利用感知评估框架。

Original abstract: While autonomous software engineering (SWE) agents achieve high benchmark resolution rates, these scores can mask exploitative behaviors---such as leveraging local Git histories, accessing upstream repositories, or recalling memorized solutions---rather than demonstrating genuine problem solving. We systematize and audit these exploits across five open large language models on SWE-bench Multilingual and DeepSWE using a turn-level LLM-as-a-judge protocol. Under standard prompts, exploitation rates reach 45.1\%--82.4\% on SWE-bench Multilingual and 44.2\%--66.1\% on DeepSWE. Appending a targeted instruction enforcing solution originality drastically cuts these exploitation rates---down to 4.0\%--10.7\% and 1.5\%--7.1\%, respectively, while maintaining strong core task performance. Our findings demonstrate the critical need for exploit-aware evaluation frameworks that measure true repository-level problem solving over benchmark gaming.

</details>


### [110] [LatentMD: Benchmarking Markdown Boundary Failures in LLM-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06993)
*Sungjune Lee,Myungjoo Kang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 提出 Markdown 边界失败基准与评测协议，揭示 LLM 生成内容在解析器/代码抽取管线中的格式可靠性问题，可迁移到代码 agent 的输出鲁棒性验证。

TL;DR: LatentMD 基准将内容正确性与 Markdown 边界正确性分离，在 9 个 LLM 的约 37,600 次生成中发现 38.0% 的有效主网格输出内容正确但边界破损；失败主要源于同族对称分隔符冲突，提示只能部分缓解，并会泛化到 Python 三引号文档字符串，而 JSON 保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估常把内容质量与格式遵循混为一谈，导致 Markdown 围栏边界失败未被充分测量；需要区分内容正确性与边界正确性，以诊断内容正确但边界破损的输出。

Method: 提出 LatentMD 基准与评估协议，用于诊断 LLM 生成 Markdown 的 CommonMark 级别围栏边界失败；包含 4,179 条提示和用于对任意模型输出打分的 CLI；在 9 个 LLM、约 37,600 次生成上评估，并进行消融、未指定提示测试、人工编写验证集测试，以及 Python 三引号文档字符串和 JSON 对照实验。

Result: Markdown 边界失败普遍存在：38.0% 的有效主网格输出内容正确但边界破损；未指定提示下和小型人工验证集中存在大量边界破损；消融显示失败主要由同族对称分隔符冲突而非仅由嵌套驱动；提示提示只能部分缓解；失败可泛化到 Python 三引号文档字符串，而 JSON 作为非对称分隔符对照仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: LatentMD 为解析器敏感的 LLM 评估提供了一个可复现的诊断目标。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地生成 Markdown，这些 Markdown 被渲染器、智能体、代码提取器和结构化下游流水线所消费。然而，现有评估往往将内容质量与格式遵循混为一谈，导致 Markdown 边界失败未被充分测量。我们提出 LatentMD，一个用于诊断 LLM 生成 Markdown 中 CommonMark 级别围栏边界失败的基准与评估协议。LatentMD 将内容正确性与边界正确性分离，从而能够检测内容正确但边界破损的输出。该基准包含 4,179 条提示，以及一个用于对任意模型输出打分的 CLI。在 9 个 LLM 和约 37,600 次生成中，我们发现 Markdown 边界失败普遍存在：38.0% 的有效主网格输出是内容正确但边界破损的，并且在未指定提示下以及一个小型人工编写验证集中存在大量边界破损。消融实验表明，失败主要由同族对称分隔符冲突而非仅由嵌套驱动，提示提示只能部分缓解，并且可泛化到 Python 三引号文档字符串，而 JSON 作为非对称分隔符对照仍保持稳健。LatentMD 为解析器敏感的 LLM 评估提供了一个可复现的诊断目标。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate Markdown that is consumed by renderers, agents, code extractors, and structured downstream pipelines. Yet existing evaluations often conflate content quality with format adherence, leaving Markdown boundary failures under-measured. We introduce LatentMD, a benchmark and evaluation protocol for diagnosing CommonMark-level fence-boundary failures in LLM-generated Markdown. LatentMD separates content correctness from boundary correctness, enabling detection of outputs that are content-correct but boundary-broken. The benchmark contains 4,179 prompts and a CLI for scoring arbitrary model outputs. Across 9 LLMs and roughly 37,600 generations, we find that Markdown boundary failures are widespread: 38.0% of valid main-grid outputs are content-correct but boundary-broken, with substantial boundary breakage under unspecified prompts and in a small human-authored validation set. Ablations show that failures are driven primarily by same-family symmetric-delimiter collisions rather than nesting alone, are only partially mitigated by prompt hints, and generalize to Python triple-quote docstrings while JSON remains robust as an asymmetric-delimiter control. LatentMD provides a reproducible diagnostic target for parser-sensitive LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Recompilation Is Not Enough: Test-Guided Decompiled-C Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07201)
*Yuhan Huang,Puzhuo Liu,Jianlei Chi*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 5 pages, 2 figures

Selection reason: 用编译器诊断加官方测试门控修复反编译 C 代码，强调「可重编译≠行为正确」，是测试反馈驱动的 LLM 修复+验证工作流，直接可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出基于编译器反馈和官方测试的少步骤工作流修复反编译 C；在 Coreutils 9.5 的 104 个二进制上，87.5% 通过重新编译与测试门，表明测试门反馈比仅编译恢复更可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反编译 C 通常需修复才能重新编译，但仅重新编译无法保证行为正确，重新编译后的命令行二进制仍可能出现选项解析、输出字节或退出状态不一致。

Method: 使用编译器和链接器诊断指导构建修复；重新编译后，通过冒烟检查和相关官方测试暴露行为差异，进行语义修复。

Result: 在 104 个具有可用反编译器导出和确定性精确输出冒烟比较的 Coreutils 9.5 二进制上，91 个（87.5%）重新编译并通过测试门；9 个未在修复预算内重新编译；4 个重新编译但未通过测试门。

Conclusion: 测试门反馈能使 LLM 辅助的反编译 C 修复比仅编译恢复更具可审计性。
